Motivation: 现有 World Action Models 在仅以文本指令为条件时，指令跟随能力有限。作者认为其部分原因在于机器人学习数据的结构性失衡：视觉-动作轨迹丰富，但语言标注稀疏且重复，导致策略更多依赖视觉上下文与动作规律来"识别任务"，而非真正 grounding 文本指令。

Method: JEPA-WAM 为每条文本指令配备一"视觉指令库"：用现成的文本到图像生成器（不训练生成器）依据文本指令采样多张"任务完成图"，作为语义对齐的视觉目标参考。为聚焦任务级语义而非外观，使用冻结的 V-JEPA 2.1 编码器对这些参考图编码，将稠密目标表征压缩为紧凑 goal token，并通过 cross-attention 同时条件化视频专家与动作专家。此外构建了覆盖分布内、分布外场景、分布外指令三类设置的真机指令跟随基准。

Result: 在该基准上，JEPA-WAM 在分布内、分布外场景、分布外指令三种设置下的成功率分别为 87.3%、74.5%、80.9%，相较 π0 与 Fast-WAM 至少分别高出 10.0、27.3、14.5 个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过随机生成的视觉目标参考（经 V-JEPA 2.1 编码为 goal token 并以 cross-attention 注入视频与动作专家），可有效缓解机器人数据语言标注稀疏带来的指令 grounding 不足问题，显著提升 WAM 的指令跟随能力与对场景、指令分布偏移的鲁棒性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) have demonstrated strong robotic manipulation capabilities by augmenting pretrained video generative models with action experts. However, current WAMs still show limited instruction-following ability when conditioned solely on text instructions. We argue that this limitation stems in part from a structural imbalance in robot-learning data: rich visual-action trajectories are often paired with sparse and repetitive language annotations, allowing policies to identify tasks from visual context and motion regularities rather than grounding the instruction itself. To address this limitation, we introduce JEPA-WAM, which augments each text instruction with a bank of stochastically generated visual instructions, providing diverse visual cues for instruction following. Specifically, JEPA-WAM uses an off-the-shelf text-to-image generator to sample multiple task-completion images conditioned on the text instruction, without training the generator. Although these generated images may differ from the current visual scene in appearance and layout, they remain semantically aligned with the instruction and serve as visual goal references. To focus on task-level semantics beyond appearance, we encode these references with a frozen V-JEPA 2.1 encoder. The resulting dense goal representations are compressed into compact goal tokens that condition both the video and action experts through cross-attention. We further construct a real-robot instruction-following benchmark covering in-distribution, out-of-distribution scene, and out-of-distribution instruction settings. On this benchmark, JEPA-WAM achieves success rates of 87.3%, 74.5%, and 80.9% in these three settings, outperforming π0 and Fast-WAM by at least 10.0, 27.3, and 14.5 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [70] [AgentPProf: Semantic Profiler for Long Horizon AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20301)
*Yusheng Zheng,Chaokun Chang,Yu Mao,Tianyuan Wu,Yuxi Huang,Tao Ma,Wenan Mao,Shuyi Cheng,Andi Quinn,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出面向AI智能体的"语义操作栈"模型与AgentPProf剖析器，将长周期智能体轨迹聚合为pprof兼容的性能剖析（profile），实现跨运行的资源归因与问题定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体长期编排用户、工具与系统资源的活动，开发者需定位故障、识别不安全效应的诱因、并找出最耗预算的任务以优化质量、安全与成本。系统软件中的性能剖析（profiling）通过聚合资源消耗并归因到代码路径来定位热点，可回答类似问题；但现有智能体可观测性工具聚焦单次执行的调试与追踪（tracing），缺乏跨运行的长期剖析能力，难以规模化回答上述问题。因此智能体可观测性不仅需要调试，更需要剖析。然而对智能体做剖析存在困难：责任实体是"诊断认证""比较分支"这类任务意图而非代码路径，且缺乏用于聚合的稳定标识符。

Method: 提出语义操作栈（semantic operation stack）模型，将剖析适配到智能体轨迹：用统一操作（uniform operations）表示所有活动，以操作栈替代运行时调用栈，支持不同粒度的层次化归因。观察到智能体的任务占据连续跨度并可分解为子任务，据此引入递归操作分割（recursive operation segmentation），在任务边界递归切分轨迹。基于该模型实现AgentPProf剖析器，将智能体轨迹聚合为pprof兼容的profile，支持火焰图（flame graph）可视化与分析。

Result: 在CodeTraceBench上，AgentPProf相对人工标注达到0.764的B³ F1；在三个问题定位基准上，其profile将MAP最多提升56%，表明其能有效归因资源、定位问题，并以可接受的剖析开销帮助优化token成本。

Conclusion: 智能体可观测性应从纯调试转向剖析。通过语义操作栈与递归操作分割，可将无稳定标识符的任务意图转化为可层次化聚合的剖析单元，AgentPProf验证了该思路在归因、定位与成本优化上的实用价值。代码已开源于 https://github.com/eunomia-bpf/agentsight。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly orchestrate long-running activities with users, tools, and system resources for days and weeks. To improve agent quality, safety, and cost efficiency, developers need to determine where failures happen, what triggers unsafe effects, and which tasks consume the most budget, then optimize those tasks. In systems software, profiling answers similar questions by aggregating resource consumption and attributing it to responsible code paths to identify hotspots. Yet existing agent observability tools focus on per-execution debugging and tracing rather than cross-run, long term profiling, making these questions difficult to answer at scale. Agent observability needs profiling, not only debugging, but profiling agents is challenging: the responsible entities are task intent like diagnose authentication, compare branches rather than code paths, and lack stable identifiers for aggregation. We propose a semantic operation stack model that adapts profiling to agent trajectories. Uniform operations represent all activities, and operation stacks replace the runtime call stack, enabling hierarchical attribution at different granularities. We observe that an agent's task occupies a contiguous span and decomposes into subtasks, so we introduce recursive operation segmentation, which recursively splits trajectories at task boundaries. AgentPProf is a profiler that aggregates agent trajectories into pprof-compatible profiles, enabling flame graph visualization and analysis. AgentPProf reaches 0.764 $B^3$ F1 against human annotations on CodeTraceBench. On three problem-localization benchmarks, the profile raises MAP by up to 56%, demonstrating that it effectively attributes resources, locates problems, and helps optimize token cost at practical profiling cost. AgentPProf is available at https://github.com/eunomia-bpf/agentsight.

</details>


### [71] [Diagnose, Recover, Certify: Task Readiness under Hidden Dynamics Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20304)
*Nguyen Viet Tuan Kiet,Huynh Thi Thanh Binh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"休眠动态漂移下的任务就绪性"决策问题，并给出基于干预的贝叶斯方法EGMPT，统一执行器故障定位、变化表征与任务就绪认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已部署的控制策略可能掩盖关键动态变化：当策略很少激发某执行器时，即使该执行器对未来尚未明确的任务至关重要，其失效也可能不影响当前任务。现有方法难以在任务无关的有限交互预算下，同时完成变化诊断与变化后的控制恢复。

Method: 形式化"休眠动态漂移下的任务就绪性"决策问题：代理需在任务身份揭示前，利用少量信息性交互判断局部动态是否及何处变化并表征之；随后为每个候选任务提供恢复策略及可达回报的校准下界，或作出弃权并回退至安全策略。提出Evidence-Gated Matched-Pulse Transport（EGMPT），一种基于干预的贝叶斯流程，通过共享的匹配响应表示将故障定位与执行器有效性估计耦合，在保持诊断可靠性的同时把局部证据转化为与恢复相关的不确定性，并将其传播至任务条件下的策略选择与就绪认证，以显式权衡期望性能、置信度与回退使用。

Result: 在涵盖多个模拟器的多样化休眠执行器基准上评估该框架，采用将诊断与能力恢复分离的协议，以就绪覆盖率、选择性风险、交互成本及回报衡量部署表现，并识别出传输证据具有决定性作用的故障机制。

Conclusion: 该框架将主动变化诊断与变化后控制恢复统一于任务无关的交互预算之下，实现了性能、置信度与安全性之间的显式权衡，并明确了证据传输机制的有效边界。

Abstract: A deployed control policy can conceal consequential dynamics changes: an actuator may lose effectiveness without affecting the current task when the policy rarely excites it, despite being critical for a future task that has not yet been specified. We introduce task readiness under dormant dynamics drift, a decision problem that unifies active change diagnosis and post-change control recovery under a limited, task-agnostic interaction budget. An agent must identify whether and where local dynamics have changed, use a small number of informative interactions to characterize the change before downstream task identity is revealed, and subsequently provide each candidate task with either a recovered policy and a calibrated lower bound on its achievable return or an abstention decision to a safe fallback. We propose Evidence-Gated Matched-Pulse Transport, an intervention-based Bayesian procedure that couples fault localization with estimation of actuator effectiveness through a shared matched-response representation, thereby preserving diagnostic reliability while converting localized evidence into recovery-relevant uncertainty. This uncertainty is propagated to task-conditioned policy selection and readiness certification, enabling deployment decisions that explicitly trade off expected performance, confidence, and fallback use. We evaluate the resulting framework on a diverse suite of dormant-actuator benchmarks spanning multiple simulators, under a protocol that separates diagnosis from capability recovery, scores deployment by readiness coverage, selective risk, and interaction cost as well as return, and identifies the fault regimes in which transported evidence is decisive.

</details>


### [72] [NeuSOGA3D: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Explainable 3D Geometric Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20323)
*Qingde Li,Qingqi Hong,Zihan Li,Jie Tian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NeuSOGA3D：混合神经-符号框架，将点云投影到主正交平面，构建符号隐式样条表示，经保形CSG融合生成粗视觉壳，并通过截面分解与体积重建恢复细节，输出显式、CAD兼容的几何表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维点云重建在计算机视觉、几何建模与CAD中仍具挑战；神经隐式方法精度高但几何被编码于潜在表示，可解释性与工程流程中的可复用性受限。

Method: 结合NeuSOGA的学习感知先验与显式符号几何推理：将点云投影到主正交平面，由观测构建符号隐式样条表示，通过保形构造实体几何（CSG）操作融合生成粗视觉壳；再以截面分解和基于Partial Shape-Preserving Splines的体积重建恢复细节。逐步将观测转化为控制多边形、隐式样条场、截面与体积放样等显式符号实体。

Result: 在ModelNet40全部40个类别上的实验表明，该框架能从多样点云观测中恢复具有结构意义且CAD兼容的几何表示。

Conclusion: 将学习感知与符号几何推理相结合，有望实现可解释的几何智能。

Abstract: Three-dimensional reconstruction from unorganized point clouds remains a challenging problem in computer vision, geometric modeling, and computer-aided design. While neural implicit methods achieve impressive reconstruction accuracy, geometry is typically encoded in latent representations that limit interpretability and reuse within engineering workflows.
  We present NeuSOGA3D (Neuro-Symbolic Geometric Abstraction in 3D), a hybrid framework that combines learned perceptual priors inherited from NeuSOGA with explicit symbolic geometric reasoning. The method projects point clouds onto principal orthographic planes, constructs symbolic implicit spline representations from the resulting observations, and fuses them through shape-preserving constructive solid geometry operations to generate a coarse visual hull. Additional geometric detail is recovered through cross-sectional decomposition and volumetric reconstruction using Partial Shape-Preserving Splines.
  Unlike conventional neural implicit approaches, NeuSOGA3D progressively transforms observations into explicit symbolic entities, including control polygons, implicit spline fields, cross-sections, and volumetric lofts. Experiments on all forty categories of the ModelNet40 benchmark demonstrate the ability of the framework to recover structurally meaningful and CAD-compatible geometric representations from diverse point-cloud observations. The results highlight the potential of combining learned perception with symbolic geometric reasoning for explainable geometric intelligence.

</details>


### [73] [Structured Four-Stage Legal Translation: From Natural-Language Traffic Rules to PROLOG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20334)
*May Myo Zin,Wachara Fungwacharakorn,Ken Satoh,Katsumi Nitta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出结构化四阶段法律翻译框架S4L，将交通法规直接翻译为可执行的Prolog逻辑，准确率达75%，优于两个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通法规面向人类理解，依赖共享背景知识与灵活表述，天然存在歧义、语境依赖和语义模糊问题，这与Prolog等计算推理引擎所需的精确逻辑结构相冲突，因此需要可靠的自动翻译方法。

Method: 评估两个基线方法：自然语言到Prolog（NL→Prolog）和逻辑英语到Prolog（LE→Prolog）；提出推理引导的S4L框架，在单一引导提示中依次完成语义角色抽取、场景补全、逻辑映射和Prolog规则生成，实现无需人工干预的端到端翻译。

Result: 在包含20条真实交通规则的基准上，从语法有效性、语义正确性和逻辑完整性三方面评估：S4L→Prolog准确率最高，正确形式化75%的规则；NL→Prolog为60%，LE→Prolog为55%。定性分析表明S4L能更可靠地捕捉隐含因果关系、道义模态和例外结构。

Conclusion: 结构化推理提示可显著提升自然语言到逻辑翻译的可靠性，适用于法律及安全攸关场景。

Abstract: Traffic regulations are written for human interpretation and therefore rely on shared background knowledge and flexible phrasing, which inherently introduce ambiguity, context dependence, and semantic underspecification. These linguistic characteristics conflict with the precision required by computational reasoning engines such as Prolog, which demand explicit logical structure. This study evaluates two baseline translation approaches, Natural Language to Prolog ($NL\rightarrow Prolog$) and Logical English to Prolog ($LE\rightarrow Prolog$), and introduces a new reasoning-guided translation framework called Structured Four-Stage Legal Translation ($S4L\rightarrow Prolog$). The proposed S4L framework performs semantic role extraction, scene completion, logical mapping, and Prolog rule generation within a single guided prompt, enabling direct translation of raw traffic rules into executable logic without human intervention. A benchmark consisting of twenty real-world traffic rules was used to evaluate each approach in terms of syntactic validity, semantic correctness, and logical completeness. $S4L\rightarrow Prolog$ achieves the highest accuracy, correctly formalizing 75 percent of the rules, while $NL\rightarrow Prolog$ reaches 60 percent and $LE\rightarrow Prolog$ reaches 55 percent. Qualitative analysis further shows that S4L captures implicit causal relations, deontic modality, and exception structure more reliably than the baselines. These results demonstrate that structured reasoning prompts can substantially improve the reliability of natural-language-to-logic translation for legal and safety-critical applications.

</details>


### [74] [A Qualitative Model for Reasoning about Path and Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20349)
*Abhishek Jaiswal,Zoe Falomir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文面向Camelot Jr.积木拼图游戏，提出一种融合定性推理与质心稳定性数学逻辑的混合定性模型，用于构建可解释、类人化的游戏辅导AI智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间推理能力与STEM领域表现高度相关，而游戏是训练儿童空间技能的有效媒介。但要让游戏实现类人化辅导与玩家引导，需要AI智能体能够从空间事件中进行常识推理。定性推理（QR）以符号表示推理，可将游戏状态无缝转化为可解释的反馈，因此被视为合适框架。Camelot Jr.要求玩家搭建多层桥梁连接两座塔上的角色，涉及平台稳定性、路径规划与块体使用约束，对精确物理推理提出要求。

Method: 提出一种混合定性模型。在定性求解器基础上，引入基于数学的质心（center-of-mass）稳定性逻辑，以应对该领域所需的精确物理判断，从而实现符号化空间推理与物理稳定性计算的结合。

Result: 该模型能够处理Camelot Jr.中对精确物理的要求，用以指导定性求解过程，支持游戏中的空间技能训练；同时为构建以人为中心、可解释的博弈智能体作出贡献。

Conclusion: 混合定性模型将定性推理的可解释性与质心稳定性计算相结合，可有效支持Camelot Jr.中的空间技能训练，并推动人本化、可解释游戏AI智能体的发展。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning abilities correlate strongly with performance in STEM fields. Games offer a compelling medium for training these critical skills in developing children who have a natural proclivity for play. However, to facilitate human-like tutoring and player guidance, these games require an AI agent capable of making commonsense inferences from spatial events. Qualitative reasoning (QR) models appear to be a suitable framework for these application domains. As these models reason in symbolic representations, they can seamlessly translate game states into interpretable feedback for human-like player guidance. This paper introduces a hybrid qualitative model designed for Camelot Jr., a block-puzzle game that requires constructing multi-level bridges to connect two avatars stationed on separate towers. The game poses a challenge for the player, who must make platforms stable, plan their path, and ensure they use all the provided blocks. To handle the precise physics required by the domain, we integrate a mathematical center-of-mass stability logic to guide our qualitative solver. Our work facilitates spatial skill training in Camelot Jr. and contributes to the development of human-centric, explainable game-playing agents.

</details>


### [75] [Generating Heterogeneous 3D Geological Microstructures from 2D Images via a Stable Diffusion-Adversarial Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20358)
*Ali Aouf,Eric Laloy,Bart Rogiers,Christophe De Vleeschouwer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出一种混合生成方法，用对抗损失替代扩散模型的标准去噪损失，从而在无3D真值的情况下从2D图像重建复杂非均质微观结构，生成质量优于SliceGAN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黏土与胶凝材料物理性质的表征需要3D成像，但3D成像昂贵、不易获取且对某些材料存在技术限制；从易获取的2D图像重建3D体积是可行替代方案。现有基于GAN的SliceGAN在同质各向同性与各向异性体系表现良好，但难以刻画复杂非均质微观结构的细节，因此需要新的生成框架。

Method: 提出一种混合生成方法，借鉴去噪扩散模型的稳定性与生成质量。由于缺乏3D真值，用对抗损失替代标准去噪损失，在实验中实现了稳定的训练过程。

Result: 该模型能生成复杂度各异的微观结构，切片伪影极少，且生成的相分数与结构描述符与真值高度一致。

Conclusion: 用对抗损失驱动的扩散式生成框架可在无3D监督下有效重建复杂非均质微观结构，克服SliceGAN在细节刻画上的不足，为材料微观结构表征提供了有前景的替代方案。

Abstract: Characterizing the physical properties of clay and cementitious materials matters across many fields, from materials science to geological waste disposal. Property simulation typically calls for 3D imaging, which is expensive, not always accessible, and technically limited for certain materials. Recent progress in deep generative models offers a way around this, reconstructing 3D volumes from the more easily acquired 2D images.
  Among GAN-based methods for 3D microstructure generation, SliceGAN has shown strong results for homogeneous isotropic and anisotropic systems. It struggles, however, to capture the finer detail of more complex heterogeneous microstructures, which motivates alternative generative frameworks.
  We introduce a hybrid approach that draws on the stability and generation quality of denoising diffusion models. Since no 3D ground truth is available, we replace the standard denoising loss with an adversarial loss, which yields a stable training process in our experiments. We show that the resulting model generates microstructures of varying complexity with minimal slice artefacts and close agreement with ground-truth phase fractions and structural descriptors.

</details>


### [76] [The Organization of Inference: Information, Resource Constraints, and AI Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20449)
*Yukun Zhang,Kemu Xu,Yishen Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文通过受控工作流实验研究AI推理的经济价值：在外部验证的软件工程任务上，比较直接执行与信息受限规划两种组织方式在12,000与24,000逻辑token上限下的表现。结果显示直接执行成功率稳定为59.6%，而规划成功率随预算扩大从36.2%升至51.2%，劣势缩小15.0个百分点。信息结构（规划者是否可见任务issue）与容量规模共同决定推理的生产性价值，其中下游执行贡献了总用量增量的89.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理的经济价值取决于容量与任务信息在AI生产各阶段之间的分布方式，而这一组织层面的边际效应此前缺乏受控的实证刻画，作者希望厘清"规模"与"工作流/信息结构"分别如何影响推理的生产性价值。

Method: 在外部验证的软件工程任务上进行受控工作流实验，设计两个匹配的资源面板（逻辑token上限为12,000和24,000），对比直接执行与信息受限规划；并开展严格的只读规划campaign，操纵规划者是否可见任务issue；采用任务簇bootstrap估计置信区间，并统计规划工作流的绑定率与下游执行的用量占比。

Result: 直接执行在两个上限下成功率均为59.6%；信息受限规划成功率由36.2%升至51.2%，劣势收窄15.0个百分点（95%任务簇bootstrap区间4.2–25.8）。在12,000 token下，可见issue使规划成功率提升约16个百分点；任务知情规划比直接执行低约10个百分点，但在24,000 token下反超29.6个百分点。规划工作流的绑定率由46.2%降至0.8%，下游执行占总用量增量的89.9%，直接执行的实际用量远低于任一上限。

Conclusion: 规模决定系统可用的容量，而工作流与信息结构塑造推理的生产性价值；提升规划者的信息可见性、并将容量配置到下游执行阶段，是提高推理经济效率的关键组织杠杆。

Abstract: The economic value of inference depends on how capacity and task information are distributed across stages of AI production. We study these organizational margins using controlled workflow experiments on externally verified software-engineering tasks. In two matched resource panels, direct execution records the same success rate of 59.6 percent at logical-token ceilings of 12,000 and 24,000, while success under information-constrained planning rises from 36.2 to 51.2 percent. The planning disadvantage narrows by 15.0 percentage points (95 percent task-cluster bootstrap interval: 4.2 to 25.8). A strict read-only planning campaign varies whether the planner sees the task issue. At 12,000 tokens, issue access raises success by about 16 percentage points over issue-hidden planning. Compared with direct execution, task-informed planning is about 10 points lower at 12,000 tokens; at 24,000 tokens, it shows a 29.6-point advantage. In the resource panels, direct execution uses substantially less than either ceiling, while the planning workflow's binding rate falls from 46.2 to 0.8 percent and downstream execution accounts for 89.9 percent of the increase in total use. Scale determines the capacity available to a system; workflow and information structure shape the productive value

</details>


### [77] [SkillAA: Attribution-Guided Skill-Graph Updating with Targeted Validation and Rollback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20455)
*Ziqiao Shang,Ling-Yue Ge,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillAA是一个针对冻结语言模型的结构化技能优化框架，将技能的适用性、执行与组合统一建模为图，借助失败归因实现精准修复与局部验证，在SearchQA、LiveMath、DocVQA上取得领先表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有外部技能方法多直接从失败轨迹中编辑技能，缺乏从"观测失败"到"可编辑位置"的结构化路由；同时已有技能图未充分利用语义边界、对象地址与拓扑依赖，导致技能检索、定向更新与范围化验证效率不足。

Method: 提出SkillAA（Skill Abductive Attribution）框架：以统一图结构表示技能的适用性、执行与组合，使同一结构支持技能选择、归因引导修复与更新验证；通过对比成功与失败执行轨迹，将候选修复路由到特定图对象，仅更新选中的局部结构，并用Local Gate与Big Gate在提交前筛选候选变更。

Result: 在使用gpt-5.6-sol时，SkillAA在SearchQA、LiveMath和DocVQA上分别达到81.5%、66.7%和91.2%，并在所有主要设置下均取得观测到的最优平均值。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了归因引导的图编辑与图范围化验证的有效性，表明结构化技能路由与局部更新是提升冻结语言模型外部技能优化的重要方向。

Abstract: External skills provide domain procedures without parameter updates, but existing methods often edit skills directly from failed rollouts without structured routing from an observed failure to an editable location; existing skill graphs also underuse semantic boundaries, object addresses, and topological dependencies for skill retrieval, targeted updating, and scoped validation. We introduce SkillAA (Skill Abductive Attribution), a structured skill-optimization framework for frozen language models. It represents skill applicability, execution, and composition in a unified graph, allowing the same structure to support skill selection, attribution-guided repair, and update validation. SkillAA contrasts successful and failed executions to route candidate repairs to specific graph objects, updates only the selected local structure, and uses Local and Big Gates to screen candidate changes before commitment. With gpt-5.6-sol, SkillAA reaches 81.5%, 66.7%, and 91.2% on SearchQA, LiveMath, and DocVQA, respectively, and attains the highest observed mean in every main setting. These results support the utility of attribution-guided graph editing and graph-scoped validation.

</details>


### [78] [How Do Agent Harnesses Create Value? Planning Information and Release Control in Stateful LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20474)
*Yukun Zhang,Kemu Xu,Yishen Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在 τ²-bench 的 Retail 与 Airline 场景中系统研究 agent harness 的三大组件（规划指导、执行组织、完成校验）对成功率、错误接受率与成本的影响，发现规划内容的增益约 7.17 个百分点，只读校验器可拒绝 61% 的错误判决且成本极低，而两组件孰重取决于对错误接受的损失设定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent harness 中的规划指导、执行组织与完成校验被广泛使用，但各组件对任务成功、错误接受（false pass）与成本的独立贡献缺乏受控实验证据，因此需要拆解并量化各部分的作用边界与依赖关系。

Method: 在 τ²-bench 上开展两个 Retail 实验与一个 Airline 试点。主要对照为预写任务特定计划（Fixed）与词数匹配的乱序策略文本（Sham），以隔离"指导内容"本身的作用；并引入只读终端校验器（read-only terminal verifier），评估其拒绝 oracle 无效轨迹与保留正确轨迹的能力及成本。成功判定采用 oracle 验证，并报告任务聚簇 bootstrap 区间。

Result: 在 265 个匹配单元上，Fixed 相对 Sham 使 oracle 验证成功率提升 7.17 个百分点（90% 任务聚簇 bootstrap 区间 1.15–13.36），增益集中于高复杂度任务。只读校验器拒绝 61% 的 Retail oracle 无效轨迹，同时误拒 17% 的正确轨迹，每回合额外成本不足 1 美分。

Conclusion: 组件重要性取决于对错误接受所赋予的损失权重：低责任代价下规划增益占主导，高责任代价下校验器避免的 false pass 占主导。独立校验器能以远低于完整"规划+校验"栈的成本，捕获其几乎全部的减少 false pass 收益。

Abstract: Agent harnesses supply planning guidance, organize execution, and check completion. We study how these components affect success, erroneous acceptance, and cost in two Retail experiments and an Airline pilot in $τ^2$-bench. The primary comparison pairs prewritten task-specific plans (Fixed) with shuffled policy text matched in word count (Sham), isolating the contribution of guidance content. Across 265 matched cells, Fixed improves oracle-verified success by 7.17 percentage points (90\% task-clustered bootstrap interval, 1.15--13.36 points), with gains concentrated in higher-complexity tasks. A read-only terminal verifier rejects 61\% of Retail oracle-invalid episodes while withholding 17\% of correct ones, at less than one cent of additional cost per episode. Which component matters more depends on the loss assigned to erroneous acceptance: at low liability the planning gain dominates; at high liability the verifier's avoided false passes dominate---and a standalone verifier captures nearly all the false-pass benefit of the full planning-plus-verification stack at a fraction of its cost.

</details>


### [79] [SoL-Pi: Recursively Scaling Auto-Research Loops for Efficient Agent Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20519)
*Haozhe Liu,Tian Ye,Sensen Gao,Qihang Cao,Yitong Li,Mingchen Zhuge,Duomin Wang,Ruihua Zhang,Ping Luo,Jiawang Bian,Lei Zhu,Ligeng Zhu,Enze Xie,Song Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将递归自我改进（RSI）思路引入智能体框架（harness）层，通过跨多环境的自动研究循环筛选出四种可复用机制构成 SoL-Pi，在保持性能的同时大幅降低 token 消耗与成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体正从受监督的代码补全转向无人值守的长时间自主探索，其工作形态从孤立预测扩展为推理、工具调用与反馈的长轨迹。因此 token 效率对扩展递归自我改进变得关键，而现有框架在该维度上缺乏系统性优化。

Method: 采用受 RSI 启发的 harness 层方法，在数量与多样性不断增长的环境中扩展自动研究（auto-research）循环以进行 harness rollout，并在该规模下筛选出可迁移到开发环境之外的通用改进。最终保留四条机制，覆盖动作执行、上下文压缩、观测处理和委托式阅读，构成 SoL-Pi。

Result: 在 51 项任务的 EdgeBench 评测上，SoL-Pi 在 GPT-5.6 Sol 与 Opus 5 上达到与 Pi 相当的性能，同时将记录的 token 流量降低 44.7-49.0%，API 成本降低约三分之一。相对原生 Codex 与 Claude Code harness 估计每小时节省 \$8.75-\$13.50，相对 Pi 节省 \$4.36-\$5.71。

Conclusion: 在 harness 层进行大规模自动研究循环可产出跨环境可复用的效率改进，推动自动化 harness 发现走向生产级结果，表明 token 效率优化是支撑递归自我改进规模化的可行且高收益的路径。

Abstract: As coding agents move from supervised code completion to unattended, around-the-clock exploration, their work expands from isolated predictions into long trajectories of reasoning, tool use, and feedback. Token efficiency therefore becomes important for scaling recursive self-improvement. We take an RSI-inspired approach at the harness layer, scaling auto-research loops across increasingly numerous and diverse environments for harness rollouts. At this scale, the process yields reusable improvements that transfer beyond their development setting, moving automated harness discovery toward production-level outcomes. Four mechanisms survive selection and form SoL-Pi, spanning action execution, context compaction, observation handling, and delegated reading. On the 51-task EdgeBench evaluation, SoL-Pi achieves performance comparable to Pi across GPT-5.6 Sol and Opus 5 while reducing recorded token traffic by 44.7-49.0% and API cost by about one third. In other words, estimated hourly savings are \$8.75-\$13.50 relative to native Codex and Claude Code harnesses, and \$4.36-\$5.71 relative to Pi.

</details>


### [80] [FreqCondNorm: Towards Cross-domain Predictive Maintenance through a Frequency-Conditioned Transformer Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20535)
*Zaynab Raounak,Camille LHermine,Zhiguo Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FreqCondNorm：FiLM式频率条件归一化+Transformer统一异构时序，预训练实现跨采样频率迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习预测性维护模型跨机器与工况迁移性差，尤其标签稀缺且信号采样频率跨越五个数量级时问题突出。

Method: 在Transformer架构中引入FiLM式频率条件归一化层，将异构时序统一到单一模型；在CWRU、MFPT、UOC18、PRONOSTIA、CMAPSS五个公开数据集上，采用掩码自编码与对比学习并结合均衡域采样进行预训练。

Result: 故障诊断方面，CWRU准确率达99.2%（较CNN提升6.4个百分点），MFPT零样本准确率82.1%，表现出跨采样频率的强迁移性；但方法未提升剩余使用寿命（RUL）预测性能。

Conclusion: 验证了频率条件归一化在跨频率迁移故障诊断中的有效性，同时指出预训练目标与RUL任务存在错配，需后续研究。

Abstract: Deep learning predictive maintenance models suffer from poor transferability across machines and operating conditions, especially when labelled data are scarce and signals span five orders of magnitude in sampling frequency (1 Hz to ~100 kHz). We propose FreqCondNorm, a Transformer-based architecture that introduces a FiLM-style frequency-conditioned normalization layer to unify heterogeneous time-series within a single model. The architecture is pretrained on five public predictive maintenance datasets (CWRU, MFPT, UOC18, PRONOSTIA, CMAPSS) using masked auto-encoding and contrastive learning with balanced domain sampling. On fault diagnosis, the model achieves 99.2% accuracy on CWRU (+6.4 pp over CNN) and 82.1% zero-shot accuracy on MFPT, demonstrating strong transfer across sampling frequencies. However, the approach does not improve remaining useful life prediction, suggesting a mismatch between pretraining and RUL objectives that warrants future investigation.

</details>


### [81] [Refuse, Decompose, Refresh: A Claim-Safe Protocol for Closed-Loop AI Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20538)
*Peiying Zhu,Sidi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"claim-safe"评估协议(Refuse/Decompose/Refresh)，在纯聚合仿真中验证：弃权成为结果的一部分，假接纳率上界0.1391，并证明"零假设"必须相对于参考分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI评估可以完全可复现却仍支持错误结论，这一风险在闭环系统中尤为严重：策略会决定访问状态、可观测组件以及哪些失败留下可测痕迹。因此需要一套确保结论合法性的协议，而非仅追求可复现性或单一PASS/FAIL判定。

Method: 提出claim-safe协议含三个动作：(1) Refuse——当缺乏干净参考流或匹配的运行期比较时弃权；(2) Decompose——将协议执行、运行期假接纳(operational false admission)与结构性假设分开报告，而非合并为一个PASS/FAIL标签；(3) Refresh——将分布漂移告警视为使参考图失效并重算的请求，而非故障证据。在纯聚合仿真中实例化：24个策略组件、3种需求regime、2类故障掩码族、独立的开发与heldout种子；预注册heldout含1,440个案例与21,600行分区数据。

Result: 仅55/72个regime-component单元通过参考接纳，其中54/55在运行期仍被接纳，弃权构成结果的一部分。稳定假接纳为0/20个被代表组件，在冻结0.20规则下单侧精确95%上界为0.1391。在接纳单元内，受影响的干净流量比名义故障单元占比更具预测性：540行unit-arm数据嵌套于20个组件簇中，cell-minus-traffic的负对数似然差为0.1264 nats/行，95%组件簇区间[0.0593, 0.1918]。漂移日志显示"null"必须相对于参考：干净故障零流在三种regime下分别触发15/15、0/15、14/15告警，仅中间regime与冻结检测器参考匹配。

Conclusion: 不应追求普适阈值，而是提供一份可执行契约，将可观测支持、统计校准与有依据的结论三者链接起来；弃权与参考相对性应被纳入评估结果本身。

Abstract: An AI evaluation can be perfectly reproducible and still support the wrong claim. This risk is acute in closed-loop systems: policy determines visited states, observable components, and which failures leave a measurable trace. We propose a claim-safe protocol with three actions. Refuse: abstain when a clean reference stream or matched runtime comparison lacks support. Decompose: report protocol execution, operational false admission, and structural hypotheses separately rather than as one PASS/FAIL label. Refresh: treat distribution-shift alarms as requests to invalidate and recompute a reference map, not as fault evidence. We instantiate the protocol in an aggregate-only simulator with 24 policy components, three demand regimes, two fault-mask families, and independent development and heldout seeds. The preregistered heldout contains 1,440 cases and 21,600 partition rows. Only 55/72 regime-component units were reference-admitted and 54/55 remained runtime-admitted, making abstention part of the result. Stable false admission was 0/20 represented components, with a one-sided exact 95% upper bound of 0.1391 under a frozen 0.20 rule. Within admitted units, affected clean traffic outpredicted nominal fault-cell fraction: across 540 unit-arm rows nested in 20 component clusters, the cell-minus-traffic negative-log-likelihood difference was 0.1264 nats per row, with a 95% component-cluster interval of [0.0593, 0.1918]. A drift log shows why "null" must be reference-relative: clean fault-null streams triggered 15/15, 0/15, and 14/15 alarms across three regimes, while only the middle regime matched the frozen detector reference. Rather than a universal threshold, we contribute an executable contract linking observable support, statistical calibration, and justified claims.

</details>


### [82] [Language-model groups overstate consensus when replaying human deliberation on a reasoning task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20543)
*Tengfei Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究将100组人类Wason推理小组与匹配的LLM智能体小组进行对照重演，在统一评分口径下比较全体共识率，并通过两种去盲法敏感性分析、去停步与去可记忆答案的重参数化，检验模拟共识的稳健性及其与集体准确率的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全体共识率常被视为集体认知的指标，但其数值高度依赖"参与度"与"最终状态"的操作化定义。人类讨论中存在大量不发言者，而LLM智能体几乎总会发言，这种测量不对称可能人为夸大或掩盖人机共识差异，因此需要在评分口径明确的前提下评估模拟小组对人群审议结果的估计效度。

Method: 以100组留出的人类Wason任务讨论组为基准，构建匹配的LLM智能体小组：按每名参与者的讨论前答案植入一个信念锚定智能体，并用同一套代码对人与智能体评分。在不同人类评分定义下估计共识率，并开展两种去盲敏感性分析——基于提交样本的比较（n=98）与参与度匹配的比较（n=45）。进一步通过取消提前停止、以及移除可记忆答案的重参数化来检验差异的稳健性。

Result: 不同人类评分定义下的共识率估计为24.0%至57.0%；约五分之一参与者从未发言，而智能体几乎全部发言。两种去盲分析均显示智能体组共识更强：提交样本比较中，聊天与推理模式的差距分别为34.0和43.9个百分点；参与度匹配比较中分别为34.1和44.4个百分点，两条路径虽校正不同的测量不对称，但结果相差在0.5个百分点以内。取消提前停止后差距依旧存在；移除可记忆答案后，推理模式小组近乎全体一致，但多数一致于错误答案。模拟共识与集体准确率不相关，信念锚定智能体组对该情境下人类组结果分布的估计存在偏差。

Conclusion: 全体共识率的数值实质依赖于评分与参与度的操作化方式，人机共识差距在多种校正与重参数化下稳健存在，且模拟出的一致性并不等同于集体准确性。该研究为评估"模拟小组能否估计人类审议结果"提供了评分口径显式化的分析框架。

Abstract: Full-consensus rates are often treated as indicators of collective cognition, yet depend on how participation and final states are operationalized. We replayed 100 held-out human Wason groups with matched large language model (LLM) agent groups, seeding one belief-anchored agent per participant's pre-discussion answer and scoring agents and people with the same code. Across human scoring definitions, estimates ranged from 24.0% to 57.0%; about one fifth of participants never posted, whereas agents almost always did. Agent groups remained more consensual in two post-unblinding sensitivity analyses: the submit-based comparison (n = 98) yielded gaps of 34.0 and 43.9 percentage points for chat and reasoning modes, and the participation-matched comparison (n = 45) yielded gaps of 34.1 and 44.4 points. These complementary routes reduced different measurement asymmetries yet converged within 0.5 percentage points. The gap persisted without early stopping and under a reparameterization removing the memorizable answer; reasoning-mode groups then agreed nearly unanimously, mostly on incorrect answers. Simulated consensus did not track collective accuracy, and belief-anchored agent groups were biased estimators of the human group-outcome distribution in this setting. These analyses provide a scoring-explicit basis for assessing simulated-group estimates of human deliberative outcomes.

</details>


### [83] [Limits of Confidence in Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20581)
*Russ Webb,Amitis Shidani,Alice Bizeul,Dan Busbridge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 离散扩散采样器每一步从逐位置分布并行写入多个词元，只有当所写入位置在已固定词元条件下条件独立时，该步才与训练分布匹配；逐位置分布之积无法匹配存在依赖的组，且逐位置分布无法判定组是否依赖。合成任务ScanAndAdd上，置信度排序写入的每个≥2未定位置组均为依赖组，生成分布总变差达采样噪声下限的29倍，而逐样本指标仍为1.0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散（remasking 与 uniform-state 采样器）逐步并行解码多个位置，是当前文本/图像/语音生成的重要范式，但其并行写入步骤是否忠实于训练分布缺乏理论刻画。作者希望从联合分布与逐位置边缘分布的关系出发，揭示并行采样的根本性局限，并给出可验证的实证证据。

Method: 理论分析：证明一个采样步匹配训练分布当且仅当所写入位置在已固定词元条件下条件独立；证明任何逐位置分布之积都无法匹配存在依赖的组；并构造两个联合分布具有相同逐位置边缘分布但在取值组合是否出现上不同，从而说明逐位置分布不足以判定组依赖性。实证：在联合分布有闭式解的合成任务 ScanAndAdd 上，检查置信度排序所写入的每个大小≥2的未定位置组是否依赖，并测量生成分布与采样噪声下限的总变差之比，同时报告逐样本指标。

Result: 在 ScanAndAdd 上，置信度排序所写入的每一个由两个或更多未定位置构成的组都被验证为依赖组；生成分布的总变差达到采样噪声下限的 29 倍，而逐样本指标（per-sample metrics）为 1.0，说明常规逐样本评价无法察觉并行采样导致的联合分布偏差。

Conclusion: 并行写入多位置的离散扩散采样存在结构性缺陷：逐位置分布既不能表达组内依赖，也不能作为依赖性的判据，因而无法匹配训练分布。ScanAndAdd 实验以分布级度量（29× 噪声下限）暴露了这一偏差，而逐样本指标（1.0）完全掩盖了问题，提示离散扩散研究需采用分布级评估而非仅依赖逐样本指标。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion, including remasking and uniform-state samplers, generate a sequence by writing multiple token positions per step, drawing each from a per-position distribution and choosing which positions to write from those same distributions. For domains of general interest (pixels, phonemes, or words) there are inherent dependencies between tokens. We show that a step matches the training distribution only when the positions it writes are conditionally independent given the tokens already fixed, that no product of per-position distributions can match a dependent group, and that per-position distributions do not determine whether a group is dependent: two joint distributions can have identical per-position marginals while differing in which combinations of values occur. On ScanAndAdd, a synthetic task whose joint distribution is available in closed form, we verify that every group of two or more undetermined positions a confidence ranking writes is dependent, and measure the generated distribution to be $29\times$ the sampling-noise floor total variation while per-sample metrics are $1.0$.

</details>


### [84] [PAA: The Probabilistic Allen Algebra: A Generative and Complete Probabilistic Extension of Allen's Interval Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20634)
*Julian Eggert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出概率Allen代数(PAA),将区间关系概率由边界分布生成,并给出完整分区、层级与分解性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Allen区间代数的13种基本关系是精确边界上的清晰谓词,无法处理语言、感知、数据库或不确定历史中时间、持续时间与边界的不确定性,也无法表达"just before""roughly during"等具有程度含义的表达。

Method: 时间点为高斯分布;区间具有高斯中点与截断高斯持续时间。每种关系在统一概率空间中表示为边界序关系谓词:点-点关系化为误差函数,点-区间和区间-区间关系化为由线性不等式诱导的多元高斯象限概率(orthant概率)。接触关系(meets, starts, finishes, equals)通过容差带获得正测度,在单一容差下13种关系构成真正分区,容差趋零时退化为清晰Allen代数。关系进一步分解为相关性感知的时间原语,类似CIDOC CRM。

Result: 推导出Allen分类而非先验设定:precedence、overlap、containment等粗粒度谓词是叶节点的并集,其概率为叶概率之和;该层级在区间塌缩为点时保持不变,13种关系约化为5种再约化为3种。代数具有尺度不变性,并能区分"shortly before"等程度表达与接触关系。所有结果经Monte-Carlo验证,并以开放、带测试的Python包发布。

Conclusion: PAA为定性时间推理提供了生成式、完整且概率化的扩展,既保持与经典Allen代数的极限一致性,又能表达带程度的不确定时间关系,并具备可实现的软件工具支持。

Abstract: Allen's interval algebra is a qualitative calculus for temporal relations, but its thirteen base relations are crisp predicates over exact interval boundaries. This is inadequate for temporal information from language, perception, databases, or uncertain histories, where times, durations, and boundaries are uncertain and expressions such as "just before" or "roughly during" have graded meaning. We develop the probabilistic Allen algebra (PAA): a generative and complete extension in which relation probabilities are derived from distributions over interval boundaries rather than assigned as scores. Time points are Gaussian; intervals have Gaussian midpoints and truncated-Gaussian durations. Every relation is a boundary-ordering predicate in one common probability space: point-point relations reduce to error functions, and point-interval and interval-interval relations to multivariate Gaussian orthant probabilities induced by linear inequalities. Contact relations (meets, starts, finishes, equals) receive positive measure through a tolerance band, and under a single tolerance the thirteen relations form a true partition that recovers crisp Allen as the tolerance vanishes. The construction derives Allen's taxonomy rather than positing it: coarse predicates such as precedence, overlap, and containment are unions of leaves whose probabilities are leaf sums, and this hierarchy is preserved as intervals collapse to points and thirteen relations reduce to five and then three. Each relation further decomposes into correlation-aware temporal primitives in the spirit of CIDOC CRM. The algebra is scale-invariant and separates graded expressions such as "shortly before" from contact relations. All results are Monte-Carlo validated and shipped as an open, tested Python package.

</details>


### [85] [Ownership in AI-Assisted Everyday Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20658)
*Megan Wei,Melanie Subbiah,Audrey Lee,Annya Dahmani,Dave Edwards,Helen Edwards,Ellie Pavlick*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 一项探索性质性调研发现，人在与AI协作任务中的"拥有感"取决于协作过程的角色：仅审批AI建议会导致心理弃权，而主导、迭代、重写则保留拥有感；此外，即便执行由AI完成，只要愿景属于自己，拥有感仍可很高；个人声音的丧失与对输出的不理解会削弱拥有感；而披露AI使用的意愿往往与真实的骄傲或拥有感脱钩，更多受社群规范与对"功劳被抹去"的担忧影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI被编织进日常任务，当机器参与到"我们所创造之物"的生产过程中时，人的拥有感（ownership）与贡献感（contribution）究竟会发生什么变化，这一问题尚未被充分考察。作者希望厘清何种协作方式能让AI辅助的成果依然"像是自己的"。

Method: 采用探索性质性调研（exploratory qualitative survey）。参与者被要求描述两项近期自选的、借助AI完成的任务：一项感觉"属于自己的"，另一项则不然。通过对这类对照性叙述的分析，提炼影响拥有感的因素。

Result: 拥有感取决于协作过程：仅审批（approve）AI建议时人会"弃权"（disown）；主导、迭代、重写则保有拥有感。拥有感也可延伸至"拥有愿景但不拥有执行"的情境——受访者报告说，在那些没有AI就无法完成的任务上拥有感很高。个人声音的丧失与对输出的缺乏理解都会侵蚀拥有感。此外，披露AI使用的意愿常与实际的骄傲或拥有感脱钩，而由社群规范和"功劳被抹去"的恐惧塑造。

Conclusion: 作者据此提出若干研究方向，旨在推动AI的发展以支持人们对自己生活的"作者感"（sense of authorship）。

Abstract: When does work done with AI still feel like ours? As AI becomes woven into everyday tasks, we must examine what happens to our sense of ownership and contribution when a machine shares in producing what we make. We report an exploratory qualitative survey in which participants were asked to describe two recent, self-selected tasks completed with AI: one that felt like their own and one that did not. We find that felt ownership depends on the process of collaboration: people disown work when they merely approve AI's suggestions, but retain ownership when they lead, iterate, or rewrite. Ownership can also extend to settings where people own the vision for a project but not the execution; respondents reported high ownership on tasks they could not have completed without AI. Loss of personal voice and a lack of comprehension of the output both erode ownership. Finally, willingness to disclose AI use is often decoupled from actual pride or ownership, and instead shaped by community norms and fear of credit erasure. We propose several research directions as a result of these findings to promote AI development that supports people's sense of authorship over their own lives.

</details>


### [86] [Deep Noir: Autonomous Steering Discovery via Architectural Chronometry in Transformer Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20722)
*Frank E. Bobe,Gregory D. Vetaw,Darshan W. Bryner,Matthew G. Cook,Jose L. Salas-Vernis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Deep Noir框架利用Logit Lens收敛与因果注意力头归因，自动发现激活引导的最优干预点与强度，跨任务跨架构显著提升性能，并揭示引导强度与提示注入漏洞的正相关关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导可在推理阶段改变大模型行为，但现有方法中"在哪里引导、以多大强度引导"仍依赖人工试错，缺乏自动化、可迁移的发现机制，制约了其规模化应用。

Method: 提出Deep Noir框架：结合Logit Lens收敛分析定位有效干预层，并利用因果层面的注意力头归因（causal head-level attribution）确定引导位置与强度，从而自主发现最优引导参数；无需修改代码即可迁移到新任务。

Result: 在三个规模层级（1B×3、2-3B×2、7-9B×4）验证：1B模型垃圾信息任务提升16.7个百分点（标准差4.7，39次运行），7-9B在四种架构上提升21至42个百分点；SST-2情感任务零代码改动提升13.1个百分点。情感任务中RepE不做头掩码无法超越基线，而Deep Noir在所有模型上均有提升（p<0.01）。此外，引导强度与提示注入攻击面呈单调递增的脆弱性关系。

Conclusion: 机制可解释性为引导点的自动发现提供了依据，所得干预点可跨任务与架构泛化；同时，部署已引导分类器的智能体系统需重视随引导强度上升的提示注入安全风险。

Abstract: Activation steering modifies LLM behavior at inference time, but identifying where and how strongly to steer remains manual. We introduce Deep Noir, a framework that uses Logit Lens convergence and causal head-level attribution to autonomously discover optimal steering parameters. Across three scales (1B x 3, 2-3B x 2, and 7-9B x 4), our engine achieves 16.7 percentage-point improvement on spam at 1B (standard deviation 4.7; 39 runs), with gains increasing to 21 to 42 percentage points at 7-9B across four architectures. On SST-2 sentiment, it achieves a 13.1 percentage-point improvement with zero code changes. Mechanistic grounding enables automated discovery of intervention points that generalize across tasks and architectures. On sentiment, RepE without head masking fails to improve over baseline, while Deep Noir improves all models (p less than 0.01). We further show that steering creates a predictable prompt-injection attack surface whose vulnerability increases monotonically with steering magnitude. This finding is relevant to agent systems deploying steered classifiers.

</details>


### [87] [Q&A on Any Spreadsheet Requires Interpreting Its Grid Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20732)
*Zofia Smoleń*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于单元格角色标注的电子表格分块框架，超越现有方法但受限于离散分类的固有瓶颈，主张转向降维技术以直接将二维非结构化表格扁平化为一维文本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升LLM驱动RAG系统中电子表格的分块可解释性，从而通过丰富的上下文辅助答案生成，而非仅依赖检索准确性。

Method: 提出一种将任意电子表格拆分为可解释分块的新框架，核心是基于单元格角色标注（cell role annotation）进行语义单元格标注与分块。

Result: 该框架优于当前最先进方法，但遭遇硬性上限；即使达到人类水平的标注，因电子表格是二维非结构化数据、单元关系连续且角色潜在无限，而分类模型仅限有限预定义类别，无法完美捕捉结构细微差异。

Conclusion: 解决电子表格到LLM的瓶颈需超越离散单元格分类，应发展降维技术，将二维非结构化电子表格直接扁平化为一维非结构化文本，使文本分块更易被下游RAG解释和生成。

Abstract: Semantic cell annotation improves chunking interpretability for spreadsheets in LLM-driven RAG systems, aiding answer generation through enriched context rather than improved retrieval accuracy. We propose a novel framework of splitting any spreadsheet into interpretable chunks using cell role annotation. Our framework beats the state of the art, yet it faces a hard ceiling. Spreadsheets are fundamentally two-dimensional unstructured data with continuous relationships and infinite potential cell roles. Because classification models are restricted to finite, pre-defined classes, they cannot perfectly capture this structural nuance, even with human-level annotation. We show that addressing the spreadsheet-to-LLM bottleneck requires moving beyond discrete cell classification. Instead, the field must develop dimensionality-reduction techniques to directly flatten 2D unstructured spreadsheets into 1D unstructured text. Text chunks would be easier for downstream RAG to interpret and generate from.

</details>


### [88] [RAFT: A Stateful Retrieval-Augmented Framework for Troubleshooting Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20754)
*Mingxuan Zhang,Xiaowen Wang,Anupma Sharan,Zhengyi Chen,Chenyu Diana Zhang,Shanshan Yang,Chittibabu Pacharu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RAFT，一种面向企业客服排障代理的状态化RAG框架，将历史工单抽象为时间线条目链并在条目级检索，从而在活跃工单的中间状态上匹配相似案例并返回以匹配状态为锚点的父案例轨迹；同时提供可选的案例级图结构。在基于Microsoft Learn文档的合成基准与真实Apache Jira重复标签数据上，RAFT在工单进展各阶段均提升Case Hit，且相对最强基线具统计显著性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统将客服工单视为静态文档，忽略了真实排障过程的多阶段、状态化特征，导致检索到的指导无法匹配当前故障所处的中间状态。企业排障代理需要的是与活跃工单当前状态对齐的可执行指引，而传统检索在条目粒度与状态锚定上均存在缺失。

Method: RAFT将每个已关闭历史案例抽象为一条有向的时间线条目链，检索在条目级进行，从而发现中间状态与活跃案例相匹配的历史案例，并返回以该匹配状态为锚点的父案例轨迹；此外引入可选的案例级图，通过可配置的相似度表示连接不同案例。评估仅针对该检索层，无需生产环境部署。数据方面，因公开多阶段排障数据稀缺，作者构建了基于Microsoft Learn Windows Server文档的合成基准，并搭配带有真实人工重复标签的Apache Jira工单作为真实数据。

Result: RAFT在工单进展的每个阶段都优于vanilla RAG与GraphRAG基线，且相对最强基线的提升具有统计显著性；Apache Jira上的结果提供了方向性证据，表明该优势可迁移至真实案例历史。作者开源了基准、实现与Apache Jira评估集。

Conclusion: 将客服案例建模为状态化时间线并在条目级检索，可有效提升多阶段排障场景下的案例命中率；该检索层可独立评估、无需生产部署，为排障代理的可扩展评估提供了可行路径。未来可进一步验证其在真实生产环境与更广泛领域中的效果。

Abstract: Effective troubleshooting agents in enterprise customer support depend on retrieving actionable guidance from similar historical cases, yet existing retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems treat support cases as static documents and overlook their multi-stage, stateful nature. We introduce RAFT (Retrieval-Augmented Framework for Troubleshooting Agents), a stateful RAG framework that abstracts each closed historical case into a directed chain of timeline entries and retrieves at the entry level, surfacing cases whose intermediate states match the active case and returning the parent-case trajectory anchored at the matched state; an optional case-level graph links cases through a configurable similarity representation. We evaluate this retrieval layer directly, which, unlike evaluating a full agent system, requires no production deployment. Because public multi-stage troubleshooting data is extremely rare, we pair a synthetic benchmark built from Microsoft Learn Windows Server documentation with real Apache Jira issues carrying human-created duplicate labels. RAFT improves Case Hit over vanilla RAG and GraphRAG baselines at every stage of case progress, with statistically significant gains over the strongest baseline; the Jira results provide directional evidence that the advantage transfers to real case histories. We release our benchmark, implementation, and the Apache Jira evaluation set.

</details>


### [89] [An Empirical Study of Harness Design for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20804)
*Run-Ze Fan,Zihao Zhang,Simin Ma,Yebowen Hu,Shouju Wang,Kaiqiang Song,Fei Liu,Hamed Zamani,Xiaoyang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过固定执行循环、仅变化规划、动作空间与上下文管理三个组件，在4个模型、SWE-Bench Verified与Terminal-Bench 2.1上评估176个匹配设置，发现上下文管理在预算收紧时价值最大、规则化裁剪先于LLM摘要最有效、规划对弱模型是准确率支架而对强模型是成本节省器、预定义工具仅在bash能力弱的模型上有增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作通常将编码harness作为整体系统评估，无法判断各组件各自的贡献，因此需要组件级对比来指导模块化的harness设计。作者希望回答"harness中哪些组件真正起作用、在何种条件下起作用"。

Method: 构建一个轻量级编码harness，固定其执行循环，仅变化三个组件：规划（planning）、动作空间（action space）、上下文管理（context management）。在4个模型上于SWE-Bench Verified与Terminal-Bench 2.1评估176个匹配设置，覆盖5种上下文管理策略、4种上下文窗口预算，以及针对规划与动作空间的有针对性消融，并进行轨迹级分析。

Result: （1）上下文管理收益随上下文窗口预算收紧而增大，收益主要来自避免上下文溢出失败。（2）先做基于规则的裁剪再做LLM摘要的staged策略整体效率最高；让被裁剪内容可恢复会增加模型很少使用的机制且无准确率增益。（3）规划对弱模型是准确率支架，对强模型转为成本节省器，准确率变化不大。（4）预定义工具提升bash能力较弱模型的表现；bash能力强的模型仅用bash接口即可有效工作且成本显著更低，尤其在命令行任务上。

Conclusion: 轨迹级分析解释了上述效应：上下文管理延长执行轨迹而不显著改变agent行为，规划改变轨迹终止位置，动作空间改变代码编写的粒度。研究结果为模型与预算感知的harness设计提供依据，并给出可模块化评估未来harness组件的框架。

Abstract: Coding harnesses shape how autonomous coding agents translate model capabilities into long-horizon software-engineering performance, yet existing work typically evaluates harnesses as monolithic systems, leaving the effectiveness of individual components unclear. To enable component-level comparisons, we study this question with a lightweight coding harness whose execution loop is fixed while three components are varied: planning, action space, and context management. Across four models evaluated on SWE-Bench Verified and Terminal-Bench 2.1, we evaluate 176 matched settings spanning five context-management strategies, four context-window budgets, and targeted ablations of planning and action space. We find that: (1) Context management becomes increasingly valuable as the context-window budget tightens, with most of its benefit coming from preventing context-overflow failures. (2) Staging rule-based elision before LLM-based summarization provides the strongest overall efficiency among the context-management strategies, whereas making elided content recoverable adds machinery that models rarely use and yields no accuracy gain. (3) Planning shifts from an accuracy scaffold for weaker models to a cost saver for stronger models, with little change in accuracy. (4) Predefined tools improve performance for models with weaker bash proficiency, whereas bash-capable models can operate effectively with a bash-only interface and achieve substantially lower cost, especially on command-line-centric tasks. Trajectory-level analysis explains these effects: context management extends execution trajectories without substantially altering agent behavior, planning changes where trajectories stop, and the action space changes the granularity at which code is written. These findings inform model- and budget-aware harness design and provide a modular framework for evaluating future harness components.

</details>


<div id='cs.CV'></div>

# cs.CV [[Back]](#toc)

### [90] [Open ultrasound foundation model for robust segmentation and clinical measurement across heterogeneous settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19230)
*Chao Qin,Fahad Shahbaz Khan,Salman Khan,Sarim Ather,Siddiq Anwar,Rao Muhammad Anwer,Shadab Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文构建了迄今规模最大的超声开源资源SonoCorpus与超声交互式分割基础模型SonoBase，在跨器官、设备、操作者与地域的15个评测集上全面超越SAM2等基线，临床衍生指标达到观察者间一致性水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声是全球部署最广的影像模态，但临床AI仍碎片化为单一任务模型，设备、操作者或解剖部位变化时即失效，缺乏统一的超声专用基础模型与大规模标注资源。

Method: 整合53个公开数据集，构建含456,963张图像与1,626,085个专家掩码的SonoCorpus，覆盖24个临床应用、17个国家；在其上预训练交互式分割基础模型SonoBase。评测涵盖15个引入新器官、新设备、新操作者与新地域的数据集，与SAM2、MedSAM2、概念提示型MedSAM3以及同数据训练的单任务专家模型对比，并验证少样本适配（5例标注）与训练协议向SAM3的迁移性。

Result: SonoBase在每个评测集上均优于SAM2、MedSAM2与MedSAM3，并匹配同数据专家模型；在完全外部数据上的精度甚至超过基线在其自身分布内基准的表现。射血分数误差6.63%、位于观察者间变异范围内，除颤器适应证阈值误分类率13%（基线18%–42%）；胎儿头围误差1.81 mm、孕龄误差1.2天，均低于观察者间变异。基线完全失败的案例占1/4，SonoBase在81%的此类案例中恢复可用分割，包括塞拉利昂与坦桑尼亚两国低训练水平用户使用手持探头场景。5个标注样本即可适配新场景，同一训练协议可迁移至SAM3，表明优势源于超声专用预训练而非特定架构。

Conclusion: 超声专用大规模预训练是提升跨域泛化与临床可用性的关键，SonoBase可作为社区平台；为保障可复现性，作者公开全部检查点、优化器状态、数据划分索引、去重哈希与入门代码。

Abstract: Ultrasound is the most widely deployed imaging modality worldwide, yet clinical AI remains fragmented into narrow single-task models that fail when device, operator, or anatomy changes. Here we present SonoCorpus, an open resource unifying 456,963 images and 1,626,085 expert masks from 53 public datasets spanning 24 clinical applications and 17 countries, and SonoBase, an interactive segmentation foundation model pretrained on it. Across fifteen evaluation datasets introducing new organs, devices, operators, and geographies, SonoBase outperforms SAM2, MedSAM2, and the concept-promptable MedSAM3 on every dataset and matches per-dataset specialist models trained on the same data; on fully external data it exceeds the accuracy these baselines achieve on their own in-distribution benchmarks. Ejection fraction derived from its segmentations falls within inter-observer variability (6.63\% error), with fewer misclassifications at the defibrillator-candidacy threshold than either promptable baseline (13\% versus 18--42\%); fetal head-circumference (1.81~mm) and gestational-age (1.2 days) errors fall below inter-observer variability. Where a baseline fails outright, one in four test cases, SonoBase recovers a usable segmentation in 81\% of them, including on handheld probes operated by minimally trained users in two low- and middle-income countries (Sierra Leone and Tanzania). Five labeled examples can help the model adapt to a new setting, and the identical training protocol transfers well to newer models such as SAM3, locating the advantage in ultrasound-specific pretraining rather than any single architecture. To ensure reproducibility and enable the community to build on SonoBase as a platform, we release all checkpoints, optimizer states, data-split indices, deduplication hashes, and starter code.

</details>


### [91] [RAUL: Reference-Assisted Ureteroscopy Localization for Skill Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19236)
*Fangjie Li,Mai Bui,Charan Mohan,Michael Miga,Matthieu Chabanas,Nicholas Kavoussi,Jie Ying Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 RAUL，一种仅用输尿管镜视频（体模场景）的参考辅助轨迹重建框架，实现高精度、高覆盖率的镜体轨迹恢复，并据此构建导航技能评估指标，区分不同经验水平的受训者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 输尿管镜碎石手术中解剖结构导航不完整会导致重复干预。熟练外科医生再干预率更低，但缺乏客观指标量化镜体导航表现，无法判断受训者何时达到熟练水平；且外部追踪设备成本高、难以规模化。

Method: 为每个体模先用一段缓慢、高质量的参考探索视频生成参考重建，再将后续探索视频定位到该参考坐标系中，从而仅依赖视频恢复输尿管镜轨迹。以电磁追踪的镜体位姿作为金标准评估定位精度，并从体模探索轨迹计算导航指标，比较不同经验层级住院医师的表现。

Result: 在 9 个体模上，平移均方根误差为 0.5 ± 0.1 mm；相比标准 SfM，逐帧定位覆盖率由 50.5 ± 14.9% 提升至 86.1 ± 7.2%。重建轨迹在既有导航指标上显示高经验与低经验受训者之间存在显著差异。

Conclusion: RAUL 相比标准 SfM 能更完整地从视频恢复输尿管镜轨迹，无需额外追踪设备即可实现基于轨迹的技能评估；据所知，这是首次在无外部追踪传感器条件下仅用视频恢复输尿管镜轨迹用于技能评估，支持输尿管镜导航技能的可扩展自动评估。

Abstract: Objective: Incomplete navigation of anatomy during ureteroscopic kidney stone surgeries can contribute to repeat interventions. While skilled surgeons have lower reintervention rates, there are no objective metrics to quantify scope-navigation performance to evaluate when a trainee becomes skilled. This work aims to recover ureteroscope trajectories from endoscopic video and derive navigation metrics to quantify differences in skill. Methods: We propose RAUL, a reference-assisted reconstruction framework for recovering ureteroscope trajectories from ureteroscope videos only in phantoms. For each phantom, we use a slow, high-quality reference exploration video to generate a reference reconstruction. We localize subsequent exploration videos against this reference. We evaluate localization accuracy against electromagnetically tracked scope pose. We compute navigation metrics from phantom exploration trajectories to compare surgical residents across experience levels. Results: The proposed reference-assisted framework achieves a mean translation root mean square error of $0.5 \pm 0.1$ mm across 9 phantoms. Compared to standard Structure-from-Motion (SfM), the proposed pipeline increases frame-wise localization coverage from $50.5 \pm 14.9\%$ to $86.1 \pm 7.2\%$ of all video frames. The reconstructed trajectories revealed significant differences between high- and low-experience trainees in established navigation metrics. Conclusion: RAUL enables substantially more complete recovery of ureteroscope trajectories from videos compared to standard SfM pipelines, enabling trajectory-based skill assessment without additional tracking equipment. Significance: To the best of our knowledge, this is the first use of video-only recovery of ureteroscope trajectories without external tracking sensors for skill assessment, supporting scalable automated assessment of ureteroscopy navigation skill.

</details>


### [92] [Can Vision-Language Models Judge Olympic Diving? From Reasoning to Scores in Zero-Shot Action Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19354)
*Henry O. Velesaca,David Freire-Obregon,Luigi Miranda,Abel Reyes-Angulo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文评估开源视觉语言模型（VLM）在奥运跳水视频上的零样本动作质量评估能力，提出基于回归的集成框架融合VLM语义推理与分阶段子分数，将Spearman相关从单模型<0.32提升至0.67。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奥运体育中的自动化动作质量评估（AQA）因人体运动复杂性和专家评判主观性而极具挑战，作者希望探索开源VLM在此任务中的零样本能力，并作为可解释、半自动化评估的辅助工具。

Method: 在AQA-7基准数据集上，用开源VLM对跳水视频进行零样本评估；构建基于回归的框架，结合TF-IDF向量化、降维与集成学习，融合VLM生成的语义推理文本与分阶段子分数来预测最终比赛分数。

Result: 单独VLM的Spearman相关低于0.32；所提集成回归框架显著提升性能，四模型配置达到0.67；文本推理特征始终优于原始数值子分数。

Conclusion: VLM在可解释、半自动化的体育表现评估中具有较强潜力，文本推理特征的价值凸显了VLM生成解释的丰富性；代码已公开于GitHub。

Abstract: Automated action quality assessment (AQA) in Olympic sports remains a challenging task due to the complexity of human motion and the subjectivity inherent in expert judging. This work evaluates the capability of open-source Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to perform zero-shot action quality assessment on Olympic diving videos using the AQA-7 benchmark dataset. In this regard, a regression-based framework is pro-posed to leverage both the semantic reasoning and phase-level sub-scores generated by the VLMs, combining TF-IDF vectorization, dimensionality reduction, and ensemble learning to predict final competition scores. Experimental results show that standalone VLMs achieve moderate Spearman correlations below 0.32, while the proposed ensemble regression framework substantially improves performance in the reported evaluation, reaching a Spearman correlation of 0.67 with a four-model configuration. Textual reasoning features con-sistently outperformed raw numerical sub-scores, highlighting the richness of VLM-generated explanations for action quality analysis. These findings suggest that VLMs hold strong potential as assistive tools for explainable and semi-automated sports performance evaluation. The code is publicly available on GitHub https://github.com/hvelesaca/olympic diving judge vlm

</details>


### [93] [Open-vocabulary 3D object detection with promptable segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19358)
*Ömer Faruk Deniz,Mustafa Taha Koçyiğit*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究免训练、开放词汇的3D目标检测：用可提示分割模型SAM3以类别名作文本提示，在环视六相机中生成实例掩码，再借助场景几何将掩码转为度量3D框。在nuScenes上通过固定2D检测、仅替换3D几何来源的三阶段对照实验，量化了各个环节的性能上限与瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶3D检测器依赖大规模人工标注3D框训练，类别表固定、无法识别表外物体。作者希望验证该任务能否在无需训练、开放词汇设定下完成，并厘清性能瓶颈究竟在2D检测还是在3D几何测量。

Method: 以SAM3配合类别文本提示在六路环视相机产生实例掩码；采用"控制变量"的三阶段对照：2D检测固定不变，仅改变3D几何来源——(1)纯图像预测几何，(2)在相同掩码内用免训练规则拟合原始LiDAR点，(3)推理时借用有监督框几何。反向实验中，用同一掩码构造三态相机见证规则来增强仅LiDAR的有监督检测器。另做覆盖率分析。

Result: 纯图像几何达0.183 mAP；掩码内LiDAR点免训练拟合达0.298 mAP / 0.348 NDS，零标注成本；借用有监督框几何提升至0.413 mAP / 0.555 NDS，且类别混淆与置信度校准在替换后仍保持。反向增强使仅LiDAR检测器由0.596提升至0.630 mAP，约为全监督相机融合增益的一半，且无需训练。覆盖率分析显示SAM3对84%范围内目标给出正确命名掩码，官方指标下失败的类别是命名错误或几何上难以拟合，而非未见类别。

Conclusion: 该任务可在免训练、开放词汇设定下求解。核心瓶颈位于3D测量精度而非2D检测，说明提升几何拟合/深度估计比改进分割更关键；相机见证规则也能以零训练成本增强纯LiDAR检测，接近部分有监督融合收益。

Abstract: Three-dimensional object detection for autonomous driving is dominated by detectors trained on large corpora of human-annotated 3D boxes. Such a detector learns a fixed category list, and everything outside it is invisible. This paper asks whether the task can be solved training-free and open-vocabulary. A promptable segmentation model (SAM3), queried with class names as text prompts, supplies instance masks in the vehicle's six surround-view cameras, and the masks are turned into metric 3D boxes using the geometry of the scene. The core is a controlled three-stage comparison on nuScenes in which 2D detection is held fixed and only the source of 3D geometry changes. Geometry predicted from images alone reaches 0.183 mean average precision (mAP) under the official protocol; fitting boxes from raw LiDAR points inside the same masks with training-free rules reaches 0.298 mAP / 0.348 nuScenes detection score (NDS) at zero labeling cost; borrowing supervised box geometry at inference time lifts the same detections to 0.413 mAP / 0.555 NDS, which locates the pipeline's largest deficit in measurement precision rather than 2D detection, while class confusion and confidence calibration survive that substitution. Reversing the direction, a three-state camera-witness rule built from the same masks improves a supervised LiDAR-only detector from 0.596 to 0.630 mAP, roughly half the gain of fully supervised camera fusion, with no training. A coverage analysis shows that SAM3 finds 84% of in-range objects with a correctly named mask; the classes that fail in the official metric are misnamed or geometrically unforgiving, not unseen.

</details>


### [94] [LinePilot Digitizer: Line-Plot Recovery with Manual and Automatic Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19377)
*Fengbo Ma,Rayan Akhtar,Aakash H. Joshi,Xiaoting Li,Haijian Sun,Zhen Xiang,Xianyan Chen,Yiping Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LinePilot Digitizer，通过连续颜色曲线恢复结合三种标定模式实现线图数值序列提取，并发布首个系统评测基准DigitizerBench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从线图中恢复数值序列需要精确的坐标轴标定与可靠的曲线提取，而此前缺乏系统性的评测基准来评估数字化工具的性能。

Method: LinePilot结合基于连续颜色的曲线恢复与三种标定模式（标准、增强、OCR）；构建DigitizerBench基准，采用涵盖信号、渲染与图结构因素的正交设计，结合自动与人工引导评测，并使用FPC-NRMSE指标（对缺失、不可用或严重失准输出赋单位损失）进行评价。

Result: 在DigitizerBench-Full上，LinePilot (OCR)取得最低平均FPC-NRMSE（0.672）与最高可信可用率（38.2%）；在DigitizerBench-Lite上，LinePilot (enhanced)取得最低平均FPC-NRMSE（0.081）、100%输出成功率与最高可信可用率（93.3%）。正交设计支持因子分析以识别影响性能的关键因素。

Conclusion: 三种标定模式在共享曲线恢复流程下，为自动化、用户控制与精度之间提供了实用的权衡方案。

Abstract: Recovering numerical series from line plots requires accurate axis calibration and reliable curve extraction. We present LinePilot Digitizer (LinePilot), which combines continuous color-based curve recovery with three calibration modes: LinePilot (standard), LinePilot (enhanced), and LinePilot (OCR). We also introduce DigitizerBench, the first dedicated benchmark for systematically evaluating digitizer performance, using an orthogonal design spanning signal, rendering, and plot-structure factors with complementary automatic and human-guided evaluations. We evaluate performance using failure-penalized capped normalized root-mean-square error (FPC-NRMSE), which assigns unit loss to missing, unusable, or catastrophically inaccurate outputs. On DigitizerBench-Full, LinePilot (OCR) achieves the lowest mean FPC-NRMSE (0.672) and highest trusted usability (38.2%) among the tested automatic pipelines. On DigitizerBench-Lite, LinePilot (enhanced) achieves the lowest mean FPC-NRMSE (0.081), 100% output success, and highest trusted usability (93.3%). The orthogonal benchmark design further enables factor analysis to identify the factors that most significantly affect digitizer performance. Together, the three calibration modes provide a practical trade-off between automation, user control, and accuracy within a shared curve-recovery workflow.

</details>


### [95] [Riemannian--Lorentz Fusion of Vision Transformers and State-Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19384)
*Badri N. Patro,Vijay S. Agneeswaran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Riemannian–Lorentz 参数融合（RLPF），在异构合并设定下按语义角色对齐 ViT 与 SSM 的参数组并做双分支融合，在 CIFAR-10、Oxford-IIIT Pet、ImageNet-1K 上超过最优单亲模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习的扩展面临数据枯竭、训练成本指数增长与资源集中等瓶颈；模型合并无需梯度下降即可整合预训练检查点，可带来数量级的成本节省。但现有权重空间合并方法通常假设检查点结构对齐、参数形状兼容，难以融合架构与参数形状不同的独立训练视觉模型（如以不同算子实现 token mixing 的 ViT 与 SSM）。

Method: 研究保留两种架构的异构合并设定：按语义角色对齐参数组。RLPF 将已对齐的参数组投影到公共坐标，选取部分坐标提升到双曲空间的 Lorentz 双曲面模型，计算带正则化的测地重心，再解码回两个分支；学习一个门控按输入组合两分支 logits。各组件组使用固定曲率，归一化参数按欧氏处理。

Result: 微调后系统在 CIFAR-10 达 82.37%、Oxford-IIIT Pet 达 75.04%、ImageNet-1K top-1 为 78.58%；对应最优单亲模型为 76.54%、71.42%、76.42%。ImageNet-1K 上报告的微调前初始化达 77.80%。

Conclusion: 结果支持继续研究几何感知的异构融合，但不构成免训练的单检查点合并：RLPF 属两分支混合方法，其门控与所报告的最终模型均经过训练。

Abstract: Scaling deep learning faces critical bottlenecks: data exhaustion, exponential training costs, and resource concentration. Model merging combines pre-trained checkpoints without gradient descent, offering orders-of-magnitude savings versus retraining. Combining independently trained vision models is difficult when their architectures and parameter shapes differ. Existing weight-space merging methods generally assume aligned, shape-compatible checkpoints, whereas a Vision Transformer (ViT) and a state-space model (SSM) implement token mixing with different operators. We study a hybrid Heterogeneous merging setting that retains both architectures while aligning parameter groups by semantic role. Our proposed Riemannian--Lorentz Parameter Fusion (RLPF) method projects aligned groups to common coordinates, lifts selected coordinates to the Lorentz hyperboloid model of hyperbolic space, computes a regularized geodesic barycenter, and decodes the result into the two branches. A learned gate then combines branch logits for each input. Component groups use fixed curvature values, with normalization parameters treated as Euclidean. In the results available in this manuscript, the fine-tuned system obtains 82.37\% on CIFAR-10, 75.04\% on Oxford-IIIT Pet, and 78.58\% top-1 accuracy on ImageNet-1K; the corresponding best-parent accuracies are 76.54\%, 71.42\%, and 76.42\%. On ImageNet-1K, the reported pre-fine-tuning initialization reaches 77.80\%. These results support further study of geometry-aware heterogeneous fusion, but not a training-free single-checkpoint merge: RLPF is a two-branch hybrid whose gate and reported final models are trained.

</details>


### [96] [WZPlanner: Safe End-to-End Path Planning for Autonomous Driving in Work Zones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19393)
*Nishad Sahu,Changzhong Qian,Guangzhou Cai,Shounak Sural,Ragunathan,Rajkumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出WorkZonePlan数据集、WAVE数据生成流程与BoundaryFormer(BF)系列模型，通过联合预测车道边界、工作区边界与驾驶轨迹，提升自动驾驶车辆在施工区(work zone)场景下的行驶安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工作区通过临时交通管控和封路改变车道几何，而车载地图往往缺失这些信息，给自动驾驶感知与规划带来挑战；同时，带结构化几何监督的公开数据集稀缺，限制了模型的泛化能力。

Method: 1) 构建WorkZonePlan数据集：149K+合成样本与5K+真实多模态样本，含车道边界、工作区边界与驾驶轨迹选项的3D标注，并提供76个闭环CARLA场景在三种天气下回放生成的228条Bench2Drive格式评估路线；2) 提出半自动化数据生成流程WAVE；3) 提出基于Transformer的BoundaryFormer(BF)，用slot attention联合预测车道与工作区边界多项式及驾驶轨迹；4) BF++在BF基础上扩展：支持Camera与Camera+LiDAR两种变体，引入度量地平面编码、类型化边界/轨迹查询、长程点锚点、图像空间曲线细化及保守门控LiDAR融合。

Result: 在评估冻结时四种模型共有的211条路线上，BF++-Camera与BF++-Camera+LiDAR的Driving Score分别为63.0与64.4，优于SimLingo(59.3)与TransFuser++(26.1)；同时BF++参数量仅为SimLingo的1/40、TF++的1/10以下，却在Driving Score上更高。

Conclusion: 消融实验表明，使用边界slot特征的独立轨迹解码器相比纯slot attention方案显著提升轨迹预测性能；研究结果支持将车道边界、工作区边界与驾驶轨迹进行联合预测，是提升自动驾驶车辆在施工区安全运行的一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Work zones alter lane geometry through temporary traffic controls and closures that may be absent from on-board maps, challenging autonomous vehicle (AV) perception and planning. Generalization is also limited by scarce public datasets with structured geometric supervision. We present WorkZonePlan, a dataset comprising 149K+ synthetic and 5K+ real-world multimodal samples with 3D annotations for lane boundaries, work zone boundaries, and driving trajectory options. It also provides 76 closed-loop CARLA scenarios replayed under three weather conditions, yielding 228 Bench2Drive-format evaluation routes. We introduce WAVE (Work-zone-focused AV data generation in Virtual and rEal Environments), a semi-automated pipeline for creating the dataset, and BoundaryFormer (BF), a transformer-based model that jointly predicts lane and work zone boundary polynomials and driving trajectories. BF uses slot attention for boundary prediction. Ablations show that a separate trajectory decoder using boundary slot features substantially improves trajectory prediction over a slot-attention-only approach. Building on this finding, BF++ offers Camera and Camera+LiDAR variants with metric ground-plane encoding, typed boundary/trajectory queries, long-range point anchors, image-space curve refinement, and conservative gated LiDAR fusion. On the 211 routes common to all four models at the evaluation freeze, BF++-Camera and BF++-Camera+LiDAR achieve Driving Scores of 63.0 and 64.4, respectively, compared with 59.3 for SimLingo and 26.1 for TransFuser++ (TF++). BF++ is 40 times smaller than SimLingo and more than 10 times smaller than TF++, while achieving higher Driving Scores. These results support jointly predicting lane boundaries, work zone boundaries, and driving trajectories as a promising direction toward safer AV operation in work zones. Code and dataset: https://github.com/Nishad-Sahu/WZPlanner.

</details>


### [97] [RGS: Reflection-aware Gaussian Splatting via Learning Geometry Continuity for Reflective Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19421)
*Xiaobiao Du,Yida Wang,Cheng Bi,Kun Zhan,Xin Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RGS（Reflection-aware Gaussian Splatting）基于物理的延迟渲染框架，利用3D基础模型先验与跨视角形状一致性正则化解决3DGS在反射区域表面塌陷问题，并设计反射感知致密化策略，实现高质量反射与新视角合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D Gaussian Splatting方法在反射区域常出现表面塌陷，导致几何质量差、高光（specular）渲染质量低，难以准确建模镜面反射区域。

Method: 1) 基于物理的延迟渲染框架；2) 引入强大3D基础模型作为几何先验，提出跨视角形状一致性正则化，约束几何表面并利用跨视角约束；3) 设计反射感知致密化策略，捕获不同视角下的高光变化。

Result: 在反射区域生成更平滑的几何表面、减少几何空洞；能更高质量地渲染新视角物体；大量实验表明可稳定渲染高质量反射物体，达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: RGS有效缓解3DGS反射区域表面塌陷，提升几何与高光渲染质量，在新视角合成反射物体任务上取得领先效果。

Abstract: Gaussian Splatting has significantly improved the quality of novel view synthesis with explicit Gaussian representation. However, we observed that existing 3D Gaussian Splatting methods (3DGS) often suffer from surface collapse issues on reflective regions, and thus produce inferior geometry and low-quality specular. In this work, we propose a physically-based deferred rendering framework, named Reflection-aware Gaussian Splatting (RGS), that can accurately model specular regions and improve novel view synthesis performance. Specifically, we found that a powerful 3D foundation model can provide a strong 3D geometric prior to foster correct geometric modeling. Based on this, we propose a cross-view shape consistency regularization to regularize the geometry surface with the large model prior and cross-view constraints. In this manner, our RGS can produce smoother geometric surfaces on reflective regions while reducing geometric hollows. To further improve rendering results on reflective regions, we present a reflection-aware densification strategy that is designed to capture specular variations across various views. With this strategy, our RGS is able to render novel views of objects in higher quality. Extensive experiments demonstrate our method consistently renders high-quality reflective objects, achieving state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [98] [Seeing Abnormal from Normal: Glomerular Abnormality in Representations of Normal Renal Morphology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19444)
*Greta Hasko,Rachit Saluja,Tianyu Shi,Leiyue Zhao,Yuechen Yang,Daniel Reisenbuechler,Tianyuan Yao,Zhenhao Guo,John Cannon,Haichun Yang,Yuankai Huo,Yuling Chi,Lorraine Gudas,Mert R. Sabuncu,Yihe Yang,Ruining Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出NoRDeC框架，利用冻结的Omni-Seg残差U-Net骨干，结合马氏正常参考评分与逐层表征分析，在无需异常标签拟合检测器的情况下实现肾小球病理的细粒度异常检测与表征刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肾小球病理的细粒度评估需区分正常肾小球与全局性、节段性肾小球硬化、废弃、缺血、固化、消失及无肾小管肾小球等多种异常。监督分类要求每个类别都有标注样本，当某些亚型罕见或在训练队列中缺失时不可行。单类异常检测通过建模正常数据并对偏差打分，可检测此前未见过的异常，从而规避该问题。

Method: 使用预训练用于分割结构正常肾脏基元的Omni-Seg冻结残差U-Net骨干（训练时不含异常亚型标签）。提出NoRDeC（Normal-Reference Detection and Characterization）框架，将马氏正常参考评分与逐层表征分析相结合，以判定肾小球病理是否被编码及其位置、空间聚合如何影响检测、以及不同异常是否以不同方式改变层间关系。基于两个机构的肾小球图像，评估骨干各层与聚合策略，将NoRDeC与PaDiM、PatchCore比较，并用中心核对齐（CKA）分析表征。

Result: 第4层配合Center-70聚合取得汇总AUROC为0.926±0.013。NoRDeC在七类异常中的六类以及汇总分析中取得最高AUROC；CKA提示层间关系存在亚型依赖性的变化，而这些变化无法仅由异常分数捕获。正常参考模型仅用正常肾小球拟合，异常标签仅用于配置选择、评估和表征分析中的分组。

Conclusion: 结果表明，冻结的肾脏特征提取器可同时支持肾小球异常的检测与表征层面的刻画，且无需使用异常样本拟合检测器。

Abstract: Fine-grained evaluation of glomerular pathology must distinguish normal glomeruli from abnormalities such as global and segmental glomerulosclerosis, obsolescent, ischemic, solidified, disappearing, and atubular glomeruli. Supervised classification requires labeled examples of every category, which is impractical when subtypes are rare or absent from the training cohort. One-class anomaly detection offers an alternative by modeling normal data and scoring deviations, allowing previously unseen abnormalities to be detected. We use the frozen residual U-Net backbone of Omni-Seg, pretrained to segment structurally normal renal primitives without abnormal-subtype labels. We propose NoRDeC (Normal-Reference Detection and Characterization), a framework combining Mahalanobis normal-reference scoring with layer-wise representation analysis to determine whether and where glomerular pathology is encoded, how spatial aggregation affects detection, and whether abnormalities alter inter-layer relationships differently. Using glomerular images from two institutions, we evaluate backbone layers and aggregation strategies, compare NoRDeC with PaDiM and PatchCore, and analyze representations using centered kernel alignment (CKA). Layer 4 with Center-70 aggregation achieved a pooled AUROC of $0.926\pm0.013$. NoRDeC achieved the highest AUROC in six of seven abnormality categories and in the pooled analysis, while CKA suggested subtype-dependent changes in inter-layer relationships not captured by anomaly scores alone. The normal-reference model is fitted using only normal glomeruli; abnormality labels are used for configuration selection, evaluation, and grouping in the representation analysis. These results show that a frozen renal feature extractor can support both detection and representation-level characterization of glomerular abnormalities without using abnormal examples to fit the detector.

</details>


### [99] [Efficient Unified Multimodal Understanding (EUMU): Winning Solution for the MUMU Track at the 8th LSVOS Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19451)
*Dayoung Kil,Seong-heum Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EUMU是一个面向MUMU挑战赛的统一多模态模型，用单一模型同时完成图像多概念打标、开放词汇目标检测和图像描述，并通过任务间线索复用的推理精炼提升性能，取得挑战赛冠军。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MUMU挑战赛要求用单一高效模型联合完成多概念图像打标、开放词汇目标检测和图像描述三项任务，需在资源约束下实现统一建模，而非独立处理各任务。

Method: 基于共享预训练多模态模型：利用其提示能力完成检测与描述，并在共享视觉特征上训练轻量头部预测质量、场景和事件标签；采用任务感知推理精炼，将任务输出作为跨任务线索——检测中用描述线索找回漏检目标，描述中用检测线索精炼文字以匹配目标，打标中用图像统计精炼质量预测、用描述与检测线索精炼场景和事件预测。

Result: 模型参数量239.169M，推理计算量23.947 GFLOPs，峰值推理显存4.5 GB，挑战赛最终得分17.3409。

Conclusion: 该设计在单一模型内统一三项任务，同时满足挑战赛资源约束，获得第8届LSVOS挑战赛MUMU赛道冠军，代码与模型已开源。

Abstract: The Mobile Unified Multimodal Understanding (MUMU) Challenge requires a single efficient model to jointly perform multi-concept image tagging, open-vocabulary object detection, and image captioning. We present Efficient Unified Multimodal Understanding (EUMU), the winning solution for the MUMU Track of the 8th LSVOS Challenge. EUMU builds on a shared pretrained multimodal model, using its prompt-based capabilities for detection and captioning and training lightweight heads on shared visual features to predict quality, scene, and event tags. Rather than treating the three tasks independently, EUMU applies task-aware inference refinement by reusing task outputs as cross-task cues. For detection, caption cues help recover objects missed by the initial detection. For captioning, detection cues help refine the caption to better reflect the detected objects. For tagging, image statistics refine quality predictions, while caption and detection cues refine scene and event predictions. This design unifies all three tasks within a single model while satisfying the challenge's resource constraints. EUMU contains 239.169M parameters, requires 23.947 GFLOPs, uses 4.5 GB of peak inference memory, and achieves a final challenge score of 17.3409. Code and models are available at https://github.com/Dayoung-Kil/EUMU.

</details>


### [100] [ParticleSplat: Self-supervised Object-centric Latent Particle Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19463)
*Lyuxing He,Daniel Guo,Elizabeth Terveen,Deepak Pathak,David Held,Tal Daniel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ParticleSplat，一种自监督的物体中心学习方法，通过前馈 3D 高斯泼溅将场景分解为表示语义实体的潜在"粒子"。该方法基于 Deep Latent Particles (DLP) 框架，将 2D 潜在粒子扩展为带有新视角合成目标的 3D 潜在粒子空间，在模拟与真实数据集上实现了无监督物体掩码学习、可控 3D 场景编辑，并提升了下游机器人操作任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DLP 框架虽能将图像表示为含位置、尺度、外观等属性的粒子集合，但其本质是 2D 的，无法进行显式的 3D 空间与几何推理，而这对机器人操作等下游任务至关重要。因此需要将物体中心的潜在粒子表示扩展到 3D 空间。

Method: 利用潜在粒子与 3D 高斯基元之间的结构相似性，引入 3D 潜在粒子空间，并以新视角合成目标进行训练。模型将多视角图像与相机位姿联合编码到共享的 3D 物体中心潜在空间，再将粒子转换为粒子对齐的 3D 高斯，其组合重建完整场景。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界数据集上，该公式化方法能够无监督地学习物体掩码，并支持可控的 3D 场景编辑（如在潜在空间中修改粒子以移动物体）。此外，所学的 3D 表示提升了下游机器人操作任务的性能。

Conclusion: 将物体中心学习从 2D 潜在粒子扩展到基于 3D 高斯泼溅的 3D 表示，能够以自监督方式获得具备几何推理能力的场景分解，兼顾无监督掩码、可控编辑与下游任务增益。

Abstract: We present ParticleSplat, a self-supervised object-centric learning method that decomposes scenes into a set of latent ''particles'' representing semantic entities through feedforward 3D Gaussian Splatting. Building on the Deep Latent Particles (DLP) framework, which represents images as a set of particles with attributes such as position, scale, and visual appearance, we address a key limitation of DLP: its inherently 2D nature, which prevents explicit 3D spatial and geometric reasoning that are critical for downstream tasks such as robotic manipulation. Leveraging the structural similarity between latent particles and 3D Gaussian primitives, we introduce a 3D latent particle space trained with a novel view synthesis objective. Our model jointly encodes multiple views with camera poses into a shared 3D object-centric latent space, then transforms particles into particle-aligned 3D Gaussians whose composition reconstructs the full scene. On simulated and real-world datasets, we show that this formulation inherently learns object masks without supervision and supports controllable 3D scene editing, such as moving objects by modifying particles in the latent space. We further establish that the learned 3D representation improves downstream performance on robotic manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [101] [SCOUT: Sim-to-Real Text-Based Person Retrieval by Embedding-Space Prediction over Frozen Video Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19483)
*Abdarahmane Traoré,Andy Couturier,Éric Hervet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SCOUT：在合成训练-真实图库的跨模态行人检索中，用全冻结编码器（V-JEPA + EmbeddingGemma）配合可训练预测器与少量精调杠杆，在 AI City Challenge 2026 Track 4 达到 84.25 mAP@10（单冻结模型 60.63），训练成本约 95 GPU 小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本-行人检索在合成训练数据与真实图像图库之间存在 sim-to-real 差距，现有方案依赖昂贵的微调交叉编码器（如 CMP 需 16 GPU 天）。作者质疑：冻结编码器系统能否与之竞争，从而降低训练成本。

Method: 将跨模态检索建模为嵌入空间预测：可训练预测器（由 Qwen3.5-0.8B 解码器初始化）在双向 InfoNCE 目标下，把冻结视频编码器（V-JEPA）的 patch token 映射到冻结文本编码器（EmbeddingGemma）的嵌入空间，基座模型不微调任何编码器。此外引入两个面向精度的杠杆：对视频编码器的参数高效 ExPLoRA 适配，以及基于视觉语言模型、免训练的属性分解重排序器；并进行本地-公开校准研究。

Result: （1）最佳冻结文本编码器是几何上与视频特征最匹配者，免训练对齐分数对三个候选文本编码器的排序与检索精度完全一致（Spearman ρ=1.0），第四个 LLM 编码器显示该规律依赖度量（邻域重叠 ρ=0.8，线性探针 ρ=-0.2）。（2）两个精度杠杆共提升 2.2 点排行榜 R@1。（3）完整 retrieve-fuse-rerank 系统 84.25 mAP@10，单一冻结模型 60.63；训练约 95 GPU 小时，CMP（16 GPU 天微调）仅作为融合成员之一。

Conclusion: 冻结编码器系统可在大幅降低训练成本的前提下接近甚至整合微调交叉编码器，其竞争力取决于文本编码器几何匹配度与面向 top-rank 精度的干预，且需通过本地-公开校准判断哪些干预可迁移到真实域。

Abstract: Text-based person retrieval under a sim-to-real gap (synthetic training data, a real-image gallery) is usually tackled with costly fine-tuned cross-encoders. We ask whether a frozen-encoder system can compete. We present SCOUT, which casts cross-modal retrieval as prediction in embedding space. A trainable predictor maps the patch tokens of a frozen video encoder into the embedding space of a frozen text encoder under a bidirectional InfoNCE objective, and no encoder is fine-tuned in the base model. The video encoder is V-JEPA, the text encoder is EmbeddingGemma, and the predictor is initialized from a Qwen3.5-0.8B decoder. We make three findings. First, the best frozen text encoder is simply the one whose geometry best matches the video features. A training-free alignment score ranks three candidate text encoders in the same order as their retrieval accuracy on our held-out split (Spearman $ρ= 1.0$); a fourth, LLM-based encoder shows the rule is metric-dependent, holding for a neighborhood-overlap score ($ρ= 0.8$) but not for a linear probe ($ρ= -0.2$). Second, two precision-targeted levers, parameter-efficient ExPLoRA adaptation of the video encoder and a training-free attribute-decomposed reranker built on a vision-language model, improve the top-rank precision that otherwise limits the frozen system, adding 2.2 points of leaderboard R@1. Third, a local-versus-public calibration study explains which interventions transfer to the real domain. On AI City Challenge 2026 Track 4 the full retrieve-fuse-rerank system reaches 84.25 mAP@10 on the final leaderboard, while a single frozen model submitted alone reaches 60.63. Our trained components cost about 95 GPU-hours. CMP, the dataset authors' fine-tuned cross-encoder that trains for sixteen GPU-days, is one fusion member of the full system, not an alternative. Code and annotations: https://github.com/abtraore/SCOUT-ECCV

</details>


### [102] [AMB3R-SLAM: Kilometer-scale SLAM with Hierarchical Backend](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19518)
*Hengyi Wang,Lourdes Agapito*

Main category: cs.CV
