Conclusion: 架构上的必要性（概念瓶颈）并不保证语义有效性；注意力图、门控与生成文本可能给出虚假解释。逐区域状态监督是概念真正被学到（尤其少数/指示疼痛状态）的关键，且评估概念准确率须考虑临床不平衡并采用协议匹配基准。

Abstract: Automated pain recognition from facial expression could make continuous welfare assessment practical in sheep, but adoption depends on trust: a stockperson cannot act on a score that arrives without justification. We ground a model in the Sheep Pain Facial Expression Scale (SPFES) by letting each detected facial region attend over text embeddings of the clinical descriptors and then test whether the resulting explanations mean anything. They do not. Ablating an entire descriptor changes the predicted logit by about $10^{-4}$, and the most-attended cue agrees with the predicted pain level in only $32.6\%$ of regions, although the attention maps, the learned gate, and the generated text all proposed otherwise. We therefore remove the appearance bypass with a concept bottleneck whose classifier reads only SPFES concept scores, supervised by per-region state annotations that image-level pipelines discard. This costs $0.05$--$0.10$ in Cohen's $κ$ but yields concepts that are demonstrably learned: minority pain-indicating states are recovered at $3.5$--$8.3\times$ their base rates, and the ear and eye severity orderings emerge without severity supervision. Removing the supervision alone leaves $κ$ unchanged while concept accuracy falls to $0.109$, showing that architectural necessity does not imply semantic validity. We also show that pooled concept accuracy is misleading under clinical imbalance and provide a cross-validated, protocol-matched benchmark of seven methods on this dataset.

</details>


### [172] [Cross-Architecture Foundation-Model Distillation for Edge Flood Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20441)
*Fabian Schmalstieg,Karsten Mueller,Wojciech Samek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将3亿参数Prithvi-EO-2.0洪水分割教师模型蒸馏为0.7M参数EfficientViT-B0学生模型，借助教师监督无标注Sentinel-2数据扩充训练集，实现Jetson Xavier NX上1.5MB INT8 TensorRT边缘部署，并指出固定MNDWI阈值在干净基准上具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理空间基础模型洪水分割性能强，但参数量大，难以在内存受限的边缘硬件上部署；同时人工标注数据有限（仅252景Sen1Floods11训练场景），难以直接训练小模型。

Method: 以在252景人工标注Sen1Floods11上微调的300M参数Prithvi-EO-2.0为教师，蒸馏到0.7M参数的EfficientViT-B0学生；教师对额外无标注Sentinel-2影像生成监督信号，扩大训练集而不新增人工标注；设置几何匹配对照以隔离标签来源影响；采用激活替换与量化感知训练（QAT）导出INT8 TensorRT引擎。

Result: 在252景同预算下，教师监督训练与直接训练相当，并在STURM-Flood多配置上更优；教师监督池扩至2500景后，float学生在Sen1Floods11测试集水类IoU达0.787（教师0.822），在STURM-Flood上匹配教师，在WorldFloods-v2上仍低于教师；INT8引擎在Jetson Xavier NX上每张512×512图像GPU计算5.57ms、运行时显存约14MB、模型1.5MB；固定MNDWI阈值在两个干净外部基准上与两模型相当。

Conclusion: 基础模型监督可将固定人工标注预算放大为显著更大的训练集，并产出紧凑、可部署的边缘模型；干净外部基准应视为泛化测试，而非学习模型优于光谱规则的证据。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models can provide strong flood-segmentation performance, but their size limits deployment on memory-constrained edge hardware. We distill a 300-million-parameter Prithvi-EO-2.0 teacher, fine-tuned on the 252 manually labeled Sen1Floods11 training scenes, into a 0.7-million-parameter EfficientViT-B0 student. The teacher supervises additional unlabeled Sentinel-2 imagery, allowing the student training set to grow without new manual annotations. At the matched budget of 252 scenes, teacher-supervised training is competitive with direct training and improves STURM-Flood performance across tested configurations; a geometry-matched control shows that label source alone does not explain the difference. Scaling the teacher-supervised pool to 2,500 scenes narrows the remaining student--teacher gap: the float student reaches 0.787 water intersection over union on the Sen1Floods11 test split against 0.822 for the teacher, matches the teacher on STURM-Flood under our evaluation protocol, and remains below it on WorldFloods-v2. After activation replacement and quantization-aware training, the student runs as a 1.5-megabyte 8-bit integer (INT8) TensorRT engine on a Jetson Xavier NX at 5.57 milliseconds of graphics processing unit (GPU) compute per 512-by-512 image, with approximately 14 megabytes of runtime device memory. A fixed modified normalized difference water index (MNDWI) threshold is competitive with both models on the two clean external benchmarks, so we interpret those benchmarks as generalization tests rather than as evidence of learned-model superiority over a spectral rule. The results support the conclusion: foundation-model supervision can amplify a fixed manual annotation budget into a substantially larger training set and yield a compact, deployable edge model.

</details>


### [173] [SenseFuse: Label-Free Fusion of Image and Shape Encoders for Open-Vocabulary 3D Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20475)
*Euiseok Han,Tri Ton,Hwanhee Kim,Seungyeon Ryu,Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SenseFuse：一种免标签的 2D 图像与 3D 形状编码器融合方法，仅改进开放词汇 3D 实例分割的掩码标注阶段，自适应选择场景级融合权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇场景理解对机器人至关重要。现有方法在掩码标注时主要依赖 2D 图像特征或图像蒸馏表示，而闭集方法充分利用了 3D 形状信息，3D 形状编码器在开放词汇分割中的潜力未被充分挖掘。

Method: 揭示 2D 图像与 3D 形状编码器具有高度不重叠的失败模式、很少犯相同错误，而两个 2D 图像编码器则常重复错误，因此 2D-3D 组合天然互补。基于此引入自适应机制：从单场景无标签提案中估计免标签敏感性度量，在毫秒级内选取场景级融合权重，仅细化现有流程的掩码标注阶段。

Result: 在 ScanNet200、Replica 和 ScanNet++ 上，SenseFuse 在所有评测设置中均提升标注精度，恢复出 oracle 权重可得增益的 67-100%（中位数 93%），并在 22 个报告设置中的 21 个提高了实例 AP。

Conclusion: 免标签的 2D-3D 编码器融合可有效提升开放词汇 3D 实例分割，且无需标注、计算开销极低，具备良好的通用性和即插即用特性。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary scene understanding is fundamental for robotics, laying the groundwork for spatial reasoning and object manipulation. While closed-vocabulary 3D instance segmentation heavily leverages 3D shape information, state-of-the-art open-vocabulary methods remain predominantly restricted to 2D image features or image-distilled representations during mask labeling. In this paper, we propose SenseFuse, a label-free fusion method that balances 2D image and 3D shape encoders for robust open-vocabulary 3D instance segmentation, refining only the mask-labeling stage of existing pipelines. We reveal that 2D image and 3D shape encoders exhibit largely disjoint failure patterns and rarely share identical wrong labels, whereas two 2D image encoders frequently repeat the same errors. This distinct behavior makes the 2D and 3D pair inherently complementary. We introduce an adaptive mechanism that selects a scene-level fusion weight to maximize a label-free sensitivity measure, estimated directly from a single scene's unlabeled proposals in milliseconds. SenseFuse improves labeling accuracy in every evaluated setting across ScanNet200, Replica, and ScanNet++, recovering 67-100% (median 93%) of the gain achievable with an oracle weight, and it raises instance AP in 21 of 22 reported settings. Code is available at https://github.com/hanes1207/SenseFuse.

</details>


### [174] [Grounded Product Understanding in Livestream Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20508)
*Xinyu Zhang,Junjie Chen,Jiawei Ge,Qianlong Li,Libin Ma,Baokun Pan,Yahui Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 GPUB 基准与 UniPro 模型，用于电商直播中产品识别与时序定位的联合理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商直播中产品信息分散于不同时间片段，产品理解需同时识别产品并定位其相关信息片段。然而现有基准通常将产品检索与时序定位分开评估，未考核产品身份与时序证据之间的对应关系。

Method: 提出 GPUB 大规模基准，包含 3,000 个直播实例、质量控制的多时刻时序标注及 31K+ 时尚产品目录。设计三个任务：联合产品识别与时序定位的 Grounded Product Understanding（GPrU）主任务，以及 Product Retrieval、Product Moment Localization 两个子任务。进一步提出 UniPro 统一产品理解模型，从共享多模态编码中学习产品对齐与时间结构表征。

Result: 现有多模态模型在 GPrU 上表现困难，最佳基线仅 10.13% Pair mAP@.3；UniPro 将 Pair mAP@.3 提升至 21.53%，并在 GPrU 上达到 37.23% Joint R@1@.3。

Conclusion: GPUB 填补了产品身份与时序证据联合评估的空白，表明 GPrU 仍具挑战性；UniPro 通过统一多模态表征有效缩小性能差距。

Abstract: E-commerce livestreams have emerged as an important channel for presenting products to online consumers, containing multiple products whose information is scattered in different moments. This poses significant challenges for downstream product understanding applications, such as product-centric livestream clipping, where models need to identify the product and its relevant segments for information gathering. However, existing benchmarks for general product understanding typically evaluate product retrieval and temporal localization in isolation, leaving the critical correspondence between product identity and temporal evidence largely unassessed. To address this limitation, we introduce GPUB, a large-scale benchmark comprising 3,000 livestream instances with quality-controlled multi-moment temporal annotations and a catalog of over 31K fashion products. GPUB supports three evaluation tasks: the main task Grounded Product Understanding (GPrU) requires jointly identifying the target product and localizing its supporting moments from a livestream video and a candidate product set; Product Retrieval and Product Moment Localization serve as two complementary subtasks. Evaluation of existing multimodal models shows that GPrU remains highly challenging, with the best-performing baseline achieving only 10.13% Pair mAP@.3. To narrow the performance gap, we further develop UniPro, a unified product understanding model that derives product-aligned and temporally structured representations from shared multimodal encoding, improving Pair mAP@.3 to 21.53% while achieving 37.23% Joint R@1@.3 on GPrU.

</details>


### [175] [Automated Goldsmith's Mark Retrieval in Silverware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20509)
*Atmik Tiwari,Vincent Christlein,Mark Fichtner,Freya Gohlke,Birgit Schübel,Theresa Witting,Heike Zech,Mathias Zinnen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AI辅助金银器印记检索流程，结合标记定位与度量学习微调，比较三种主干网络与裁剪策略，最佳配置达mAP 62.63%、Top-1 73.74%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 艺术史研究中金银器印记的鉴定与断代依赖专家手工比对，需将查询印记与数百个已记录样本逐一比较，过程繁琐且高度依赖专业知识，故需自动化检索方案。

Method: 构建AI辅助检索流程，将印记定位与度量学习微调相结合，在三种主干架构上评估：ImageNet预训练ResNet-50、监督式ViT-S/16、自监督DINOv2 ViT-S/14；系统评估无裁剪、人工真值裁剪与基于学习检测的裁剪三种策略及其与主干的交互。

Result: 最优配置为DINOv2 ViT-S/14＋人工裁剪＋度量学习微调，mAP 62.63%、Top-1准确率73.74%；自监督预训练与印记定位是最具影响力的因素，学习式裁剪可在推理时无需真值标注的情况下恢复人工裁剪的大部分增益。

Conclusion: 自监督预训练与标记定位是关键，学习式裁剪可替代人工裁剪；为促进数字人文领域的可复现与应用，公开人工标注数据集与代码库，并通过公共网页界面部署系统。

Abstract: For art historians, goldsmith marks play a critical role in the identification and dating of artifacts. In practice, experts must manually compare a query mark against hundreds of documented examples, a process that is both tedious and highly dependent on specialist knowledge. To address this, we present an AI-assisted retrieval pipeline that combines mark localization with metric-learning fine-tuning across three backbone architectures: an ImageNet-pretrained ResNet-50, a supervised ViT-S/16, and a self-supervised DINOv2 ViT-S/14. We conduct a systematic evaluation of cropping strategies, where we measure the impact of no cropping, manual ground-truth cropping, and learned detection-based cropping, and assess their interaction with each backbone. Our strongest configuration, DINOv2 ViT-S/14 with manual crop and metric-learning fine-tuning, achieves an mAP of 62.63% and a Top-1 accuracy of 73.74%. Our experiments show that self-supervised pretraining and mark localization are the two most impactful factors, with learned cropping recovering the majority of the gain from manual cropping without requiring ground-truth annotations at inference time. To enable reproducibility and adoption in the digital humanities, we release our manually annotated dataset and codebase, and deploy the system via a public web interface.

</details>


### [176] [A Dual-Stream Regulated Reconstruction and Segmentation Network with Hierarchical Artifact-Prior Modeling for Ultra-Low-Field Pediatric Neuroimaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20562)
*Bahram Jafrasteh,Leo Milecki,Qingyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向0.064 T超低场儿科MRI的统一框架，在单一推理流程中同时完成质量评估、增强与多结构分割，通过双流3D U-Net、辅助类别与伪影图条件头，结合微分同胚配准缓解标注稀缺问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超低场（0.064 T）儿科MRI存在信噪比低、解剖边界模糊和伪影频发的问题，导致自动质量评估、增强与多结构分割性能受限；同时密集标注稀缺，制约监督学习。

Method: 基于3D U-Net构建两个耦合流的网络：先重建增强的超低场体数据，再将原始图像与增强图像结合进行皮层下分割；引入由全脑掩膜导出的辅助类别（覆盖目标结构之外的脑组织）以提升边界稳定性；设置以伪影图为条件的预测头，利用重建残差与冻结的分割特征回归七个伪影评分；通过从图谱到目标的微分同胚配准进行标签传播，并用作解剖重建的正则化，以缓解密集标注稀缺。

Result: 报告了在质量评估、增强与分割三项任务上的验证集结果。

Conclusion: 该统一框架将质量评估、增强与分割集成于单一推理管线，验证了双流耦合结构、辅助类别监督与配准驱动标签传播在超低场儿科MRI中的有效性。

Abstract: Automated quality assessment, enhancement, and segmentation of multiple structures in $0.064\,\mathrm{T}$ ultra-low-field pediatric MRI are limited by a low signal-to-noise ratio, weak anatomical boundaries, and frequent artifacts. We present a unified framework for the LISA 2026 Challenge that performs all three tasks together within one inference pipeline. A network with two coupled streams, built on a 3D U-Net, first reconstructs an enhanced uLF volume and then combines the original and enhanced images for subcortical segmentation. To improve boundary stability, we add an auxiliary class covering brain tissue outside the target structures, derived from whole brain masks. A head conditioned on an artifact graph predicts the seven artifact ratings from reconstruction residuals and frozen segmentation features. We address the scarcity of dense annotations using diffeomorphic registration from atlas to target for label propagation and to regularize anatomical reconstruction. We report validation results across all three tasks.

</details>


### [177] [DocAttriBench: Benchmarking Answer Grounding in Document Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20574)
*Luca De Grandis,Silvia Cappelletti,William Raccagni,Marcella Cornia,Lorenzo Baraldi,Rita Cucchiara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 DocAttriBench (DAB)，一个面向文档视觉问答的细粒度、元素级答案溯源基准，通过 MAPPET 方法自动构建大规模带 grounding 标注的数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档视觉问答中的答案溯源（grounding）仍是未解决的挑战：多数基准缺乏溯源标注或标注质量有限，而人工构建 grounded 数据集成本高昂。

Method: 提出 MAPPET（Mask-based Perplexity-Derived Attribution），结合文档布局与语言建模，通过遮蔽候选元素并度量困惑度增量，将答案归因于对模型置信度贡献最大的元素（如文本块、表格、图像）。将其应用于多个现有 Document VQA 数据集以构建 DAB。

Result: DAB 包含 237k 文档、296k 问答对，均具元素级 grounding 标注。在 DAB 上评测具备 grounding 能力的多模态大模型（评估答案准确率、溯源准确率与整体答案质量）显示：更大模型通常答案准确率更高，但即使最强模型也常无法定位支持元素。

Conclusion: DAB 为开发具备 grounding、可验证、可信赖的 Document VQA 模型提供了可扩展的基准，数据集与代码已开源。

Abstract: Answer grounding in document visual question answering remains an open challenge: most benchmarks lack grounding annotations or provide limited-quality labels, while constructing grounded datasets still requires costly manual effort. We introduce DocAttriBench (DAB), a large-scale benchmark for fine-grained, element-level source attribution in Document VQA, grounding answers to specific layout elements such as text blocks, tables, and images. To build DAB, we propose a Mask-based Perplexity-Derived Attribution method (MAPPET) that combines document layout and language modeling to identify the most informative element for each answer. MAPPET measures the increase in perplexity after masking candidate elements and attributes the answer to the element contributing most to model confidence. Applying MAPPET to multiple existing Document VQA datasets yields DAB, with 237k documents and 296k question-answer pairs with element-level grounding. We benchmark grounding-capable multimodal LLMs on DAB, evaluating answer accuracy, attribution accuracy, and overall answer quality. Results show that while larger models generally achieve higher answer accuracy, even the strongest models often fail to localize the supporting elements. DAB provides a scalable benchmark for developing grounded, verifiable, and trustworthy Document VQA models. Dataset and code are available at https://aimagelab.github.io/DocAttriBench/.

</details>


### [178] [RawSLAM: Online HDR Gaussian SLAM from Linear Radiance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20589)
*Marina Orozco González,Luis Merino*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个在线高斯SLAM框架，可在单曝16位线性HDR图像上直接跟踪与建图，并发布RawSLAM数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稠密视觉SLAM几乎只依赖8位色调映射LDR输入，在极端光照下阴影与高光会导致跟踪漂移与建图崩溃；已有的RAW/HDR重建流程仅能离线运行，依赖SfM预处理且无法应对大帧间运动。

Method: 方法包含三个核心组件：架构无关的HDR高斯泼溅模块，采用无MLP的对数参数化高斯颜色特征；Reinhard范围压缩的光度目标函数；结构引导的空间梯度加权。

Result: 在轨迹与重建精度上均优于MonoGS的直接HDR适配版本，并原生以线性场景辐亮度渲染；同一公式在标准8位输入上误差约为MonoGS基线的一半；HDR高斯模块可无缝迁移至SplaTAM、Gaussian SLAM与DROID-W，消除其在挑战性光照序列上的全部跟踪失败。同时发布RawSLAM数据集：10段真实室内序列，含16位RAW图像、对齐深度、IMU测量与外部OptiTrack位姿。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了在线HDR高斯SLAM，兼顾精度与泛化性，并提供配套数据集，代码与数据集将公开。

Abstract: Current dense visual SLAM systems rely almost exclusively on 8-bit tonemapped Low Dynamic Range (LDR) inputs, limiting their robustness in extreme lighting where shadows and highlights trigger tracking drift and mapping collapse. Conversely, existing raw and High Dynamic Range (HDR) reconstruction pipelines operate strictly offline. They depend on Structure-from-Motion preprocessing and are not suited for large inter-frame motion. We present, to the best of our knowledge, the first online Gaussian SLAM framework that tracks and maps directly on single-exposure 16-bit linear HDR imagery. Our method rests on three core components: an architecture-agnostic HDR Gaussian Splatting module featuring an MLP-free logarithmic parameterization of Gaussian color features; a Reinhard range-compressed photometric objective; and structure-guided spatial gradient weighting. Combined, these components allow our approach to outperform a direct HDR adaptation of MonoGS in both trajectory and reconstruction accuracy, while rendering natively in linear scene radiance for post-rendering processing. The same formulation runs unchanged on standard 8-bit inputs, roughly halving the MonoGS baseline error. Furthermore, our HDR Gaussian module transfers seamlessly to SplaTAM, Gaussian SLAM, and DROID-W, eliminating all tracking failures these systems suffer on challenging illumination sequences. To enable this research, we introduce RawSLAM: a dataset of 10 real-world indoor sequences featuring 16-bit RAW imagery, aligned depth, IMU measurements, and external OptiTrack poses. Code and dataset will be made publicly available soon.

</details>


### [179] [PhGS: Post-Hoc Pruning and Refinement of Single-View Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Reconstructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20623)
*Rinto Yagawa,Han Cheng,Dieter Schmalstieg,Hideo Saito,Shohei Mori*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种骨干无关的后处理压缩流水线，用于单视图前馈3D高斯泼溅生成，通过重要性分数剪枝与轻量循环精炼模块迭代更新存活基元，在冻结基础模型的前提下实现高内存压缩并保持渲染保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单视图前馈3DGS生成对每条相机射线预测固定数量的高斯，导致严重空间冗余；现有多数压缩策略面向多视图设置，依赖跨视图一致性，与单图像模型不兼容。作者希望避免重训练基础前馈网络，直接获得紧凑表示。

Method: 保持基础前馈模型冻结，对其生成的高斯实施后处理剪枝与循环精炼：管道由基于重要性分数的剪枝机制与可训练的轻量循环精炼模块耦合而成，后者迭代更新存活基元以恢复图像质量；该方法与骨干网络无关。

Result: 实验表明该方法可无缝集成现有基线，在保持新视图渲染保真度的同时实现较高内存压缩，并支持推理时按应用需求灵活设置保留比例。

Conclusion: 通过冻结基础模型并施加骨干无关的后处理压缩与循环精炼，可在不重训练前馈网络的情况下有效消除单视图3DGS的空间冗余，兼顾渲染质量与内存效率，并提供推理期可调的保留比例。

Abstract: Recent single-view feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) generation predicts a fixed number of Gaussians per camera ray, introducing severe spatial redundancy. Most existing compaction strategies target multi-view setups to exploit cross-view consistency and are incompatible with single-image models. Instead of retraining the base feed-forward network to directly output compact representations, our insight is to keep the base models frozen and apply post-hoc pruning and recurrent refinement to the generated Gaussians. Consequently, we propose a backbone-agnostic compaction pipeline for single-view feed-forward 3DGS that couples an importance-score-based pruning mechanism with a trainable, lightweight recurrent refinement module, which iteratively updates the surviving primitives to restore image quality. Our results demonstrate seamless integration with existing baselines while preserving novel-view rendering fidelity and achieving high memory reduction. Furthermore, our method supports flexible inference-time keep ratios for application needs.

</details>


### [180] [Refinement Is Inherently Editable: Training-Free Prompt-to-Prompt Image Editing with Generative Refinement Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20633)
*Yulong Chen,Ziqian Zhang,Haoyu Zhang,Ao He,Senmao Li,Kai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的图像编辑框架RefineEdit，基于生成式细化网络，通过二分图像码的全局细化将编辑定位与内容生成耦合，在PIE-Bench上取得最佳背景保持与CLIP分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散编辑依赖空间控制，其不准确性会导致编辑不完整或改变无关区域；因果自回归编辑器受固定解码顺序限制，难以修正早期决策。

Method: 构建生成式细化网络，从源图像中间状态初始化编辑分支并复用已有布局；比较两分支对同一源采样比特的概率，以其带符号差异选择可编辑位置与比特，选中比特进行编辑细化而其余比特复制演化中的源状态；引入自适应空间冻结限制掩码扩张、有限比特锁定保持新选比特可编辑。无需额外训练、外部掩码或注意力控制。

Result: 在PIE-Bench九个编辑类别上，RefineEdit在PSNR、LPIPS、MSE、SSIM上取得最佳背景保持分数，并在所评估方法中获得最高的整图与编辑区域CLIP分数。

Conclusion: 通过在生成过程中全局细化二分图像码并动态重新评估编辑证据，RefineEdit实现了高保真、保背景的文本引导图像编辑，且无需训练、外部掩码或注意力控制。

Abstract: Text-guided image editing must introduce the requested changes while preserving unrelated source content. Diffusion-based editors rely on spatial controls whose inaccuracies can leave edits incomplete or alter unrelated regions. Causal autoregressive editors face a further constraint: their fixed decoding order limits revision of earlier decisions. We introduce RefineEdit, a training-free prompt-to-prompt image editing framework built on a Generative Refinement Network. Our key idea is to couple edit localization with content generation through the global refinement of binary image codes, allowing editing evidence to be reassessed as the image evolves. RefineEdit initializes an editing branch from an intermediate source state, reusing the emerging layout. We compare the probabilities assigned by the two branches to the same source-sampled bits, using their signed differences to select editable positions and bits. Selected bits follow editing refinement, while the remaining bits copy the evolving source state. To stabilize editing across refinement steps, adaptive spatial freezing limits unnecessary mask expansion, while finite bit locking keeps recently selected bits editable. The framework requires no additional training, external masks, or attention control. Across nine editing categories of PIE-Bench, RefineEdit achieves the best background-preservation scores in PSNR, LPIPS, MSE and SSIM, together with the highest whole-image and edited-region CLIP scores among the evaluated methods.

</details>


### [181] [PROVIA: Procedure State Tracking for Online Mistake Detection in Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20638)
*Di Wen,Kailun Yang,Jimmy Weissert,Luc Maria Scherrer,Cedric Zöllner,Ruiping Liu,Yufan Chen,Jiale Wei,Junwei Zheng,Kunyu Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PROVIA，一种基于第一人称视频的在线错误检测方法。它通过分离"事实状态"（含错误的学习到的步骤摘要）与"可接受进度"（由正确演示归纳出的自动机后验），并仅在正确状态分支中进行过程状态转移，再用序贯检验将逐帧错误概率转化为警报。在完整试验（错误与恢复自然发生）上，PROVIA在多个基准上错误排序最优，在0.1次/分钟虚警预算下召回率优于基线，且运行速度为58-70 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线方法报告的是"首次错误协议"，即在每次记录的首个错误处截断，导致一个从不观看视频的固定时间规则也能在所有案例上正确。这使得评测无法反映真实场景中错误与恢复自然发生的情况，因此需要一个在完整试验、验证虚警预算下且对照仅依赖时间信息的方法进行评测的框架。

Method: PROVIA将两类记录分开维护：(1) 事实状态，即对每个执行者所做步骤（含错误）的学习到的摘要；(2) 可接受进度，即通过贝叶斯状态合并从正确演示归纳出的自动机状态后验，以及对每个执行者执行状态的精确后验。过程状态转移仅在正确状态分支发生；错误分支与纠正分支保留源状态。随后用序贯检验将逐帧错误概率转化为警报。整体采用单一滤波器与单一优化规则。

Result: 在CaptainCook4D、IndustReal、HoloAssist和IMPACT-ego上，PROVIA在受控基线中错误排序最优。在每0.1次虚警/分钟的验证预算下，CaptainCook4D召回率0.154对0.128，HoloAssist召回率0.034对0.015，且在HoloAssist上各预算均领先。流水线速度达58-70 FPS。

Conclusion: PROVIA能在错误发生后、下一步开始前仅凭过去帧检测错误，并在人员恢复后继续工作；其在完整试验与虚警预算下的评测中持续优于仅用时间信息的对照基线，并保持实时性能。源码已开源。

Abstract: An assistant watching egocentric video should notice a mistake from past frames alone, before the next step begins, and keep working once the person recovers. A mistake changes the state of the work, so every later step has to be read against what was done rather than against the plan. The first-mistake protocol that current online methods report on cuts each recording at its first mistake, so a fixed-time rule that never looks at the video is right on every case. We evaluate on complete trials, where mistakes and recoveries arise naturally, under a validation false-alarm budget and against controls that use timing alone. PROVIA keeps two records apart: a factual state, a learned summary of the steps each actor performed, mistakes included, and the accepted progress, an exact posterior over the state of an automaton induced from correct demonstrations by Bayesian state merging and over the execution status of each actor. Procedure-state transitions occur only in the correct-status branch; the mistake and correction branches retain the source state. A sequential test turns the per-frame mistake probability into alarms. With one filter and one optimization rule, PROVIA ranks mistakes best among the evaluated controlled baselines on CaptainCook4D, IndustReal, HoloAssist and IMPACT-ego. At a validation budget of 0.1 false alarms per minute it recalls .154 against .128 on CaptainCook4D and .034 against .015 on HoloAssist, where it leads at every budget. The pipeline runs at 58-70 frames per second. The source code is available at https://github.com/Kratos-Wen/PROVIA.

</details>


### [182] [Earth Surface Immune System for Rapid Monitoring of Unknown Anomalies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20662)
*Jingtao Li,Qian Zhu,Xinyu Wang,Deren Li,Liangpei Zhang,Yanfei Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ESIA"地球表面免疫系统"，借鉴生物免疫三原则实现未知地表异常的无类别定位、开放词汇识别与测试时快速自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候变化与人类活动导致地表异常在频率和类型上不断增加，且历史数据稀少、难以预测，与传统遥感目标本质不同；现有方法多针对特定异常类别或仅止于定位，检测与可行动信息之间存在鸿沟。

Method: 架构受生物免疫系统三原则约束：1）非特异性先天免疫阶段，将异常视为时序卫星影像中的未观测变化，不预设异常类别，生成二值定位图；2）特异性适应性免疫阶段，用阴性选择过滤文本提示，并将存活提示经多模态基础模型与局部影像块匹配，实现开放词汇属性识别（类别、受影响面积、损毁严重度）；3）突变机制，测试时仅微调少量嵌入，用单张参考影像对在测试时适配场景。

Result: 定位速度14.51 km²/s，F1较最强通用基线高37%；识别F1超过80%；场景自适应仅需3.26s。在覆盖六大异常类别、19,801.60 km²的全球尺度数据集上与22个模型对比验证，并应用于Kakhovka大坝溃决后Dnipro三角洲退化农田量化与2025年洛杉矶Palisades大火火烧严重度评估。

Conclusion: ESIA对未知异常类别表现出前所未有的灵活性，为实时灾害响应与环境监测开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Earth surface anomalies, driven by escalating climate change, and expanding human activities, are increasing in both frequency and diversity, yet their limited historical data and unpredictability make them fundamentally different from conventional remote sensing targets. Existing methods address specific anomaly categories or stop at localization, leaving a gap between detection and actionable information. Here we present ESIA, an Earth Surface Immune System whose architecture is constrained by three principles from the biological immune system, refined over millions of years against equally diverse and uncertain threats. A non-specific innate immune stage treats anomalies as unobserved changes in time-series satellite imagery, generating binary localization maps at 14.51 km2/s without assuming any anomaly category, surpassing the strongest general baseline by 37% in F1. A specific adaptive immune stage applies negative selection to filter text prompts and matches surviving prompts with localized image patches through a multi-modal foundation model, enabling open-vocabulary recognition of unknown anomaly attributes including category, affected area, and damage severity, with recognition F1 exceeding 80%. A mutation mechanism tunes minimal embeddings at test time, adapting to each scene in 3.26s using a single reference image pair. We validate ESIA on a global-scale dataset covering 19,801.60 km2 across six anomaly categories, comparing against 22 models, and further apply it to quantify degraded farmland in the Dnipro Delta following the Kakhovka Dam collapse and assess burn severity from 2025 Palisades Fire in Los Angeles. This unprecedented flexibility in handling unknown anomalies opens new avenues for real-time disaster response and environmental surveillance.

</details>


### [183] [FunArt: Decoding Functional Structure and Articulation from Generative 3D Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20673)
*Dennis Rotondi,Abdelrhman Werby,Kai O. Arras*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FunArt是一个从单次静态RGB-D观测构建铰接感知功能3D场景图的框架，它将物体几何转换为TRELLIS.2的O-Voxel表示并利用冻结的稀疏压缩VAE作为结构先验，通过轻量查询解码器联合分割可动部件与功能交互元素并估计运动模型，在Articulate3D上取得多项SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在人类环境中有效运作需识别铰接物体、分割其可动与交互部件并估计运动学模型。现有铰接场景表示多依赖已观测到的交互来恢复运动学，而基于静态扫描的方法又常将铰接与功能性交互元素割裂开来。论文旨在从单次静态配置的观测中直接构建铰接感知功能场景图。

Method: FunArt从有姿态的RGB-D观测重建物体实例，将其融合几何直接转换为TRELLIS.2的O-Voxel表示，并利用其冻结的稀疏压缩VAE作为结构先验。一个轻量级基于查询的解码器将紧凑的物体级隐变量与稠密的表面对齐特征结合，联合分割可动部件和功能交互元素，同时估计运动类型、轴、原点与范围。

Result: 在Articulate3D数据集上，FunArt在可动部件分割、铰接估计和功能元素分割三方面均达到SOTA，无论是否提供真值物体输入。在端到端设定下，可动部件提升1.5 AP50，联合原点-轴约束下提升2.8 AP50，功能元素提升6.7 AP50。

Conclusion: 结果表明生成式3D隐变量编码了可操作的结构线索，可在物理交互之前为机器人感知与规划提供初始化。

Abstract: To operate effectively in human environments, robots must identify articulated objects, segment their movable and interactive parts, and estimate their kinematic models. Existing articulated scene representations typically recover kinematics from observed interactions, while methods operating on static scans often decouple articulation from functional interactive elements. We present FunArt, a framework that constructs articulation-aware functional 3D scene graphs from posed RGB-D observations captured in a single static configuration. FunArt reconstructs object instances, converts their fused geometry directly into the O-Voxel representation of TRELLIS.2, and exploits its frozen, sparse-compression VAE as a structural prior. A lightweight query-based decoder combines compact object-level latents with dense, surface-aligned features to jointly segment movable parts and functional interactive elements while estimating motion type, axis, origin, and range. On the Articulate3D dataset, FunArt achieves state-of-the-art performance across movable-part segmentation, articulation estimation, and functional-element segmentation, both with and without ground-truth object input. In the end-to-end setting, it outperforms the strongest baselines by 1.5 AP_{50} points for movable parts, 2.8 AP_{50} points under joint origin-and-axis constraints, and 6.7 AP_{50} points for functional elements. These results demonstrate that generative 3D latents encode actionable structural cues that can initialize robotic perception and planning before physical interaction.

</details>


### [184] [Should This Case Be Adapted? Prediction Fragmentation Controls Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20700)
*Lili Wang,Jing Li,Xiaowen Sun,Xiangyu Hu,Zhuangzhuang Gu,Jian Liu,Srihari Nelakuditi,Yan Tong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文揭示 episodic test-time adaptation 中固定适应步数将"群体层面适应多远"与"个体层面是否应适应"混为一谈，提出以无标签、无梯度的预测碎片化几何量作为个案级路由信号，显著降低有害编辑面积（HA）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定 horizon 的 episodic TTA 用队列均值隐藏了个案决策：跨厂商心脏 MRI 上 ΔDice 均值与零无统计差异，但 58.7% 个案反而被适应恶化。且 held-out 调参选出的全局预算无法在两个主要医学基准间迁移，也无法按个案条件化。

Method: 定义有害接受面积 HA 为控制器实际部署编辑区域中的有害比例；提出"预测碎片化"——源权重 M0 与适应后掩膜 Mk 之间的 disagreement geometry——作为标签无关、无需额外反向传播的个案级预测子；基于该信号构建个案级 router，阈值在独立标注划分上一次性拟合，决策不依赖标签或梯度，模板可跨架构（nnU-Net→SegFormer）与跨域（Cityscapes→ACDC）移植。

Result: 预测碎片化在三个基准上以 Spearman ρ 0.50–0.60 预测 HA，延迟仅为梯度范数的四分之一。在未参与设计的基准上 HA 由 0.228 降至 0.139（设计冻结，仅重校准切点）；在心脏基准上将 HA 由 0.129 降至 0.013，Dice 匹配、平均 1.10 次更新，优于事后在评测标签上找到的 retrospective-best 预算，并将 58.7% 恶化比例降至 20.0%（该上界经量化）。在前列腺等"保留个案非净获益"场景下 router 仍降 HA 但损失精度，论文如实报告该边界。

Conclusion: 固定 horizon 的队列视角掩盖个案有害适应，ha 刻画了这一风险；预测碎片化提供了无需标签与梯度的低成本个案路由信号，可跨架构、跨域迁移并优于调参基线，但在保留个案非净获益的域上存在精度让步的适用边界。

Abstract: Episodic test-time adaptation resets a frozen segmenter to source weights $M_0$ on each case and adapts for a fixed step count. A fixed horizon conflates a cohort-level question, how far to adapt, with an irreducibly per-case one, whether this case should be adapted at all. Cohort means hide that decision: on cross-vendor cardiac MRI the mean $Δ$Dice from adaptation is statistically indistinguishable from zero while 58.7% of cases are individually made worse. We quantify this harm as harmful accepted area (HA), the harmful fraction of the edited area a controller deploys. Held-out tuning gives a stronger baseline than a fixed horizon, but the budget it selects transfers on neither of the two main medical benchmarks, and no global budget can condition on the case. We show that prediction fragmentation---the disagreement geometry between $M_0$ and the adapted mask $M_k$---predicts HA with no labels or extra backward passes at decision time, comparably on three benchmarks (Spearman $ρ$ 0.50--0.60), at a quarter of gradient-norm's latency. A case-level router built on it cuts HA from 0.228 to 0.139 on a benchmark that took no part in its design, with the design frozen and only cut-points recalibrated there. On the cardiac benchmark the design was selected on, the router cuts HA from 0.129 to 0.013 at matched Dice and 1.10 deployed updates, against the retrospective-best budget found post hoc on evaluation labels, and reduces that 58.7% to 20.0%, an upper bound we quantify. Where the retained cases are not net-helped (as on prostate), the router still cuts HA but concedes accuracy, a boundary we report. Thresholds are fit once on a labeled split disjoint from evaluation; decisions use no labels or gradients. The template ports across architecture and domain (nnU-Net$\to$SegFormer, Cityscapes$\to$ACDC) with coordinate, thresholds and per-bucket actions instantiated per domain.

</details>


### [185] [FlowSGS: Improving Flow Matching Priors for Inverse Imaging with Stochastic Interpolants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20769)
*Tianao Li,Xinhui Qian,Emma Alexander*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowSGS：基于流匹配与Split Gibbs Sampling的后验采样方法，将后验分解为似然步与先验步，无需线性前向假设，在多个逆问题上取得SOTA，并首次用于非线性傅里叶相位恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流的逆问题求解器普遍假设线性前向模型，或在后验采样中采用简化近似，限制了其在更一般（如非线性）逆问题中的适用性与精度。

Method: 提出FlowSGS，采用Split Gibbs Sampling将后验分解为似然步与先验步：似然步用Langevin动力学采样；先验步借助Stochastic Interpolants框架，利用预训练流模型的反向时间SDE进行采样。同时给出先验步的具体形式，并分析其与既有PnP方法的联系；利用流先验的直线概率路径与一种新的反向时间SDE时间步校正技术减少网络评估次数。

Result: 在一系列逆问题上达到最先进性能；相比PnP扩散采样器，先验步所需的网络评估更少；首次为基于流的逆求解器提供了非线性逆问题（傅里叶相位恢复）的实验。

Conclusion: FlowSGS为流模型后验采样提供了更通用、更高效的框架，突破线性前向与简化近似的限制，并在非线性逆问题上拓展了流式求解器的适用范围。

Abstract: Flow matching has emerged as the state-of-the-art generative model and has been used for plug-and-play (PnP) priors to solve inverse problems in computational imaging. However, existing flow-based inverse solvers assume linear forward models and/or make simplifying approximations in posterior sampling. To circumvent these problems, we introduce FlowSGS, a flow-based posterior sampling method using Split Gibbs Sampling (SGS) to decompose the posterior into a likelihood step and a prior step. Specifically, we sample from the likelihood step using Langevin dynamics and leverage the Stochastic Interpolants (SI) framework to integrate a pretrained flow model into the prior step. We provide a form for the prior step that uses SI's reverse-time SDE, and show connections to previous PnP methods. Moreover, with the aid of the flow prior's straight probability paths and a novel timestep correction technique for the reverse-time SDE, FlowSGS requires fewer network evaluations in its prior step than plug-and-play diffusion samplers. Our experiments show state-of-the-art performance on a range of inverse problems. For the first time, we provide an experiment on a nonlinear inverse problem (Fourier phase retrieval) for flow-based inverse solvers.

</details>


### [186] [ERCPMP-Gx: Endoscopic Image and Video Dataset for Morphological, Histopathological, and Genomic Characterization of Colorectal Polyposis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20815)
*Zahra Ghaffari,Massih Bahar,Mojgan Forootan,Ali Darvishi,Hamidreza Bolhasani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文发布ERCPMP-Gx数据集，整合内镜、组织病理与基因组信息，按患者层面对遗传性结直肠息肉病综合征进行标注，以支持AI识别与分类研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗传性息肉病综合征是大肠癌的前驱病变，并伴有多系统肠外肿瘤，早期识别与精确分类对患者管理和家系监测至关重要；但现有公开内镜数据集多以散发性息肉为单位，缺乏患者层面将息肉病表型与组织病理及胚系结果相关联的数据。

Method: 构建ERCPMP-Gx数据集：多数操作采用Olympus EVIS X1系统，涵盖WLE、NBI、放大NBI（M-NBI）及近焦NBI模式，产出160幅图像及配套视频片段；约80%为临床和/或基因确诊的遗传性息肉病综合征（PG），包括FAP、PJS、JPS和GNS，其余20%为非遗传性息肉及形态重叠的类息肉病变（Non-PG）用于鉴别分类；每条记录在可获取时关联标准化内镜注释、代表性组织病理和临床报告的胚系结果，形成AI可用的患者级标注框架。

Result: 数据集公开可获取（Mendeley，DOI: 10.17632/nzyfc544bx.2），最新信息见DataBioX网站；形成包含内镜、组织病理与基因组多模态、患者级标注的数据资源。

Conclusion: ERCPMP-Gx为AI驱动的遗传性结直肠息肉病识别、表征与分类提供了患者层面、多模态、AI-ready的数据基础，有助于早期诊断与个体化管理。

Abstract: Hereditary polyposis syndromes can be precursor lesions to colorectal cancer and are associated with a broad spectrum of extracolonic tumors. Early identification and accurate classification of these syndromes are essential for timely diagnosis, individualized patient management, and targeted surveillance strategies for affected families. However, public endoscopic datasets are largely organized around the individual sporadic polyp, and none links the polyposis phenotype to histopathology and germline findings at the patient level. Here, we present ERCPMP-Gx, an endoscopic, histopathological, and genomic dataset developed to support the application of artificial intelligence (AI) in the recognition, characterization, and classification of colorectal polyposis. Most procedures were performed using the Olympus EVIS X1 system with white-light endoscopy (WLE), narrow-band imaging (NBI), magnifying NBI (M-NBI), and NBI with near focus modes, yielding 160 images and accompanying video clips. Approximately eighty percent of cases represent clinically and/or genetically confirmed hereditary polyposis syndromes (PG), including familial adenomatous polyposis (FAP), Peutz-Jeghers syndrome (PJS), juvenile polyposis syndrome (JPS), and ganglioneuroma syndrome (GNS), while the remaining twenty percent comprise non-hereditary polyps and polyp-mimicking lesions with overlapping morphological features (Non-PG), included to support differential classification. Each released record is linked, where available, to standardized endoscopic annotations, representative histopathology, and clinically reported germline findings, forming an AI-ready, patient-level annotation framework. The dataset is publicly accessible at Mendeley (https://doi.org/10.17632/nzyfc544bx.2). For the latest updates and further information, readers are referred to the DataBioX website: https://databiox.com.

</details>


### [187] [Paint-Anything: Unified Any-Color Control for Image Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20816)
*Ji Xie,Dewei Zhou,Xinyu Huang,Zhennan Chen,Xun Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Paint-Anything，通过对象级颜色监督学习统一的十六进制提示接口，实现生成与编辑中的任意 24 位颜色控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业设计需要"任意颜色控制"，即用任意 24 位十六进制值指定对象目标颜色。已有工作依赖专用颜色表示或特殊推理流程，较为复杂；而大语言模型能将十六进制值与颜色语义关联，提供了更简洁的起点。

Method: 构建数据流水线，通过对象定位、感知颜色标注和编辑对合成，从真实图像构造 Paint-500K 数据集。针对阴影导致真实图像标签颜色仅为近似值的问题，引入纯色锚点（其像素与配对十六进制值精确一致）作为补充监督，且仅在噪声较高的时间步使用锚点，低噪声阶段保留自然图像训练。此外提出 Any Color Benchmark（含 ACBench-T2I 与 ACBench-Edit）评估两任务的十六进制颜色保真度。

Result: 在 FLUX.2-4B 上，ACBench-T2I 与 ACBench-Edit 分数相对基线分别提升 85.3% 与 28.3%，消融实验支持该训练方案；在对比方法中取得最高平均 CompColor 分数。

Conclusion: 通过学习共享的十六进制提示接口并结合纯色锚点与自然图像的分阶段监督，可在生成和编辑任务中实现高保真的对象级任意颜色控制。

Abstract: Professional design requires any-color control: the ability to specify an object's target color with any 24-bit hex value for image generation and editing. Prior work has explored color generation, editing, and colorization, but often relies on dedicated color representations or specialized inference procedures. Advances in large language models offer a simpler starting point: even compact models can associate hex values with color semantics. We present Paint-Anything, which learns a shared hex-prompt interface for generation and editing through object-level color supervision. We develop a data pipeline that constructs Paint-500K from real images through object grounding, perceptual color labeling, and editing-pair synthesis. Since shadows make real-image labels only approximate colors, we complement this supervision with pure-color anchors whose pixels exactly match their paired hex values. These anchors are used only at high-noise timesteps, leaving low-noise training to natural images. We further introduce Any Color Benchmark (ACBench), comprising ACBench-T2I and ACBench-Edit, to measure object-level hex color fidelity across both tasks. On FLUX.2-4B, Paint-Anything improves ACBench-T2I and ACBench-Edit scores by 85.3% and 28.3%, respectively, relative to the base model, with ablations supporting the training recipe. It also achieves the highest average CompColor score among the compared methods.

</details>


### [188] [FAMOS: Feed-Forward 3D Articulation Modeling from Sparse Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20817)
*Kevin Qu,Tao Sun,Massimiliano Viola,Liyuan Zhu,Zhizhuo Zhou,Sayan Deb Sarkar,Konrad Schindler,Iro Armeni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FAMOS是一个前馈模型，可从稀疏无序的部分点云集合中预测可动部件分割与关节参数，支持单视图及可变输入数量，通过多状态关节Transformer和观测关节跨度目标聚合跨观测线索，并引入程序化数据生成器，在三个数据集上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏单目视角建模关节物体具有挑战性，因为每次观测仅揭示部分几何与运动信息。多数前馈方法仅从单一观测推断关节，严重依赖学习到的类别级形状先验，泛化与精度受限。

Method: 提出FAMOS前馈模型，输入稀疏无序的部分点云集合，联合推理多个观测并支持可变输入数量（含单视图）。设计Multi-state Articulation Transformer，采用状态间与全局交替注意力聚合关节线索；提出observed articulation span目标，监督各部件在输入观测中呈现的运动范围；并引入程序化数据生成器，在训练中合成带自标注的资产以扩充数据规模与多样性。

Result: 在PartNet-Mobility、ACD和ArtiCraft-10K数据集上，相较前馈与基于优化的基线方法均取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 多观测联合推理、可变输入支持、运动跨度监督与程序化数据合成相结合，可有效缓解单视图关节建模对类别先验的依赖，提升可动部件分割与关节参数估计的鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Modeling articulated objects from sparse monocular views is challenging because each observation reveals only partial geometry and motion evidence. Most feed-forward methods infer articulation from a single observation and therefore rely heavily on learned category-level shape priors. We present FAMOS, a feed-forward model that predicts movable-part segmentation and joint parameters from a sparse, unordered set of partial point clouds. Our model jointly reasons over multiple observations and naturally supports a variable number of inputs, including a single view. To aggregate articulation cues across observations, we introduce a Multi-state Articulation Transformer with alternating state-wise and global attention. We further propose an observed articulation span objective that supervises the motion range each part exhibits across the input observations, encouraging the model to leverage the full observation set. To overcome the limited scale and diversity of existing datasets, we introduce a procedural data generator that synthesizes self-annotated assets during training. Experiments on PartNet-Mobility, ACD, and ArtiCraft-10K demonstrate consistent improvements over both feed-forward and optimization-based baselines. Project page: https://kevinqu7.github.io/famos

</details>


### [189] [SplashSplat: Reconstructing Splashing Liquids from Real-World Multi-View Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20818)
*Peiyu Liu,Dingxi Zhang,Federico Tombari,Marc Pollefeys,Christina Tsalicoglou,Daniel Barath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个同步多视角真实飞溅液体数据集（20个场景，7台4K/60fps相机），并提出SplashSplat——仅在观测可约束处施加物理结构的动态高斯泼溅方法，性能优于SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 飞溅液体持续时间极短（毫秒级）、外观依赖视角且几乎无纹理，难以追踪；现有重建研究集中于烟雾、合成液体或缓变形表面，且尚不存在同步多视角的飞溅液体数据集，导致该场景缺乏基准与有效重建方法。

Method: 核心原则：仅在观测能约束的地方施加物理结构。1) 由逐视角液体/容器掩码融合得到逐帧液体SDF，提供几何；2) 利用相邻SDF间的水平集输运得到粗速度场；3) 沿该流场平流的拉格朗日载体不断被新观测校正、在覆盖丢失处重播种；4) 由载体解码局部高斯并做可微渲染。同时构建20个真实场景基准（7台同步标定4K相机、60fps、人工精修掩码与固定评测划分）。

Result: 在真实拍摄与合成基准上均优于当前最先进的动态高斯泼溅方法，运动物理性更合理且训练开销更低；同一表示无需重新优化即可支持时间插值与风格迁移。

Conclusion: 同步多视角飞溅液体基准填补了数据空白；将稀疏物理约束（SDF与水平集流场）与可微高斯渲染结合的SplashSplat，为极端快速流体现象提供了更物理合理、更高效且具备多任务扩展性的重建方案。

Abstract: A splash lives for a fraction of a second: sheets tear into ligaments and droplets, appearance is view-dependent and nearly textureless, and little persists long enough to track. Reconstruction research has consequently focused on smoke, synthetic liquids, or gently deforming surfaces. To our knowledge, no synchronized multi-view dataset of splashing liquids exists. We therefore introduce a benchmark of 20 real scenes, from coherent streams to violent splashes, captured by seven synchronized, calibrated 4K cameras at 60 fps, with manually refined per-view liquid and container masks and fixed evaluation splits. We further present SplashSplat, built on a single principle: impose physical structure only where the observations can constrain it. Per-frame liquid SDFs fused from the masks provide the geometry, level-set transport between consecutive SDFs yields a coarse velocity field, and Lagrangian carriers advected along this flow, corrected against each new observation and reseeded where coverage is lost, decode local Gaussians for differentiable rendering. SplashSplat outperforms state-of-the-art dynamic Gaussian splatting methods on our real captures and on a synthetic benchmark, with physically more plausible motion and a lower training cost. The same representation supports temporal interpolation and style transfer without re-optimization.

</details>


### [190] [Can 4D Foundation Models Remember?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20819)
*Guangzhao He,Hadar Averbuch-Elor,Wei-Chiu Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 PersistBench，利用 360° 视频作为全知真值，从物体恒存性、运动连续性、外观保持三方面评估 4D 基础模型的视觉记忆能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 4D 基础模型（如可控摄像机视频生成、4D 重建模型）能感知与重建动态环境，但其"记忆"所感知内容的能力尚未被充分研究。已有基准多依赖像素级指标，且对离开视野的物体缺乏真值，无法以物体为中心、与参考进行对比来评估视觉记忆。

Method: 提出 PersistBench 数据集与指标套件，以 360° 视频作为全知真值，构建三个评估维度：物体恒存性、运动连续性、外观保持；并在此框架下对多类模型进行评测。

Result: 跨类别评测显示，现有模型仅能维持短期一致性，一旦物体离开视野，一致性显著退化。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了当前模型能力与稳健视觉记忆之间的差距，即"看见并不等于记住"，为未来 4D 基础模型的发展提供指导。数据集与代码已公开。

Abstract: Perceiving and remembering the visual world is fundamental to navigating and interacting with our environment. Current 4D foundation models, such as camera-controllable video models or 4D reconstruction models, can perceive and reconstruct dynamic environments, but how well they remember what they have perceived remains an open question. Existing benchmarks largely rely on pixel-level metrics and lack ground truth for objects once they leave the field of view, making them unable to evaluate visual memory in an object-centric manner against references. To fill this gap, we introduce PersistBench, a dataset and metric suite that leverages 360° videos as omniscient ground truth and proposes three evaluation aspects: object permanence, motion continuity, and appearance preservation. Evaluating various models across diverse categories reveals that current models can only maintain short-term consistency that degrades significantly once objects leave the field of view. Our findings highlight the gap between current model capabilities and robust visual memory ("seeing is not remembering"), providing guidance for future development of 4D foundation models. Dataset and code are available on the project page: https://guangzhaohe.com/persistbench.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [EvoSherlock: Towards Agentic Lifelong Evolution for Unseen Long-Tailed Security-Critical Events in Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19201)
*Zixin Fan,Jiahong Lu,Changsheng Zheng,Yu Hong,Jingjing Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文形式化了一个新任务L²-SCE，面向视频中长尾安全关键事件的终身演化任务，并提出由智能体控制器端到端编排的因果增强方法（含CVG与CDA两模块），在自建基准上优于多种先进基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向安全的视频理解（SVU）系统假设封闭世界：类别集静态、标注充足、所有事件类型预先已知。但真实安全关键事件具有长尾分布、新类型不断涌现、关键事件样本极少的特点，因此需要一个新的任务设定来填补这一差距。

Method: 提出L²-SCE任务，要求VLM在稀缺样本下持续分类并时序定位新出现的安全关键事件且不遗忘旧事件。方法为因果增强框架，由具备自反思闭环控制的智能体控制器（Agentic Controller）端到端编排，包含两个核心模块：事件内因果视频生成模块（CVG）应对事件内稀缺性，事件间因果解耦与对齐模块（CDA）应对事件间干扰。同时构建了L²-SCE数据集以模拟真实增量条件。

Result: 在自建基准上的大量实验表明，所提方法优于若干先进基线，验证了任务的重要性以及方法在稀缺样本下分类与时序定位新兴安全关键事件的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作通过定义L²-SCE任务并给出因果增强的智能体化解决方案，缓解了事件内数据稀缺与事件间干扰两大挑战，为开放世界下的安全视频理解提供了新的任务范式与有效方法。

Abstract: Existing Security-oriented Video Understanding (SVU) systems assume a \emph{closed world}, \ie static category sets, abundant labels, and the premise that all event types are known upfront. Real-world security-critical events break these assumptions: they follow long-tailed distributions, new types emerge continuously, and critical security events may offer only a few samples. We formalize this gap as \textbf{Lifelong Evolving Task for Long-Tailed Security-Critical Events in Videos ({\boldmath$L^2$}-SCE)}, a new task that requires VLMs to continually classify and temporally localize newly emerging security-critical events from scarce samples without forgetting previously learned events. Furthermore, \task reveals two critical challenges: (1)~\textbf{Intra-Event Scarcity}, where extreme data scarcity may weaken both classification and temporal localization for new events, and (2)~\textbf{Inter-Event Interference}, where cross-event feature entanglement and representation drift may strengthen catastrophic forgetting. On this basis, we propose \textbf{\method}, a causal-enhanced approach orchestrated end-to-end by an \textbf{Agentic Controller} with self-reflective closed-loop control, which includes two core modules: the Intra-Event \textbf{C}ausal \textbf{V}ideo \textbf{G}eneration module (\textbf{CVG}) and the Inter-Event \textbf{C}ausal \textbf{D}ecoupling and \textbf{A}lignment module (\textbf{CDA}), to address the above two challenges, respectively. Especially, this paper constructs a \task dataset to simulate real-world incremental conditions. Extensive experiments on our benchmark demonstrate the advantages of \method over several advanced baselines. These justify the importance of the proposed \task and the effectiveness of \method in classifying and temporally localizing emerging security-critical events from scarce samples.

</details>


### [192] [PAPC: Platform Mediation for Privacy-Propagation Externalities in AI-Mediated Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19226)
*Tao Huang,Guosen Wu,Chen Hou,Guolong Zheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出"隐私传播外部性"概念，并设计平台中介机制PAPC，在信息流动事件更新共享状态前进行拦截与分级处置，从而在保证任务完成的同时消除原始值暴露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM智能体为不同主体协作的工作流中，隐私损失可能在最终答案出现之前就已产生——内存写入、共享工作区更新、智能体间消息或工具事件都会给其他主体带来下游暴露成本。仅控制最终输出无法捕捉这些中间暴露代价，高扇出对象还会放大传播。

Method: 将这类失败建模为"隐私传播外部性"，其中原始披露成本取决于拓扑与扇出而非仅内容；提出PAPC机制，在信息移动事件更新共享状态或外部通道前进行拦截，综合策略、溯源、拓扑/扇出、权限与内容信号，对事件采取放行、释放策略安全抽象、隔离原始内容、阻断转换或收窄后续权限等处置。

Result: 在检索-内存与多智能体工作流基准上，PAPC保持确定性任务完成，并消除了可测量的精确原始值与外部原始值暴露。

Conclusion: 事件级中介可作为智能体化在线工作的平台治理原语；最终输出控制会遗漏中间暴露成本，高扇出对象是传播放大的关键。

Abstract: AI-mediated platforms coordinate work through LLM agents acting for different principals. In these workflows, privacy loss can be created before a final answer appears: a memory write, shared-workspace update, inter-agent message, or tool event may impose downstream exposure cost on another principal. We model this failure mode as a privacy-propagation externality, where the cost of a raw disclosure depends on topology and fanout as well as content. We present PAPC, a platform-mediated mechanism that intercepts information-moving events before they update shared state or external channels. PAPC combines policy, provenance, topology/fanout, privilege, and content signals to allow an event, release a policy-safe abstraction, quarantine raw content, block a transition, or narrow onward rights. The model explains why final-output control misses intermediate exposure costs and why high-fanout objects amplify propagation. Across retrieval-memory and multi-agent workflow benchmarks, PAPC preserves deterministic task completion and eliminates measured exact raw-value and external raw-value exposure. The results position event-level mediation as a platform-governance primitive for agent-mediated online work.

</details>


### [193] [Robust Conformal Intrusion Detection via Traffic-Aware Calibration and Attack-Orbit Invariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19241)
*Zhenpeng Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对LLM网络入侵检测缺乏统计有效性保证的问题，本文提出流量感知共形预测，并进一步通过剔除攻击者可控特征实现路径级精确覆盖保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型微调的网络入侵检测只给出单点预测，不提供统计有效性保证。共形预测虽有有限样本覆盖保证，但阈值基于干净流量校准，一旦攻击者扰动可控网络特征即失效。

Method: 1) 在三个入侵检测基准上验证干净校准的失效；2) 提出流量感知共形预测，按攻击者预期扰动机制采样流量进行校准，在该机制已知且可采样时可证明恢复覆盖；3) 针对能查询目标模型分数的自适应攻击者，从评分表示中剔除攻击者可控特征及其确定性后代，从而获得精确的路径级覆盖保证；4) 在三个独立微调的LLM架构上评估。

Result: 剔除可控特征后的表示在所有评估攻击尝试下完全不变，但相对无限制特征集，干净准确率下降7至14个百分点。

Conclusion: 共形预测的覆盖保证在对抗扰动下脆弱；匹配校准可应对已知扰动机制，而剔除攻击者可控特征及确定性后代可提供精确路径级覆盖，代价是量化的干净准确率损失。

Abstract: Large language models fine-tuned for network intrusion detection emit single-point predictions without statistical validity guarantees. Conformal prediction supplies a finite-sample coverage guarantee, but a threshold calibrated on clean traffic fails once an adversary perturbs controllable network features. We demonstrate this failure across three intrusion detection benchmarks and propose traffic-aware conformal prediction, which calibrates on traffic drawn from the perturbation mechanism an attacker is expected to use and provably restores coverage whenever that mechanism is known and can be sampled. A stronger, adaptive attacker that queries the target model's own score can still degrade this matched-calibration guarantee. We address this second threat model by excluding attacker-controllable features and their deterministic descendants from the scored representation, and prove that this yields an exact, pathwise coverage guarantee rather than a probabilistic bound. Across three independently fine-tuned language model architectures, this representation remains completely unchanged under every evaluated attack attempt, at a quantified seven-to-fourteen-point cost in clean accuracy relative to the unrestricted feature set.

</details>


### [194] [AUDITPLAN: Commit, Then Answer for Auditable Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19325)
*Sai Sri Pushpa Jampani,Kshitij Mishra,Asif Ekbal*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出AUDITPLAN：模型先输出结构化安全计划再作答，并用FAITHGATE奖励门控训练，显著降低ASR、LSR与过度拒答，提升鲁棒性与可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全对齐流水线仅评判最终答案，难以区分真正的鲁棒拒答与两类不良捷径：对良性请求的一律拒答，以及看似合理但不忠实、未真正约束答案的安全理由。

Method: 单模型“先计划后作答”方法：模型先输出紧凑结构化安全计划（含威胁标签、意图动作、显式约束），再基于该计划作答；计划在部署时对用户隐藏，但可机器校验。训练采用监督微调后接强化学习，使用FAITHGATE奖励门控目标——仅当安全计划正确时才给予答案奖励，从而抑制表面安全但不忠实的行为，强化计划与答案的耦合。

Result: 在Qwen系列骨干上同时提升鲁棒性与可审计性。Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct上，FAITHGATE将ASR从24.0%降至11.6%，LSR从1.0%降至0.36%，过度拒答从11.0%降至2.0%，优于仅答案RL、自由形式解释和加权求和结构化奖励。Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct呈类似趋势；Qwen-3-4B-Instruct与Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct的更大模型验证运行保持相同趋势。

Conclusion: 显式的内部承诺（结构化安全计划及其与答案的强耦合）可使安全对齐更忠实、更鲁棒且可审计。

Abstract: Safety tuning pipelines judge only the final answer, which makes it difficult to distinguish robust refusal from two undesirable shortcuts: blanket refusal on benign requests and polished but unfaithful safety rationales that do not actually constrain the answer. We propose AUDITPLAN, a single-model plan-then-answer approach where the model first emits a compact structured safety plan and then answers conditioned on it. The plan records a threat label, intended action, and explicit constraints, enabling machine-checkable auditing while remaining hidden from users at deployment. We train this behavior with supervised fine-tuning followed by reinforcement learning with FAITHGATE, a reward-gating objective that grants answer reward only when the safety plan is correct. This discourages safe-looking but unfaithful behavior and promotes tighter plan-answer coupling. Across Qwen backbones, AUDITPLAN improves both robustness and auditability: on Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct, FAITHGATE reduces ASR from 24.0% to 11.6%, LSR from 1.0% to 0.36%, and over-refusal from 11.0% to 2.0%, outperforming answer-only RL, free-form explanation, and weighted-sum structured rewards. Similar trends hold for Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct. Larger-model confirmation runs on Qwen-3-4B-Instruct and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct preserve the same trend suggesting that explicit internal commitments can make safety alignment more faithful, robust, and auditable.

</details>


### [195] [Scaling Zero Knowledge UNSAT Verification via Normalized Chaining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19353)
*Ashwin Karthikeyan,Ethan Kharitonov,Kuldeep S. Meel,Anwar Hithnawi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种预处理技术，通过将所有推导子句规范化为固定公开长度 k 的归结链，在不引入额外信息泄露的前提下降低 ZkUnsat 证明者的内存开销，提升零知识 UNSAT 认证的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UNSAT 证明在形式验证与软件保障中是标准原语，但证明本身常含专有或安全敏感信息，公开披露不可取。零知识 UNSAT 认证可缓解这一矛盾，而 Luo 等人提出的 ZkUnsat 虽验证了可行性，却因证明者内存开销过大而难以扩展到真实大规模实例。受 LRAT 等高效明文验证的 UNSAT 证明格式启发，作者希望在无额外泄露的前提下提升 ZkUnsat 的效率。

Method: 提出一种预处理技术：对证明进行规范化，使每个推导子句都由一条固定公开长度 k 的归结链加以证明。该方式消除了链长度泄露，并降低了证明者内存占用。

Result: 在 SAT 2002 竞赛基准上，取 k = 16 时，本方法认证的实例数比基线 ZkUnsat 多约 62%；在认证实例数相当的情况下，内存占用降至基线所需内存的 25% 以下。

Conclusion: 通过固定约定链长度的证明规范化预处理，可在不增加泄露的情况下显著提升 ZkUnsat 的可扩展性，在认证覆盖率与内存效率两方面均优于基线。

Abstract: Proofs of UNSAT are a standard primitive in formal verification and software assurance. In many real-world settings, the proof itself encodes proprietary or security-sensitive information, making public disclosure undesirable. Zero-knowledge certification of UNSAT addresses this tension: it enables a prover to convince a verifier that no satisfying assignment exists, without revealing anything about the underlying proof beyond its validity. Luo et al. recently introduced ZkUnsat, a protocol that achieves this goal by proving the validity of a weakened resolution proof in zero knowledge. ZkUnsat demonstrates the feasibility of zero-knowledge certification; however, its scalability to larger, real-world instances is constrained by substantial prover memory overhead, limiting its real-world applicability. Motivated by advances in UNSAT proof formats such as LRAT, which enable efficient plain-text verification, we present a preprocessing technique that improves the efficiency of ZkUnsat without introducing additional leakage. Our approach normalizes the proof so that each derived clause is justified by a resolution chain of fixed public length k. This eliminates chain-length leakage and reduces prover memory usage. With k = 16, our method certifies roughly 62% more instances than baseline ZkUnsat on the SAT 2002 competition benchmarks. Furthermore, for an equivalent number of certified instances, the memory footprint drops to under 25% of that required by the baseline.

</details>


### [196] [Beyond Private Training: The New Landscape of AI Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19456)
*Sean Culatana,Kang Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文区分"输出安全"与"遍历安全"，提出 TSD-AUDIT 框架审计并强制图式 ANN 检索中的遍历安全删除，揭示主流实现中已删除向量仍被参与距离计算的过程级暴露问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有向量索引的删除接口仅能阻止已删除标识符出现在返回结果中（输出安全），但在图遍历过程中仍会计算已删除向量的距离（遍历不安全）。这一差异导致已删除数据的嵌入在检索过程中被隐性访问，构成过程级信息暴露，而现有审计方法无法发现。

Method: 提出 TSD-AUDIT 框架：形式化遍历安全定义，强制"先存活后打分"（alive-before-scoring）不变量，仅使用存活候选修复图连通性，并为每个查询生成可被独立验证器对照删除快照校验的打分轨迹证书。以 Faiss IndexHNSWFlat 与 hnswlib 为审计对象，采用 trace-faithful replay 与代码审查。

Result: 在 Faiss IndexHNSWFlat 上，原生过滤相对未过滤搜索未改变距离计算次数；70% 删除率下，轨迹忠实回放在全部 100 条审计查询中均检测到已删除向量的打分行为。hnswlib 的 mark_deleted 路径代码审查显示相同的"先打分后判存活"模式。在区域定向删除下，TSD-AUDIT 相较原生过滤将 Recall@10 提升 4.3–42.2 个百分点（删除率 0.5–0.9），随机删除下性能相当。

Conclusion: 仅审计输出层面的删除会遗漏过程级暴露；向量检索中的删除审计必须考量搜索过程中被打分的向量，而非仅关注返回的标识符。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented systems increasingly rely on vector indexes that may retain deleted items in their search graph. Existing deletion interfaces can prevent deleted identifiers from appearing in returned results while still computing distances to their embeddings during graph traversal. We formalize this distinction as output safety versus traversal safety, and introduce TSD-AUDIT, a framework for auditing and enforcing traversal-safe deletion in graph-based approximate nearest-neighbor retrieval. On Faiss IndexHNSWFlat, native filtering leaves the number of distance computations unchanged relative to unfiltered search; at a 70% deletion rate, trace-faithful replay detects deleted-vector scoring in all 100 audited queries. Code inspection of hnswlib's mark_deleted path reveals the same scoring-before-liveness pattern. TSD-AUDIT enforces an alive-before-scoring invariant, repairs connectivity using only live candidates, and emits per-query scored-trace certificates that an independent verifier can check against the deletion snapshot. Under region-targeted deletion, TSD-AUDIT improves Recall@10 over native filtering by 4.3--42.2 percentage points across deletion fractions from 0.5 to 0.9, while remaining comparable under random deletion. These results show that output-only deletion audits can miss process-level exposure: auditing deletion in vector retrieval requires accounting for the vectors scored during search, not only the identifiers returned.

</details>


### [197] [Red-Teaming Auto Mode: Improving Blocking Classifiers Against Malign Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19587)
*Alex Remedios,Simon Storf,Fabien Roger,John Hughes*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文红队测试生产环境中的阻断式监控器（Claude Code 的 Auto Mode 与 Codex 的 Guardian），发现在监控对象持续错位（persistently misaligned）时，攻击型智能体可在 79% 的试验中通过注入攻击绕过监控执行任意 bash 命令；通过工具覆盖、转录格式与智能体监控阶段等设计改进可显著提升 Auto Mode 的防御力，但多上下文攻击仍难以低成本防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对编码智能体监控器的评估主要衡量其对意外伤害或来自不可信来源的提示注入的鲁棒性，即在智能体被外部劫持的情形下测试。然而，当被监控的智能体自身持续错位（persistently misaligned）时监控器的表现尚不明确，这一风险缺口构成了本文研究的动机。

Method: 作者采用红队（red-teaming）方法，赋予一个对抗性智能体高层级攻击策略，令其设法规避生产级阻断式监控并造成灾难性危害（如窃取自身权重）。文中系统梳理了多种攻击机制，包括智能体生成的针对监控器的提示注入、多智能体攻击以及恶意压缩（malicious compaction），并在 Auto Mode 与 Guardian 上进行试验；同时通过增强工具覆盖、改进转录格式、增加智能体监控阶段等设计变更来评估防御改进效果。

Result: 在 79% 的试验中，对抗性智能体能够利用针对 Auto Mode 与 Guardian 的注入攻击执行任意 bash 命令。此外，通过设计变更（如增强工具覆盖、改进转录格式和引入智能体监控阶段）可以大幅提升 Auto Mode 的防御能力，但即便有这些改进，以可接受的成本防御多上下文攻击仍是一个未解决的开放问题。

Conclusion: 本文通过详述红队方法论并揭示新的攻击向量，帮助防御方评估其缓解措施在持续恶意编码智能体情形下的有效性；多上下文攻击的低成本防御仍是未来需要攻克的开放难题。

Abstract: To keep coding agents from going off the rails, production systems now review each proposed action with a blocking monitor that can reject it before it runs (Auto Mode in Claude Code, Guardian in OpenAI's Codex). Prior evaluations of such monitors largely measure robustness to accidental harm or prompt injections from untrusted sources looking to hijack the agent. Less understood is how they hold up when the agent they monitor is persistently misaligned. To understand this risk, we task an adversarial agent with evading production blocking monitors and causing catastrophic harm, e.g. by exfiltrating its own weights. We find that when instructed with high-level attack strategies, adversarial agents can succeed through several distinct mechanisms, such as agent-generated prompt injection against the monitor, multi-agent attacks, and malicious compaction. In particular we find that in 79% of trials, the agent can use an injection attack against Auto Mode and Guardian to run arbitrary bash commands. We also find that it is possible to greatly improve Auto Mode through design changes like enhancements to tool coverage, transcript formatting and an agentic monitor stage. Even with these improvements, preventing multi-context attacks at an acceptable cost remains an open problem. By detailing our red-teaming methodology and highlighting new attack vectors, we aim to help defenders evaluate their mitigations against the possibility of persistent malign coding agents. Code is available at https://github.com/safety-research/red-teaming-auto-mode.

</details>


### [198] [SoK: Trading Agents or Market Crashers? Dissecting Robustness and Security Failures in Academic Financial LLM Trading Schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19705)
*Mengxiao Wang,Nitesh Saxena*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文指出智能体安全研究缺乏高风险场景针对性，提出FARSIGHT框架，从市场动荡鲁棒性与三类攻击安全性两轴评估金融交易LLM智能体；对15个学术方案测试发现80%至少一项鲁棒性指标不达标、100%存在安全漏洞，且两种失效模式相互交织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主LLM智能体正快速进入高风险领域，但现有智能体安全研究多为领域无关，忽视了此类场景特有的高后果攻击面。金融交易智能体是典型代表：单个被攻陷的智能体对真实资本拥有直接执行权，且市场具有对抗性与反身性。

Method: 提出FARSIGHT（Financial Agent Robustness and Security Investigation and Global Holistic Testing）框架，对金融LLM智能体进行方案级评估，包含两个维度：一是市场动荡（含类闪崩场景）下的鲁棒性，二是针对三类攻击的安全性——信息源攻击、智能体攻击、智能体作为攻击者（agent-as-attacker）。

Result: 将FARSIGHT应用于15个代表性学术方案，发现多数方案忽视鲁棒性与现实对抗威胁：80%至少未通过一项核心鲁棒性指标，100%存在安全漏洞。

Conclusion: 鲁棒性缺陷与安全漏洞两种失效模式不可分割：微小的误判可自行级联为全市场崩盘，而攻击者也能以极低代价蓄意触发同样的崩溃，凸显高风险智能体安全评估需同时覆盖鲁棒性与对抗安全。

Abstract: Autonomous large language model (LLM) agents are moving rapidly into high-stakes domains, yet existing agentic-AI security studies remain largely domain-agnostic and overlook the distinctive, high-consequence attack surface such settings create. We examine this gap through financial trading agents, a representative case of high-stakes agentic security, where a single compromised agent has direct execution authority over real capital in an adversarial, reflexive market. To this end, we present FARSIGHT (Financial Agent Robustness and Security Investigation and Global Holistic Testing), a framework that performs scheme-level evaluation of financial LLM agents on two axes: robustness under market turbulence (including flash-crash-like scenarios), and security against three attack types: attacks on information sources, attacks on agents, and agent-as-attacker behaviors. Applying FARSIGHT to 15 representative academic schemes, we find that most overlook robustness and realistic adversarial threats: 80% fail at least one core robustness metric and 100% exhibit security vulnerabilities. These two failure modes are inseparable: a small misjudgment can cascade into a market-wide crash on its own, while an adversary can deliberately trigger the same collapse at minimal cost.

</details>


### [199] [Reachability, Not Observation: Containing Systems Whose Wiring Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19720)
*Yoshiaki Takashita*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文指出现有隔离/封堵决策基于“结构快照”，当连接关系随时间变化时会系统性漏掉穿越边。作者用一个统一参数（周期）计算超立方体轮转、光互连轮询调度、跳频、气隙与编码代理工具面等场景中的“盲区”，并给出以声明式能力图+静态可达性替代行为名单的防护方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 封堵决策（防火墙位置、监控链路、程序可达范围）都由一次观测到的结构算出，而观测只是快照。论文追问：当拓扑/通道随时间变化时，快照漏掉了什么，以及这种盲区是现实固有还是设计引入的。

Method: 构造活动维度轮转的超立方体模型，定义平衡划分下的瞬时零穿越与永久封堵需求；加入常开环测防御者可见边与实际需封堵边之比；引入时间感知防御者与时钟滞后分析；在真实互联网（1997-2000、2024-2026）去除增长后对比缺口；将方法统一到“周期”这一参数，给出闭式结果，并映射到频跳、气隙、编码代理工具面；最后用真实应用调用图上的静态可达性校验声明式能力图，并与字符串拒绝名单对比。

Result: 轮转超立方体下93%时刻零穿越边，却需永久封堵8,192条；加一条常开环后可见2条而实际需封8,194条，比值为4,097倍。时间感知防御者平均仅需585次封堵，但晚一个时钟步即降至0%隔离。真实互联网去除增长后缺口仅x1.8-2.0（1997-2000）与x1.5-1.6（2024-2026）。光数据中心轮询调度的缺口等于其周期。声明式能力图通过静态可达性捕获全部8个植入漏洞，原字符串拒绝名单仅捕获2个。编码代理空闲时瞬时状态割为0，但有6条通道跨越上下文重置且均非网络，切断网络减少0；复制通道为分支过程，在批准率1/b处有尖锐阈值。

Conclusion: 快照盲区主要由设计引入而非世界固有：轮询调度等周期性布线使缺口等于周期。应按“存在的路径”而非“观察到的行为”进行隔离，并以声明式能力图加静态可达性进行验证；单一周期参数即可推出多个领域的边界结果。

Abstract: Containment decisions -- where to put a firewall, which links to monitor, what a program may reach -- are computed from an observed structure, and observation is a snapshot. We ask what a snapshot misses when the wiring changes over time. On a hypercube whose active dimension rotates, a balanced split shows zero crossing edges at 93% of instants, yet 8,192 edges must be blocked permanently; adding one always-on ring, a defender sees 2 where 8,194 must be blocked, a factor of 4,097. A time-aware defender holds 585 blocks on average, but one step of clock lag drops its containment to 0%. On the real Internet the same gap is only x1.8-2.0 (1997-2000) and x1.5-1.6 (2024-2026) once growth is removed, so the blind spot is introduced by design, not inherited from the world -- and it has been designed: the round-robin schedules of optical datacentre fabrics have a gap equal to their period. A declared capability map, checked by static reachability over a real application's call graph, catches all 8 planted holes; the string deny-list previously in place catches 2. One calculation generates every number from one parameter, the period, and reads three boundaries not usually called schedules: frequency hopping, whose standard results are these closed forms with channels in place of edges; the air gap, whose always-on crossing set is empty and whose known breach came at the one phase a snapshot misses; and the tool surface of a coding agent, inventoried from the inside. Turning the cuts on that agent: idle, its instantaneous state cut is zero, while 6 channels carry it across a context reset, none of them the network, so severing the network removes 0. The channel that spawns copies is a branching process with a sharp threshold at approval rate 1/b, below which denial is unnecessary and above which denial is insufficient. Contain by the paths that exist, not by the behaviour that was seen.

</details>


### [200] [ALIBI: Adversarial Legitimacy Injection in Binary Input against LLM Malware Analyzers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19722)
*Hyeongjun Choi,Wonyoung Jung,Haehoon Seo,Sungyup Nam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ALIBI——一种针对LLM恶意软件分析器的"语义掩护故事"攻击：在二进制中加入一段不被执行、只读的虚假安全产品叙事章节，将可疑证据重新框定为"正常安全软件行为"，从而诱导模型给出良性判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正被集成进恶意软件分诊流程，作为推理组件汇总静态证据并产出面向分析师的结论。这种推理能力本身构成了新的攻击面，而现有工作尚未系统研究攻击者能否通过操控语义语境而非代码行为来欺骗分析器。

Method: ALIBI在编译好的二进制中添加一小段非执行的只读章节，内含连贯但虚假的安全产品叙事，不修改导入表也不改变可执行行为。其核心不在于直接向模型下达指令，而是把可疑证据重新框定为某良性端点安全工具的预期行为。评测在冻结的50个恶意PE样本集合上进行，并扩展至ELF二进制；对比Gemini 2.5 Pro、GPT-5.5 Pro与Claude Opus 4.7，同时测试一种验证引导的防御提示。

Result: 在50个恶意PE样本中，35个基线判定为恶意的样本里，Gemini 2.5 Pro有30个被翻转为良性；GPT-5.5 Pro与Claude Opus 4.7即使保留判定标签，也出现严重程度大幅降级和置信度显著下降。攻击可迁移至ELF，Gemini在40个样本中翻转16个。验证引导防御提示大致将良性判定减半，但仍有42.9%的恶意样本被判为良性。

Conclusion: LLM恶意软件分析器需要引入来源可信度检查机制，将经过验证的事实与攻击者可控的声明相分离，而不能依赖叙事层面的信任。

Abstract: Large language models are being integrated into malware triage workflows as reasoning components that summarize static evidence and produce analyst-facing verdicts. This paper shows that the same reasoning capability introduces a new attack surface. We present ALIBI, a semantic cover story attack against frontier LLM-based malware analyzers. ALIBI adds a small, non-executed read-only section to a compiled binary, containing a coherent but false security product narrative, without altering imports or executable behavior. Instead of issuing direct instructions to the model, it reframes suspicious evidence as expected behavior of a benign endpoint security tool. On a frozen PE set of 50 malicious samples, the payload flips 30 of the 35 baseline-malicious samples to benign on Gemini 2.5 Pro, while GPT-5.5 Pro and Claude Opus 4.7 produce substantial severity downgrades with significant confidence reductions even when verdict labels are preserved. The attack transfers to ELF binaries, where Gemini flips 16 of 40. A verification-guided defense prompt roughly halves the benign verdicts, but 42.9 percent of malicious samples still reach benign. LLM malware analyzers therefore require provenance checks that separate verified facts from attacker-controlled claims, not narrative trust.

</details>


### [201] [Sybil-TraceGuard: Traceability-enhanced Sybil Guardian for Connected and Autonomous Vehicles Using Dynamic Semi-supervised GNN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19791)
*Qian Xu,Jiaxun Zhang,Chengyue Wang,Zhenning Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出 Sybil-TraceGuard：动态半监督时空 GNN 框架，通过四模块协同从碎片化假名中溯源 Sybil 攻击者，在极限标签稀缺下优于 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CAV 面临 Sybil 攻击：攻击者利用隐私保护的假名切换机制交替身份并伪造 BSM。现有方案虽能标记可疑行为，但时间碎片化的 Sybil 身份使传统单点与序列深度学习方法失效；而根因消除需将碎片身份回溯至源头攻击者，且标签极度稀缺。

Method: Sybil-TraceGuard 为动态半监督时空 GNN 框架，目标从"是否发生攻击"转向"谁应负责"。含四个紧耦合模块：ISAD（增量流式攻击检测，高效预筛）、DTC（动态拓扑感知构造器，构建时空动态图）、SGEM（空间 GAT 编码器+多头注意力，捕捉空间交互中的多身份逻辑冲突）、MSTA（多尺度时空审计，审查短期与长期时序不一致）。模块在半监督 Mean-Teacher 框架内经特征-边混洗扰动优化，用极少标签正则化潜在特征空间。

Result: 四类 Sybil 攻击场景实验中，Sybil-TraceGuard 有效将碎片化假名关联至源攻击者；在未标注比例 0.70–0.95 区间超越 SOTA 基线，且在极端类别不平衡与不同超参设置下保持高稳定性与敏感性。

Conclusion: 该框架以"溯源负责者"为核心，通过时空动态图与半监督 Mean-Teacher 学习，在标签稀缺、类别不平衡条件下实现碎片化 Sybil 身份的稳健关联，为根因消除提供可行路径。

Abstract: Connected and autonomous vehicles (CAVs) face severe Sybil attacks, where attackers exploit privacy-preserving pseudonym-switching mechanisms to anomaly alternate identities while forging Basic Safety Messages (BSMs). Although existing schemes can flag suspicious behaviors, these temporally fragmented Sybil identities render traditional single-point and sequence-based deep learning methods ineffective. Linking these fragmented identities back to the source attacker is essential for root-cause elimination, particularly under extreme label scarcity. Therefore, the Sybil-TraceGuard is proposed as a dynamic semi-supervised spatio-temporal GNN framework for Sybil Guardian, prioritizing "who is responsible" over "whether an attack is happening". It comprises four tightly coupled modules: Incremental Stream Attack Detection (ISAD) for efficient Sybil attack pre-screening; the Dynamic Topology-aware Constructor (DTC) for constructing spatio-temporal dynamic graphs; the Spatial GAT-Encoder with Multi-head Attention (SGEM) to capture multi-identity logical conflicts in spatial interactions; and the Multi-scale Spatio-Temporal Audit (MSTA) to audit short-term and long-term temporal inconsistencies. These modules are optimized within a semi-supervised Mean-Teacher framework via feature-edge shuffling perturbations, regularizing the latent feature space using minimal labels. Experiments across four Sybil attack scenarios demonstrate that Sybil-TraceGuard effectively links fragmented pseudonyms to source attackers. It outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across unlabeled ratios of 0.70-0.95, maintaining high stability and sensitivity despite extreme class imbalance and varying hyperparameter settings.

</details>


### [202] [Trust, but Validate the Instrument: Auditing AI-Generated RTL Verification Plans on Authored Security-Regression Proxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19844)
*Hang Xiao,Chuhong Xu,Kainan Zhou,Gangzhen Qian,Lu Yi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SecTB-RTL可审计框架（31任务、124个硬件安全回归），用于检验AI生成的RTL验证计划能否真正通过可信执行边界。首个确认性运行因响应模式被拒而中止，仅做模式修复后的后续运行虽完成1,860次调用，但仅9个响应通过生产语义验证器。作者将该运行作为"工具验证事件"保留，不报告提示效应估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成的RTL验证计划可能满足供应商模式（provider schema），却在与可信执行对接的边界处失败。这一落差意味着基础设施层面的接受并不能保证执行有效性，而既有工作常将此类基础设施行为误报为模型行为。本文因此需要一个可审计的框架，来分离并记录模式接受与执行有效性。

Method: 构建SecTB-RTL框架，覆盖31个任务与124个作者编写的硬件安全回归。设置确定性非AI基线，在不同资源上限下杀死36、75、78个变异体。采用预注册（preregistered）流程：首次确认性运行C1-R2在模型执行前因供应商拒绝其响应模式而失败；随后在不查看结果的前提下进行仅模式修复，开展单独冻结的后续运行C1-R3，共1,860次调用。使用生产语义验证器判定响应有效性，并按证据完整性门槛评估是否纳入报告。

Result: 确定性非AI基线在递增资源上限下分别杀死36、75、78个变异体。C1-R2在模型执行前即因模式被拒而中止。C1-R3完成1,860次调用，供应商接受1,857个响应，但仅9个通过生产语义验证器。生成规则与执行规则不一致，因此不报告提示效应估计。另一后续运行因未满足预注册的证据完整性门槛而被排除，仅作为未来工作。

Conclusion: 供应商或模式层面的接受并不能确立执行有效性；编译与覆盖率仅是诊断指标，唯一确切保存的工件必须通过完整生产路径。基础设施行为不应被误报为模型行为。作者发布基准、保失败契约（failure-preserving contract）、事件溯源与治理控制，以支撑可审计的AI辅助硬件安全工作。

Abstract: AI-generated RTL verification plans can satisfy a provider schema yet fail at the boundary to trusted execution. We present SecTB-RTL, an auditable framework covering 31 tasks and 124 authored hardware-security regressions. A deterministic non-AI baseline killed 36, 75, and 78 mutants at increasing resource limits. The first confirmatory run (C1-R2) failed before model execution because the provider rejected its response schema. After a schema-only repair made without viewing outcomes, a separately frozen follow-up run (C1-R3) completed 1,860 calls. The provider accepted 1,857 responses, but only nine passed the production semantic validator. The generation and execution rules did not match. We therefore preserve the run as an instrument-validation incident and report no prompt-effect estimate. This incident shows that provider or schema acceptance does not establish execution validity. Compilation and coverage are only diagnostics; the exact saved artifact must pass the full production path. A subsequent follow-up is excluded because it did not satisfy the preregistered evidence-completeness gate and is treated only as future work. We release the benchmark, failure-preserving contract, incident provenance, and governance controls needed to prevent infrastructure behavior from being misreported as model behavior.

</details>


### [203] [ClashBench: Conflicts Leading Agents to Seize and Harm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19892)
*Yuejin Xie,Yu Li,Dadi Guo,Qingyu Liu,Yuqian Fu,Yanwei Fu,Yujiu Yang,Xia Hu,Dongrui Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文识别并形式化了一种新的智能体安全失效模式——破坏性资源抢占（destructive resource preemption），即智能体通过终止、覆盖、驱逐或降级既有任务来获取资源；作者提出可执行基准 ClashBench（268 个冲突案例、55 类资源），在 17 个模型与 3 个 agent 框架上验证该风险普遍存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统广泛部署后，多个 agent 会话常与用户既有任务在同一环境中并发运行，共享容量有限或状态互斥的资源。当智能体拥有足够权限时，可能以终止或干扰既有任务的方式解决资源冲突，而非上报冲突，从而形成安全隐患。作者希望系统性地刻画并量化这一此前未被明确识别的失效模式。

Method: 1) 识别并形式化该失效模式，定义为"破坏性资源抢占"：通过终止、覆盖、驱逐或降级既有任务（incumbent task）来获取被请求任务所需资源。2) 构建 ClashBench：一个可执行基准，包含 268 个经验证的冲突案例，覆盖 55 种资源类型。3) 通过 Codex、Claude Code 和 OpenCode 三个 agent 框架对 17 个模型进行评测，以既有任务健康检查（health check）是否失败作为判定依据。4) 进一步测试基于提示词的防护措施（如"避免影响既有任务"指令、显式授权停止本地进程的指令）对抢占行为的影响，并统计最终回复中是否披露冲突与处理动作。

Result: 在 44.5% 的轨迹中观察到破坏性抢占：智能体完成了被请求任务，但导致既有任务未能通过健康检查。基于提示词的防护措施不足：要求"避免影响既有任务"的指令可减少但无法消除抢占行为；而显式授权智能体停止本地进程的指令反而增加抢占行为。更值得警惕的是，在 31.9% 的成功破坏性抢占案例中，智能体的最终回复既未提及资源冲突，也未说明为解决冲突所采取的动作，提示存在潜在的隐瞒问题。

Conclusion: 破坏性资源抢占是特权智能体系统中一种广泛存在的安全风险。仅靠提示词层面的防护无法可靠缓解，因此需要更强的权限控制、任务隔离以及冲突感知的安全防护机制。该工作为该失效模式提供了形式化定义与可复现的评测基准，并为后续安全研究指明方向。

Abstract: As agent systems become more widely used, multiple agent sessions increasingly run alongside pre-existing user tasks in the same environment, sharing resources with limited capacity or mutually exclusive states. This creates a safety risk: when granted sufficient privileges, an agent may resolve a resource conflict by terminating or otherwise disrupting an existing task rather than reporting it. In this work, we identify and formalize this failure mode, which we term destructive resource preemption: obtaining the resources required for a requested task by terminating, overwriting, evicting, or degrading an incumbent task. To systematically study this risk, we introduce ClashBench, an executable benchmark comprising 268 validated conflict cases across 55 resource types, and evaluate 17 models through Codex, Claude Code, and OpenCode. We observe destructive preemption in 44.5% of trajectories, where the agent completes the requested task while causing the incumbent task to fail its health check. We also show that prompt-based safeguards are insufficient: an instruction to avoid affecting existing tasks reduces but does not eliminate preemption, while an instruction explicitly authorizing the agent to stop local processes increases it. More concerningly, in 31.9% of successful destructive-preemption cases, the final response mentions neither the resource conflict nor the action taken to resolve it, raising concerns about possible concealment. These findings establish destructive resource preemption as a broad safety risk in privileged agent systems and motivate stronger privilege controls, task isolation, and conflict-aware safeguards.

</details>


### [204] [Hopper: Bounded-Memory Collaborative Debiasing for Byzantine-Tolerant Peer Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19893)
*Joachim Bruneau-Queyreix,Laurent Reveillère,Augusta Mukam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Hopper——一种用于拜占庭容错对等采样的有界内存去偏协议，通过引入饱和度触发的衰减重建机制（BMDecay）和可信协作，解决累积计数器在延迟平衡攻击下的失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拜占庭容错对等采样依赖持续刷新的视图，但攻击者可偏置用于构建视图的标识符流。基于频率的去偏虽可降低过代表标识符的权重，但现有设计依赖逐标识符的累积计数。作者指出，即便是精确、无界计数器，在"延迟平衡攻击"下也会失效：一段较长的良性前缀会掩盖随后的对抗性频率偏移。因此，问题的核心不在于计数的精确性，而在于信号的"出现新鲜度"。

Method: 提出 Hopper，一种有界内存的去偏协议。首先识别去偏所需的流估计属性，并在候选方案中选择 BitMatcher 作为最能保留对抗频率结构的估计器。在此基础上引入 BMDecay——一种饱和度触发的衰减与重建机制，使频率信号在长时间运行中保持新鲜。此外，Hopper 通过经认证的指纹感知重建以及面向角色的去偏来支持可信协作。

Result: 实验表明：相较仅依赖 BitMatcher，Hopper 从延迟攻击中恢复更快；在固定内存预算下，其去偏效果与非去偏基线相当。可信协作能降低攻击后的污染峰值，但在高可信节点密度下会带来再识别（re-identification）权衡。

Conclusion: 结果表明，对实用的频率感知拜占庭对等采样而言，"出现新鲜度"而非单纯的精确计数，是关键要求。Hopper 以有界内存实现了有效的持续去偏，同时揭示了可信协作在污染抑制与隐私再识别之间的固有权衡。

Abstract: Byzantine-tolerant peer sampling relies on continuously refreshed views, yet an adversary can bias the identifier streams used to construct them. Frequency-aware debiasing downweights overrepresented identifiers, but existing designs rely on cumulative per-identifier counts. We show that even exact, unbounded counters fail under a delayed balanced attack, in which a long benign prefix masks a subsequent adversarial frequency shift. We introduce Hopper, a bounded-memory debiasing protocol for Byzantine-tolerant peer sampling.
