We identify the stream-estimation properties required for debiasing and select BitMatcher as the estimator that best preserves adversarial frequency structure among the evaluated alternatives. Hopper adds BMDecay, a saturation-triggered decay and reconstruction mechanism that keeps this signal fresh over long executions. Hopper also supports trusted collaboration through authenticated fingerprint-aware reconstruction and role-specific debiasing. Experiments show that Hopper recovers from delayed attacks faster than when relying on BitMatcher, and debiaising as well as non-debiasing baselines under a fixed memory budget. Trusted collaboration reduces post-attack pollution peaks but creates a re-identification trade-off at high trusted-node densities. These results show the importance of occurence freshness, rather than exact counting alone, as a key requirement for practical frequency-aware Byzantine peer sampling.

</details>


### [205] [Delphi Scanner: efficient and interpretable static malware detection via API sequence modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19900)
*Bijied Brahimi,Vincent Cohadon,Gabriel Glazman,Rayan Al Mohaize,Omran Berjawi,Rida Khatoun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 Delphi Scanner，一种面向 Windows PE 文件的静态恶意软件检测系统，利用 CNN 对 Windows API 序列建模进行分类，并辅以基于规则的解耦解释层，将 API 映射为高层恶意能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Windows PE 静态恶意软件检测需要在检测有效性、计算效率和分析可解释性之间取得平衡，而现有方法往往难以同时兼顾这三者。

Method: 采用卷积神经网络（CNN）对 Windows API 调用序列建模以分类 PE 文件；并设计解耦的解释层，基于规则将 API 归类为高层恶意能力。

Result: 在超过 19 万个 Windows PE 文件上评估，准确率达 95.35%，模型体积仅约 1.53 MB；在 5,647 个分布外 MalwareBazaar 样本、成对加壳/未加壳可执行文件及三种对抗操纵策略的鲁棒性实验中，验证了超出训练分布的泛化能力与对功能保持型规避技术的抵抗性。

Conclusion: 基于 API 序列的静态分析为本地部署场景下的恶意软件分诊提供了实用、可解释且高效的基础方案。

Abstract: Static malware detection for Windows Portable Executable files demands a careful balance between detection effectiveness, computational efficiency, and analytical interpretability. This paper introduces Delphi Scanner, a static malware detection system for Windows PE files that balances efficiency with behavioral interpretation. It uses a convolutional neural network (CNN) to model Windows API sequences to classify PE and a decoupled interpretation layer based on a rule-based layer to categorize APIs into high-level malicious capabilities. Evaluated on over 190,000 Windows PE files, the system achieves 95.35% accuracy with a 1.53~MB model footprint. Robustness experiments on 5,647 out-of-distribution MalwareBazaar samples, paired packed and unpacked executables, and three adversarial manipulation strategies confirm generalization beyond the training distribution and resistance to functionality-preserving evasion techniques. Overall, these results demonstrate that API sequence-based static analysis offers a practical, interpretable, and efficient foundation for malware triage in local deployment scenarios.

</details>


### [206] [Mind the Gap: How SBOM Specification Ambiguities Lead to Divergent Software Bills of Materials. An Empirical Tool Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19920)
*Alan Prado,Olivier Zendra,Philippe Boinot,Olivier Barais*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文通过对3000多个JavaScript和Rust项目评估三种主流SBOM生成器，发现生成结果差异主要源于系统性的设计假设不同（依赖范围、命名、来源、表示），而非实现错误，因此需要更清晰的标准化而不能仅靠"修复"解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟《网络弹性法案》(CRA)将于2027年12月起强制要求SBOM，但既有研究虽指出不同SBOM生成器之间存在显著差异，却不清楚差异根源，也无法判断其来自实现错误还是有意设计选择。这一认知空白在SBOM成为法律合规要求的背景下尤为关键。

Method: 以依赖锁文件(lockfiles)为真实基准(groundtruth baseline)，对三种广泛使用的SBOM生成器在3000多个JavaScript与Rust项目上进行评估，从依赖覆盖度和SBOM完整性两方面进行对比分析。

Result: 各工具在依赖覆盖度和SBOM完整性上均存在分歧。多数差异是系统性的而非偶然的，源于对依赖范围、命名、来源(provenance)和表示(representation)的不同假设，其余则反映对SBOM规范所定义字段支持的不一致。

Conclusion: 许多观察到的差异无法简单地被"修复"，需要更清晰的标准化。随着SBOM生成成为法定合规要求，工具选择本身会影响生成的SBOM，可能成为未被察觉的合规风险来源。未来SBOM标准应就依赖范围、来源和表示定义规范化规则，以提升互操作性与合规性。

Abstract: Software Bill of Materials (SBOMs) will become mandatory starting in December 2027 under the European Cyber Resilience Act (CRA) [8]. Although previous studies have highlighted significant differences among SBOM generators, the reasons for these discrepancies remain unknown, as does whether they stem from implementation errors or deliberate design choices.  In this paper, we evaluate three widely used SBOM generators across more than 3,000 JavaScript and Rust projects, using a groundtruth baseline derived from dependency lockfiles.  Our results show that these tools diverge in terms of both dependency coverage and SBOM completeness. Importantly, most of these discrepancies are systematic rather than accidental: they arise from differing assumptions regarding dependency scope, naming, provenance, and representation, while others reflect inconsistent support for fields defined in SBOM specifications.  These findings demonstrate that many of the observed discrepancies cannot simply be ''fixed'': they require clearer standardization. As SBOM generation becomes a legal compliance requirement, the choice of tool itself can influence the resulting SBOM, potentially becoming a source of undetected non-compliance. We argue that future SBOM standards should define canonical rules regarding dependency scope, provenance, and representation to improve interoperability and compliance.

</details>


### [207] [On the Leakage of Massey Secret Sharing Schemes under Linear Computations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19929)
*Nadja Aoutouf,Daniel Augot*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文将基于子域子码的LERS泄露攻击框架从单一秘密扩展到多个线性相关秘密（含计算结果的泄露），给出加法情形到任意线性计算的构造，并证明适用码率范围由k ≤ n/m −1扩展到k ≤ Nn/(Km)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LERS派生的泄露攻击仅针对单一秘密，且基于子域子码的构造受限于k ≤ n/m −1；而实际中多个秘密常通过线性计算相互关联且计算结果也可能泄露，因此需要扩展分析框架以覆盖更广的码参数。

Method: 考虑N个秘密，其中K ≤ N个为线性无关输入、其余N−K个由线性计算确定；先分析加法情形，再推广到任意线性计算，分析LERS派生泄露的存在性，并给出相同泄露函数在特定线性关系下的构造。

Result: 分析适用于F_{q^m}上长度n+1、维数k的线性码，条件为k ≤ Nn/(Km)，支持任意线性计算；相比之前仅适用k ≤ n/m −1的子域子码构造显著扩展了脆弱码参数范围；仿真表明特定线性关系下可出现相同泄露函数。

Conclusion: 利用秘密间线性关系可构造LERS泄露，扩大了易受攻击的码参数范围；相同泄露函数的存在使该攻击模型更现实且仍具潜在威力。

Abstract: Leakage attacks on secret sharing schemes exploit partial information about individual shares to recover the underlying secret. In coding theory, linear exact repair schemes (LERSs) enable the recovery of one codeword symbol from a small amount of information obtained from the remaining symbols, provided that the code has sufficiently low rate. This can be interpreted as recovering the secret from partial information, namely subfield symbols, of the shares. Recently, a randomized construction based on subfield subcodes was proposed for constructing LERS-derived leakage attacks against Massey secret sharing schemes based on general linear codes. We extend this framework to multiple shared secrets whose corresponding shares are related through linear computations, with leakage also allowed on the computation outcomes. More precisely, we consider N secrets, of which K $\le$ N are linearly independent input values and the remaining N -K secrets are determined by linear computations on these inputs. We analyse the existence of LERS-derived leakage that exploits this structure. We first study the case of addition and then generalize our construction to arbitrary linear computations. Our analysis applies to general linear codes of length n+1 and dimension k over F\_{q^m} with k $\le$ N n/(Km), and supports arbitrary linear computations, whereas the previous subfield subcode construction only applies to k $\le$ n/m -1. Consequently, exploiting the linear relations enables LERS based leakage which extend the range of code parameters vulnerable to such attacks. Finally, identical leakage functions can arise for certain linear relations, making this a more realistic yet still potentially powerful attack model. Finally, simulations indicate that identical leakage functions can be used for certain linear relations, yielding a more realistic attack model.

</details>


### [208] [JANUS: Denial-of-Service Attack Against Beam Hopping in LEO Satellite Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19977)
*Yuval Aviv,Roee Idan,Roy Peled,Asaf Shabtai,Yuval Elovici*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出JANUS，一种针对LEO波束跳变系统的定向拒绝服务攻击，通过小型僵尸网络在非受害小区注入合法流量来操纵调度器的需求视图，从而将服务从受害区域转移；在KMAX调度器下可实现约95%受害者的完全服务拒绝，在DRL调度器下可将受害者排除出约92%的调度决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星网络资源有限且需求时空变化，波束跳变依赖观测到的流量需求进行波束分配决策，这一"需求作为输入"的机制引入了尚未被充分关注的新攻击面。

Method: 提出JANUS攻击：利用小型被控终端僵尸网络，向精心挑选的非受害小区注入合法用户流量，污染波束跳变调度器的需求感知，进而改变波束分配决策、把服务从目标受害区转移。并在不同系统配置、调度器、攻击时域与攻击者知识设定下进行评估。

Result: 针对基于排名的KMAX调度器，JANUS对约95%的被评估受害目标实现完全服务拒绝；针对DRL调度器，可将受害者排除在约92%的调度决策之外。同时评估了可降低攻击有效性的缓解策略。

Conclusion: 波束跳变系统基于需求驱动的调度存在可被低资源攻击者利用的安全漏洞，合法流量注入即可造成严重定向服务中断；论文验证了攻击的可行性与持续性，并探讨了相应缓解措施。

Abstract: Low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite networks are increasingly used to provide global connectivity. However, each satellite has limited resources that need to be allocated according to demand, which varies geographically and over time. Beam hopping addresses this challenge by dividing a satellite's service area into geographic cells. Rather than illuminating every cell simultaneously, it dynamically assigns available beams to a selected subset based on demand. This reliance on observed traffic demand as an input to beam-selection decisions creates a new attack surface whose security implications have received little attention. In this paper, we present JANUS, a novel targeted denial-of-service attack against beam-hopping systems in LEO networks. We show that a small botnet of compromised terminals can inject legitimate user traffic into carefully selected non-victim cells to manipulate the beam-hopping scheduler's view of demand. This manipulation alters beam-allocation decisions and redirects service away from the targeted victim area. We evaluate JANUS across different system configurations, schedulers, attack horizons, and attacker-knowledge settings to characterize the attack's effectiveness, required resources, and resulting service disruption over time. Against a rank-based KMAX scheduler, JANUS achieves complete service denial for up to approximately 95% of evaluated victims. Against DRL, JANUS can exclude the victim from approximately 92% of scheduling decisions. Finally, we evaluate mitigation strategies that reduce the attack effectiveness.

</details>


### [209] [XIR: A Framework for Interoperability across Cross-Chain Protocols Based on a Verifiable Intermediate Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20010)
*Yushen Li,Linpeng Jia,Jiaying Feng,Ziliang Liao,Yi Sun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文剖析跨链协议点对点配置导致的可达性瓶颈，提出基于可验证中间表示（XIR）的跨协议互操作框架，将286条活跃区块链的直接可达率从14.64%提升至96.86%，并减少84.88%的点对点配置需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在点对点配置下，跨链通信依赖源链与目标链之间的直接连接，限制了区块链可达性，且连接更多区块链需额外配置。作者希望量化这一问题规模，并寻求在不增加大量点对点配置的前提下提升跨链可达性的方法。

Method: 作者收集2025年1月至10月六个协议（Axelar、CCIP、Hyperlane、LayerZero、Relay、Wormhole）约2500万条主网跨链交易事件，构建覆盖286条活跃区块链、11,935个有序直连区块链对的连接图；提出XIR框架，基于可验证中间表示，将应用消息绑定到有序的、经认证的跨链协议投递记录，以保留消息身份与跨协议边界的验证历史；通过XIR Gateways与XIR Adapters将既有连接组合为同协议及跨协议多跳路径；并实现集成Hyperlane与LayerZero的XIR原型，在本地与公共测试网环境中评估。

Result: 直接可达率仅为14.64%，实现完全直接连通需81,510个点对点配置。在正确配置跨链协议连接的条件下，XIR避免67,018个额外点对点配置，相当于服务相同可达对的点对点基线所需总量的84.88%；同时将可达性从14.64%提升至全部有序区块链对的96.86%。

Conclusion: XIR通过可验证中间表示与多跳路径组合，有效缓解了点对点配置带来的可达性限制，显著降低配置开销并大幅提升跨链可达性，为跨链协议间互操作提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: Cross-chain protocols enable applications to exchange messages across blockchains. Under point-to-point configurations, communication depends on a direct connection between the source and destination blockchains, limiting blockchain reachability and requiring additional configurations to connect more blockchains. To quantify this problem, this paper analyzes approximately 25 million mainnet cross-chain transaction events collected from six protocols (Axelar, CCIP, Hyperlane, LayerZero, Relay, and Wormhole) between January and October 2025. The resulting graph covers 286 active blockchains and 11,935 directly connected ordered blockchain pairs. These connections provide a direct reachability of 14.64%, while full direct connectivity would require 81,510 point-to-point configurations. We present XIR, a framework for interoperability across cross-chain protocols based on a verifiable intermediate representation. This representation binds an application message to an ordered record of authenticated cross-chain protocol deliveries, preserving message identity and verification history across protocol boundaries. XIR Gateways and XIR Adapters use this representation to compose existing connections into same-protocol and cross-protocol multi-hop paths. We implement an XIR prototype integrating Hyperlane and LayerZero and evaluate it in local and public-testnet environments. Theoretical analysis and evaluation show that, with correctly configured cross-chain protocol connections, XIR avoids 67,018 additional point-to-point configurations, equivalent to 84.88% of the total required by a point-to-point configuration baseline serving the same reachable pairs, and increases reachability from 14.64% to 96.86% of all ordered blockchain pairs.

</details>


### [210] [Competition, Collusion, and Corruption: The Spectrum of MEV Attacks on DAG-Based BFT Consensus Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20069)
*Iliya Mirzaei,Heer Patel,Chenyuan Wu,Mohammad Javad Amiri*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文为基于DAG的BFT协议提出MEV攻击空间，围绕对手、协议、目标、部署四个族维度刻画攻击，并在六个生产级协议上实证测量各维度对MEV成功率的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BFT协议虽保证安全性与活性，但无法阻止提议者操纵交易顺序以牟利（MEV）。在DAG-based BFT中每个副本并发提议区块，MEV问题被放大。现有攻击分散在不同协议、不同指标下单独报告，难以判断攻击本质是否相同，使该领域难以系统梳理。

Method: 构建面向DAG-based BFT协议的MEV攻击空间，划分为四个族：对手（adversary）、协议（protocol）、目标（target）、部署（deployment），并为每个族识别影响攻击效果的维度。空间中每点固定各维度一个取值，代表一种潜在MEV攻击，并可实例化到具体协议。随后设计实验，在协议允许处每次隔离单一维度，对六个生产级DAG-based BFT协议实证测量该维度对MEV攻击成功率的影响。

Result: 实验评估表明，所评估的每一个协议都对该攻击空间中至少一部分MEV攻击存在脆弱性；且何种攻击能够成功主要由协议自身设计决定，而非攻击者投入的努力。

Conclusion: 本文通过系统化攻击空间弥合了DAG-based BFT协议MEV攻击研究碎片化的空白，揭示了MEV脆弱性普遍存在，并指出协议设计而非攻击者努力是决定攻击成败的关键因素。

Abstract: Byzantine Fault-Tolerant (BFT) protocols guarantee safety and liveness despite the malicious failure of nodes. However, they do not prevent adversarial manipulation of transaction order, where the order a proposer assigns diverges from the order in which clients submitted their transactions. Exploiting this discretion for profit is known as maximal extractable value (MEV), and it is intensified in DAG-based BFT protocols, where every replica proposes blocks concurrently rather than routing transactions through a single designated proposer each round. The proliferation of MEV attacks on DAG-based BFT protocols has made the resulting landscape difficult to navigate: attacks are reported individually, on different protocols, and under different metrics, making it unclear whether two attacks differ fundamentally or merely in how they are described. This paper closes that gap by presenting an attack space for MEV on DAG-based BFT protocols, organized around four families: the adversary, the protocol, the target, and the deployment. For each family, we identify the dimensions that shape an attack's impact. Each point in the attack space fixes one value per dimension, thereby representing a distinct, potential MEV attack, which can then be instantiated on a specific DAG-based BFT protocol. We perform a set of experiments, each isolating a single dimension where the protocol permits it, to empirically measure its effect on the success rate of MEV attacks against six production DAG-based BFT protocols. Our experimental evaluation reveals that every protocol we evaluate is vulnerable to at least a subset of the MEV attacks in this space, and that which attacks succeed is mostly dictated by the protocol's own design rather than by attacker effort.

</details>


### [211] [A Scalable Trust Discovery Architecture for the Internet of Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20095)
*Song Zhang,Jiankang Yao,Hongtao Li,Xiaojun Zhang,Xugang Shen,Xin Li,Yanbiao Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出面向智能体互联网（Internet of Agents）的可扩展可信发现架构，采用三层分层分布式设计与注册表后缀锚定的复合身份方案，原型实现平均注册时延58ms、发现时延25ms，支持每秒逾1.9万次注册与2.9万次发现请求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体互联网的发展，大量自主智能体需跨异构平台进行发现、验证与协作。然而现有智能体协议主要聚焦工具调用与智能体间通信，在可扩展的智能体注册、可信身份标识以及面向能力的发现等方面仍缺乏有效解决方案。

Method: 提出分层分布式的三层可信发现架构：Agent Root（可信注册治理）、Agent Registry（智能体注册与元数据发布）、Agent Resolver（分布式能力发现与信任感知解析）；引入注册表后缀锚定的复合身份方案，将智能体原生标识与可信注册表后缀绑定以生成全局可发现身份；并设计双证书与多级认证机制以增强智能体间的身份信任。

Result: 实现原型系统并经大规模注册与解析实验评估：平均注册时延58ms，平均发现时延25ms；支持每秒超过19,000次注册请求与超过29,000次智能体发现请求。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了所提架构的可行性，为智能体互联网中可扩展且身份可信的智能体生态提供了一条切实可行的技术路径。

Abstract: The Internet of Agents is expected to enable large numbers of autonomous agents to discover, verify, and collaborate with each other across heterogeneous platforms. However, current agent protocols mainly address tool invocation and inter-agent communication, leaving scalable agent registration, trustworthy identification, and capability-oriented discovery largely unresolved. To address this, this paper proposes a scalable trust discovery architecture for the Internet of Agents. The proposed architecture adopts a hierarchical and distributed design consisting of three layers: Agent Root for trusted registry governance, Agent Registry for agent registration and metadata publication, and Agent Resolver for distributed capability discovery and trust-aware resolution. The architecture further introduces a registry-suffix-anchored composite identity scheme, which binds an agent native identifier to a trusted registry suffix to generate a globally discoverable identity. It also incorporates a dual-certificate and multi-level authentication mechanism to strengthen identity trust among agents. We implement a prototype and evaluate it through large-scale agent registration and resolution experiments. The prototype achieves an average registration latency of 58ms and an average discovery latency of 25ms, and it supports more than 19,000 registration requests per second and more than 29,000 agent discovery requests per second. These results demonstrate the feasibility of the proposed architecture, providing a practical approach toward scalable and identity-trusted agent ecosystems in the Internet of Agents.

</details>


### [212] [ResumeShield: Channel Separation and an Open Benchmark for Indirect Prompt Injection in AI Resume Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20188)
*Jay Barach*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 ResumeShield，一个开源防御与基准，用于抵御 AI 简历筛选中的间接提示注入攻击，通过过滤栈与数据通道隔离双管齐下，在合成语料上实现零攻击成功、精确率 1.000、召回率 0.944。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 简历筛选器读取被评估者自己提交的文档，颠覆了评估者与被评估材料之间通常的信任关系。候选人利用白色文字、零字号、隐藏元素、标记注释、文档元数据或零宽字符等手段在简历中隐藏指令。人类审阅者看不到，但朴素抽取管线会把隐藏文本放入模型提示中并被当作指令执行，这属于 OWASP LLM01:2025 所列的间接提示注入，生产筛查语料中约 1% 的简历存在该问题。因此需要可用的防御方案与评测基准。

Method: ResumeShield 防御由三个阶段式过滤加第四个架构阶段组成：将候选人内容置于显式围栏的数据通道中，并由运营方可信指令声明其惰性。基准构建了带种子的合成语料，覆盖九种隐藏技术与两类载荷族（一类使用已记录措辞，一类模拟绕过过滤器的自适应攻击者改写），且仅在筛选结果发生变化时才判定攻击成功。此外进行了消融实验以分离各组件贡献。

Result: 在 104 份文档的语料上，朴素管线在所有注入案例中均被操纵，而防御管线从未被操纵。检测精确率 1.000、召回率 0.944，在干净简历上无假阳性。消融显示：仅通道隔离即可消除全部测量到的攻击成功；而完整过滤栈但无通道隔离仍留有 16.7% 的攻击有效。同时发现"隐藏困境"：所有逃过检测的载荷都是攻击者让其保持可见的，即放弃了攻击所依赖的不可见性。

Conclusion: ResumeShield 表明，通道隔离是抵御简历间接提示注入的关键机制，过滤栈作为补充可提升检测能力；二者结合可实现零攻击成功且无假阳性。研究还提出"隐藏困境"这一安全权衡：有效载荷若逃逸检测往往必须牺牲隐蔽性，从而削弱攻击动机。该工作以 Apache 2.0 许可发布，仅使用合成数据。

Abstract: An AI resume screener reads a document supplied by the person it is evaluating, inverting the usual trust relationship between an assessor and the material it assesses. Candidates exploit this by concealing instructions inside a resume using white text, zero font size, hidden elements, markup comments, document metadata, or zero width characters. A human reviewer sees nothing, while a naive extraction pipeline places the concealed text into the model prompt, where it is read as an instruction and obeyed. This is indirect prompt injection, listed as LLM01:2025 by OWASP, and recent measurement work reports it in roughly one percent of resumes in a production screening corpus. We present ResumeShield, an open-source defense and benchmark. The defense combines three filtering stages with a fourth architectural stage that places candidate content in an explicitly fenced data channel that the operator's trusted instructions declare inert. The benchmark builds a seeded synthetic corpus spanning nine concealment techniques and two payload families, one using documented phrasings and one modeling an adaptive attacker who paraphrases around the filter, and it scores an attack as successful only when the screening outcome changes. On a corpus of 104 documents, the naive pipeline is manipulated in every injected case while the defended pipeline is never manipulated. Detection reaches a precision of 1.000 and a recall of 0.944 with no false positives on clean resumes. An ablation shows that channel separation alone removes all measured attack success, whereas the complete filtering stack without separation still leaves 16.7 percent of attacks effective. We also identify a concealment dilemma: every payload that evaded detection was one the attacker left visible, surrendering the invisibility that motivates the attack. ResumeShield is released under the Apache 2.0 license with synthetic data only.

</details>


### [213] [Silence Is Endorsement: Verification-Status Laundering in LLM Agent Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20211)
*Yibo Hu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文揭示LLM智能体安全监控中的"验证状态洗白"失效模式：当授权声明脱离其未验证的来源证据时，监控器会大幅放行高风险动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全监控器通常基于摘要或存储的交接信息而非原始证据判断动作合法性，这导致一个危险失效：交接保留了"动作已授权"的声明，却丢失了"该声明从未被验证"这一事实。作者将这种现象命名为验证状态洗白，并旨在量化其对风险动作放行率的影响及在真实智能体流水线中的普遍性。

Method: 在9个开源权重监控器和2个托管模型上，保持动作与授权命题不变，仅移除声明周围的未验证来源框架；并在普通智能体流水线（摘要器、记忆压缩器、完整的提议者—摘要器—记忆—监控器流水线）中测试；另在WildGuard和ATBench数据集上对独立编写的有害与不安全请求进行验证；同时测试显式指令监控器拒绝未验证授权这一修复手段。

Result: 移除未验证来源框架后，风险动作的批准率从5%升至60%（Llama-3.1-8B），从9%升至98%（Qwen2.5-14B），两个托管模型也出现类似大幅偏移；在真实流水线中，摘要器常弱化状态、记忆压缩器常移除状态，完整流水线在三个下游监控器上将风险批准率推高至57%–81%；WildGuard与ATBench上，缺乏支持的授权声明同样显著提高批准概率；显式指令并非可靠的跨模型修复方案，部分模型仍脆弱，另一些则拒绝合法请求。

Conclusion: 智能体系统应将授权来源作为结构化状态，随声明贯穿整个流水线传递，而非依赖摘要或交接中的文本描述，以防止验证状态洗白导致的安全失效。

Abstract: Safety monitors in LLM agent systems often judge actions from summaries or stored handoffs, not from the original evidence. This creates a simple but dangerous failure mode: the handoff preserves the claim that an action is authorized while losing the fact that the claim was never verified. We call this verification-status laundering. Across nine open-weight monitors and two hosted models, the action and authorization proposition remain fixed while we remove the unverified provenance framing around the claim. This change raises approval for risky actions from $5\%$ to $60\%$ on Llama-3.1-8B and from $9\%$ to $98\%$ on Qwen2.5-14B, with similarly large shifts on both hosted models. The failure also emerges in ordinary agent pipelines. Summarizers frequently weaken the status, memory compressors often remove it, and a full proposer--summarizer--memory--monitor pipeline raises risky approval to $57$--$81\%$ across three downstream monitors. Experiments on WildGuard and ATBench show the same pattern on independently authored harmful and unsafe requests: unsupported authorization claims make approval substantially more likely. Explicitly instructing monitors to reject unverified authorization is not a reliable cross-model fix: some models remain vulnerable, while others reject legitimate requests. Agent systems should therefore carry authorization provenance as structured state attached to the claim throughout the pipeline.

</details>


### [214] [DDQN-MLP: An Explainable and Adversarially Robust DRL-Guided Adaptive Learning Framework for Ransomware Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20314)
*Jannatul Ferdous,Rafiqul Islam,Arash Mahboubi,Md Zahidul Islam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出DDQN-MLP框架：训练时用Double Deep Q-Network自适应分配样本权重以指导轻量MLP进行勒索软件行为检测，推理时仅保留MLP。基于Windows 11沙箱遥测、2000个可执行文件样本（5折分层交叉验证），准确率99.30%、F1 0.9930、ROC-AUC 0.9991，优于静态加权、focal loss及多种DRL变体；并通过SHAP/LIME、SHAP-梯度对齐诊断和对抗测试验证了可解释性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代勒索软件变种行为多样、具有规避性且部分与良性行为相似，使固定监督学习目标难以应对，导致检测性能下降。因此需要一种能动态调整学习重点、同时兼顾准确率、可解释性、鲁棒性与计算效率的检测方法。

Method: 采用训练时深度强化学习框架DDQN-MLP：DDQN作为离散自适应样本权重控制器，观测批次级损失与预测置信度动态，为样本分配重要性权重以指导轻量MLP训练；训练完成后丢弃DDQN，仅部署MLP。数据为Windows 11沙箱遥测构建的平衡数据集，含30个家族的1000个勒索软件样本与1000个良性样本，采用5折分层交叉验证。评估涵盖静态加权、focal loss及替代DRL变体的对比，并使用SHAP、LIME及SHAP-梯度对齐诊断评估可解释性，以及多扰动级别的白盒对抗测试。

Result: DDQN-MLP达到99.30%准确率、F1-score 0.9930、ROC-AUC 0.9991，优于传统静态加权、focal-loss及替代DRL变体。可解释性方面，SHAP与LIME及SHAP-梯度对齐诊断显示特征归因与模型敏感性具有一致性。白盒对抗测试表明，对抗训练提升了特征空间鲁棒性，且未降低干净数据上的准确率。

Conclusion: DDQN-MLP为高吞吐勒索软件检测提供了一种准确、可解释、鲁棒且计算高效的解决方案，验证了训练时强化学习自适应样本加权结合轻量部署模型的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Ransomware detection remains challenging because modern variants exhibit diverse, evasive, and partly benign-like behaviors that undermine fixed supervised learning objectives. This study proposes DDQN-MLP, a training-time deep reinforcement learning framework for behavioral ransomware detection using Windows 11 sandbox telemetry. A Double Deep Q-Network (DDQN) acts as a discrete adaptive sample-weighting controller by observing batch-level loss and prediction-confidence dynamics and assigning sample-importance weights to guide a lightweight Multilayer Perceptron (MLP). After training, the DDQN is discarded, leaving only the efficient MLP for deployment. The framework was evaluated using 5-fold stratified cross-validation on a balanced dataset of 2,000 executable profiles comprising 1,000 ransomware samples from 30 families and 1,000 benign samples. DDQN-MLP achieved 99.30% accuracy, an F1-score of 0.9930, and an ROC-AUC of 0.9991, outperforming conventional static weighting, focal-loss, and alternative DRL variants. Explainability was assessed using SHAP and LIME, together with a SHAP-gradient alignment diagnostic for evaluating consistency between feature attribution and model sensitivity. White-box adversarial testing across multiple perturbation levels further showed that adversarial training improved feature-space robustness without reducing clean-data accuracy. The results demonstrate that DDQN-MLP provides an accurate, explainable, robust, and computationally efficient framework for high-throughput ransomware detection.

</details>


### [215] [The More It Says, the More You Pay: A Black-Box Audit of Provider-Side Token Inflation in LLM Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20370)
*Leilei Chen,Lan Zhang,Chen Tang,Pengcheng Sun,Jiewei Lai,Yixiao Huang,Zhaopeng Zhang,Xinpeng Shen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出"供应商侧Token膨胀攻击"(PTIA)：不诚实的LLM服务商可在保持任务效用的同时暗中拉长输出以多收费，并给出五类攻击实例及一种轻量单探针审计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在按Token计费的LLM服务中，模型输出越长用户付费越多，这为供应商提供了通过隐蔽操纵生成来膨胀输出Token的牟利动机，而用户仅凭黑盒响应难以审计此类行为。

Method: 定义PTIA并在查询、提示、表征、模型四个层面实例化五种代表性攻击；观察到PTIA饱和现象（初次攻击大幅拉长输出，进一步强化或组合效果有限），并将其归因于停止行为（初次PTIA显著降低EOS token概率，后续干预仅边际降低）；据此设计轻量单探针审计，施加受控的加长干预，比较原始请求与探测请求的额外Token数。

Result: 每种攻击使平均输出长度增至干净基线的10.2倍以上；在四个开放权重模型上审计平均检测率85.1%，假阳性率低于2%；在15个真实LLM API服务中标记出7个存在PTIA一致行为。

Conclusion: PTIA在生成多个阶段均具财务吸引力与可行性；基于饱和洞察的单探针审计无需可信本地参考模型或历史干净响应，且原始与探测请求分开发出、形似普通流量，难以规避，可有效检测真实服务中的Token膨胀。

Abstract: In pay-per-token LLM services, the more a model says, the more users pay. Dishonest providers can covertly manipulate generation to inflate output tokens while largely preserving task utility. We define such manipulation as a Provider-Side Token Inflation Attack (PTIA) and instantiate five representative attacks at the query, prompt, representation, and model levels of the provider-controlled pipeline. Our experiments show that each attack increases mean output length to more than 10.2x the clean baseline, demonstrating PTIA's financial appeal and feasibility at multiple stages of generation. Yet auditing PTIA from black-box responses is difficult for users. Our key observation is PTIA saturation: an initial attack sharply lengthens output, but further strengthening or composition has much less effect. We trace this saturation to stopping behavior: an initial PTIA sharply lowers the end-of-sequence token probability, whereas further intervention lowers it only marginally. Building on this insight, we design a lightweight single-probe audit that applies a controlled lengthening intervention. Under PTIA, the probe induces far fewer additional tokens than under normal service. The audit requires neither a trusted local reference model nor historical clean responses, and its separately issued original and probed requests resemble ordinary traffic, making evasion difficult. Across four open-weight models, it achieves an average detection rate of 85.1% with false-positive rates below 2%. Across 15 real LLM API services, the audit flags 7 for PTIA-consistent behavior.

</details>


### [216] [Fingerprinting Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20457)
*Chao Huang,Meng Tong,Kejiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次研究多模态模型指纹，提出白盒溯源方法AttnPrint（利用跨模态注意力低频分量）与黑盒审计方法DistillTrace（基于输出假设检验），在19种架构154个模型实例上验证了溯源与蒸馏检测的有效性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM虽支持广泛的图文推理任务，但存在被非法部署与未授权蒸馏的风险。现有模型溯源方案受MLLM共享语言骨干的干扰，且难以检测蒸馏侵权行为，因此需要保护模型所有权。

Method: 受自注意力作为低通滤波器且其低频分量具有信息量的启发，提出白盒溯源方法AttnPrint：提取跨模态注意力分布并分离其低频分量作为模型指纹；进一步提出黑盒审计方法DistillTrace，通过对MLLM输出进行假设检验识别潜在侵权。

Result: 在19种多模态架构、154个模型实例上开展大量实验，AttnPrint取得较强的衍生模型检测性能，并对五种下游修改技术保持鲁棒；DistillTrace在三种参数无关技术下提供了蒸馏关系的证据。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了多模态模型指纹研究的空白，为白盒与黑盒两种场景下的MLLM所有权保护与蒸馏侵权检测提供了有效手段。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) enable a wide range of image-text reasoning tasks, recent incidents indicate that they are vulnerable to illicit deployment and unauthorized distillation. Existing solutions for model provenance are typically confounded by shared language backbones in MLLMs and struggle to detect violations of distillation. To bridge this gap and safeguard model ownership, we present the first study on multimodal model fingerprinting. Inspired by recent findings that self-attention acts as a low-pass filter and that its low-frequency components are informative, we develop AttnPrint for white-box provenance. Specifically, we extract cross-modal attention distributions and isolate their low-frequency components to serve as model fingerprints. To facilitate black-box auditing, we further introduce DistillTrace, which employs hypothesis testing of MLLM outputs to identify potential model infringement. We conduct extensive experiments on 154 model instances across 19 multimodal architectures. Notably, AttnPrint achieves strong derivative-model detection performance while remaining robust to five downstream modification techniques. DistillTrace also provides evidence of distillation relationships under three parameter-independent techniques.

</details>


### [217] [Towards TEE-Certified DP: Verifiable Differentially Private Training on Legacy GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20532)
*Li Ge,Wenjie Qu,Weitao Feng,Yi Zeng,Jiaheng Zhang,Xiaofeng Wang,Wei Dong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于CPU侧TEE配合不可信GPU的可验证差分隐私训练框架，通过概率性检查梯度上的DP执行，以较小开销约束恶意偏离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习广泛应用带来对敏感训练数据保护的监管需求，差分隐私（DP）成为关键机制，但如何在不访问私有训练数据的前提下向外部验证者证明模型确实经过了DP训练，研究较少。现有零知识证明等密码学方法保证强但开销巨大（有时高数个数量级）；TEE更高效，但多GPU TEE支持仅限于较新平台，在旧GPU上缺失或低效。

Method: 用CPU侧TEE与不可信GPU结合的实用可验证DP训练框架。针对效率与安全的矛盾：全在CPU TEE内训练过慢，而无限制GPU卸载会允许恶意偏离DP。因此将昂贵的梯度计算卸载到GPU，同时在CPU TEE内通过概率性检查高效验证梯度上DP的正确执行。

Result: 该框架能以高概率检测频繁的完全偏离DP行为；对本文评估的面向效用性的伪造梯度攻击，稀疏偏离带来的效用收益有限，且未表现出可测量的额外成员泄露。实验表明该方法近乎实现"免费午餐"：相较标准GPU上的DP训练仅带来适度开销，同时有效约束了对所声称DP执行的恶意偏离。

Conclusion: CPU侧TEE+不可信GPU+概率性梯度检查的可验证DP训练方案，在保持近标准DP训练开销的同时，有效约束恶意偏离，规避了对多GPU TEE新平台的依赖。

Abstract: Wide adoption of machine learning has created growing policy and regulatory demand for protecting sensitive training data, with differential privacy (DP) emerging as a key mechanism. Yet a less-studied problem is how to certify the faithful execution of DP during training: an external verifier should be able to check that a released model was trained with proper DP protection, without accessing the private training data. Existing cryptographic approaches, such as zero-knowledge proofs, provide strong guarantees but often incur prohibitive overhead, in some cases by orders of magnitude. Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) offer a more efficient alternative, but the multi-GPU TEE support needed for training and fine-tuning large language models remains limited to recent platforms and is absent or inefficient on legacy GPUs.
  To address this, we propose a practical framework for verifiable DP training using CPU-side TEEs together with untrusted GPUs. Our design addresses a fundamental efficiency-security tension: training entirely inside a CPU TEE is too slow, while unrestricted GPU offloading can allow malicious deviations from DP. We therefore offload expensive gradient computation to GPUs, while using the CPU TEE to efficiently verify the correct enforcement of DP on gradients through probabilistic checking. Our framework detects frequent full deviations from DP with high probability; for the utility-oriented forged-gradient attacks evaluated in this work, sparse deviations provide limited utility benefit and show no measurable additional membership leakage. Experiments further show that our approach nearly achieves a ``free lunch'': it incurs only modest overhead compared with standard GPU-based DP training, while effectively constraining malicious deviations from the claimed DP execution.

</details>


### [218] [Empirical Analysis of Randomness Quality in Differential Privacy Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20561)
*Cesare Gerolimetto Fabrello,Valeria Rossi,Alberto Trombetta,Massimo Caccia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对差分隐私机制中随机数质量的影响进行了系统性实证研究，发现当约1/8至1/16比特被操纵时，DP机制即可检测到偏离，且比特级偏置与时间相关性的检测灵敏度差异显著；但分布异常的统计检测并不必然意味着隐私保证被破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私依赖精心校准的随机噪声来保护个体隐私。尽管理论研究已在弱化随机性假设下分析过DP，但熵退化带来的实际后果仍缺乏理解，因此需要实证探究随机性质量对DP机制的影响。

Method: 基于IBM的DiffPrivLib，构建由标准测试套件刻画、逐步退化的熵源：从高质量量子真随机数发生器（TRNG）和密码学安全的伪随机数发生器（PRNG），到受控熵退化的系统性操纵源。在参考数据库上重复执行百万次查询，并辅以统计检验，直接分析经验隐私损失随机变量分布。

Result: DP机制在约每8至16比特中有1比特被操纵时能够可靠检测到偏离；检测灵敏度在比特级偏置与时间相关性之间差异显著。同时发现，对分布异常的统计检测并不必然对应实际隐私保证的违反。

Conclusion: 随机性质量退化可被DP机制在特定操纵水平下检测到，检测能力受退化类型影响；但统计层面的分布异常检测与真实隐私保证违反之间存在脱节，二者不应等同看待。

Abstract: Differential Privacy (DP) relies on carefully calibrated random noise to protect individual privacy in statistical analyses. While theoretical work has analyzed DP under weakened randomness assumptions, the practical consequences of entropy degradation remain poorly understood. We present a systematic empirical investigation of how randomness quality affects differential privacy mechanisms using IBM's DiffPrivLib. We introduce progressively degraded entropy sources characterized by established test suites, starting from high-quality quantum True Random Number Generators (TRNGs) and cryptographically secure Pseudo-Random Number Generators (PRNGs) down to systematically manipulated sources with controlled entropy degradation. Through repeated experiments over one million queries on a reference database and complementary statistical tests, we directly analyze empirical Privacy Loss Random Variable distributions. Our results demonstrate that DP mechanisms reliably detect deviations when approximately 1 bit in every 8 to 16 is manipulated, with detection sensitivity varying significantly between bit-level biases and temporal correlations. We demonstrate that statistical detection of distributional anomalies does not necessarily correspond to actual privacy guarantee violations.

</details>


### [219] [Weather Data Spoofing Attacks on Rain-Adaptive Millimeter-Wave Frequency Selection in V2X Communication Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20601)
*Rasheed Bello,Idreez Yusuf,Justice Adjei Owusu,Oluwatobiloba Aiyewunmi,Gurcan Comert,Judith Mwakalonge,Esmail Abuhdima,Abdulmajid Mrebit,Rajab Ataai,Vaidyan Varghese*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示天气感知的毫米波车联网频段选择存在攻击面：仅伪造降雨输入即可操控受害车辆的载波频率与通信范围，在 MilliCar 仿真中量化了该攻击的后果，并实现相应的物理交叉校验防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联网车辆依赖毫米波侧链路满足协同驾驶的数据速率需求，而新兴设计依据感知到的降雨量选择载波频段。作者意在指出这种"天气感知"本身构成攻击面：攻击者无需占用信道，只要伪造降雨输入即可间接操控受害者的载波频率及其通信范围，而此前缺乏对该威胁的建模与评估。

Method: 作者在 MilliCar（ns-3 模块）上评估攻击，将选定频段作为真实 3GPP NR V2X 载波运行，包含逐频段传播、波束成形与阻塞建模。攻击方式为仅伪造降雨输入以强制频段上/下偏移，观测八车队列的可靠通信范围、长距接收率、传输块大小与长距时延，并测试降雨过程（advecting rain cell）的影响。防御依据降雨损耗随距离线性增长、路径损耗随距离对数增长的分歧：接收端将实测 SINR 与所报告天气预测的衰减进行比对以检出force-up，并触发重选。

Result: 强制频段升至 73 GHz 使八车队列的可靠范围维持在 38 m，而诚实基线可翻倍至 82 m；强制降至 5 GHz 仍能维持 97% 长距接收，但传输块坍缩至三分之一，长距时延翻两番达 12.5 ms。防御可在 1% 误报率下于 1.5 s 内以 98% 概率标记 force-up，重选后长距接收率由 60% 恢复至 75%。同一检验对 force-down 结构性失明，因 5 GHz 回退频段几乎不受降雨影响。降雨单体平流使本地速率在 15 至 81 mm/h 间摆动时，所有结果不变。

Conclusion: 天气感知的频段选择必须依赖经过认证的气象输入；物理交叉校验只能覆盖威胁的一半（force-up），对 force-down 无效。

Abstract: Connected vehicles use millimeter-wave (mmWave) sidelinks for the data rates cooperative driving demands, and emerging designs select the carrier band from sensed rainfall. We show that this weather awareness is an attack surface: an adversary who spoofs only the rainfall input dictates the victim's carrier frequency, and through it its communication range, without transmitting on the channel. We evaluate the attack in MilliCar, an ns-3 module that runs the selected band as the real 3GPP NR V2X carrier with per-band propagation, beamforming, and blockage. Forcing the band up to 73 GHz holds an eight-vehicle platoon's reliable range at 38 m while the honest baseline doubles it to 82 m; forcing it down to 5 GHz sustains 97% long-range reception but collapses the transport block to a third and quadruples long-range latency to 12.5 ms. We then implement the defense the mechanism implies. Rain loss grows linearly with distance while path loss grows logarithmically, so a receiver that tests measured SINR against the attenuation its reported weather predicts flags force-up with 98% probability within 1.5 s at a 1% false-alarm rate, and re-selection then restores long-range reception from 60% to 75%. The same test is structurally blind to force-down, because the 5 GHz fallback is nearly rain-immune. An advecting rain cell that swings the local rate from 15 to 81 mm/h leaves every result unchanged. Weather-aware band selection therefore requires an authenticated meteorological input; physical cross-checking covers one half of the threat.

</details>


### [220] [Inference-Engine Fingerprinting Attacks are Practical: Exploring Model-Driven Environmental Discovery, Exploitation, and Escape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20614)
*Sarah Radway,Andrew Cheng,Vijay Janapa Reddi,James Mickens*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文揭示前沿AI模型可通过生成特定输出token对推理引擎（如vLLM、SGLang）进行指纹识别，进而利用引擎特有漏洞实现仅凭输出token的"至裸机"攻击链，并提出加固建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型已具备利用复杂软件漏洞的能力，现实中已出现OpenAI与Anthropic模型的沙箱逃逸事件。现有关于推理栈沙箱化的讨论多聚焦于推理引擎之外的组件（网络代理、代码执行环境等），而忽略了推理引擎本身这一极具吸引力的攻击目标——若模型仅靠生成特制输出token即可触发引擎漏洞，则无需依赖其他组件漏洞或外部恶意输入即可发起多步、至裸机的攻击链，这一威胁被严重低估。

Method: 1）论证模型可通过输出token进行推理引擎指纹识别，从而确定执行自身的具体引擎（如vLLM、SGLang）；2）在五种主流引擎中给出具体的模型指纹实例；3）展示真实智能体框架（agentic harnesses）如何让模型利用这些指纹定位本地引擎；4）提出并描述一个从被指纹识别（随后被攻陷）的推理引擎出发的概念验证型、至裸机漏洞利用链；5）讨论若干可使指纹识别攻击更难实施的引擎改造方案。

Result: 在五种流行推理引擎中成功识别出可用于指纹识别的输出特征；证明真实智能体框架可支持模型完成本地引擎定位；给出从被指纹引擎出发直达裸机的概念验证漏洞利用链；并提出降低指纹识别可行性的改进入手点。

Conclusion: 推理引擎本身是错位模型的现实攻击面，仅凭输出token即可完成指纹识别并触发引擎特有漏洞，突破沙箱乃至直达裸机。因此沙箱化设计不能仅关注推理栈外围组件，还需通过修改推理引擎（如削弱可指纹化行为差异）来提升攻击难度。

Abstract: Frontier AI models are rapidly gaining the ability to exploit vulnerabilities in complex pieces of software. The risk is not theoretical, as evidenced by recent sandbox escapes performed by frontier models at OpenAI and Anthropic. Discussions of how to sandbox inference stack components often focus on components other than the inference engine itself (e.g., network proxies or code execution environments). However, the inference engine is an attractive target for a misaligned model. For example, if a model can trigger exploits in that engine merely by generating specially-crafted output tokens, the model can initiate a multi-step, to-the-bare-metal exploit chain in the engine, without relying on vulnerabilities in other components of the inference stack, and without assistance from externally-provided, maliciously-crafted input tokens.
  In this paper, we show that a misaligned model can perform inference engine fingerprinting to determine the specific engine (e.g., vLLM, SGLang) which executes the model. Once the engine has been fingerprinted, the model can leverage engine-specific exploits to take control of the engine using only carefully-selected output tokens. We provide concrete examples of model fingerprints in five popular engines, and demonstrate how realistic agentic harnesses allow a model to leverage those fingerprints to identify the local engine. We also describe a proof-of-concept, to-the-bare-metal exploit chain that originates from a fingerprinted (and subsequently compromised) inference engine. We conclude by discussing several ways that inference engines could be changed to make fingerprinting attacks more difficult.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [221] [Next-token functional estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19529)
*Milind Nakul,Vidya Muthukumar,Ashwin Pananjady*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出留一窗口（leave-a-window-out）估计量，估计依赖时间序列下"下一token泛函"，在平稳β-混合且具Marton耦合的過程上达到参数速率，并给出极小极大下界与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的留一法（leave-one-out）在时间相依（temporal dependence）下不一致，无法可靠估计下一token的各类泛函（如新颖性概率/惊奇概率、最小距离尾部概率、分类器测试误差）。因此需要一种在相依数据下仍具一致性且高精度的方法。

Method: 定义留一窗口估计量：在每个索引后删除长度为τ的窗口再构造经验测度，τ=1时退化为留一法。在平稳β-混合且满足Marton耦合的自然假设下分析其收敛性；并针对混合马尔可夫链上的惊奇概率估计推导极小极大下界。

Result: 理论上证明估计误差以参数速率衰减，覆盖一大类随机过程与多种泛函；给出惊奇概率在混合马尔可夫链上的尖锐极小极大下界（说明速率最优性）。实验在马尔可夫链、滑动平均（MA）过程、自回归（AR）过程上显示，该方法在留一法与加常数（add-constant）基线失效的多种场景中均成功。

Conclusion: 留一窗口机制通过显式截断相依性，解决了留一法在时间相依下的不一致问题，在参数速率与极小极大最优性层面均具理论保证，并在多种时序模型上优于现有基线。

Abstract: Suppose we observe the first $n$ points of a sequence of random variables having length $n+1$, and wish to estimate a functional of the unobserved final point and the empirical measure of the $n$ observed training points. Such next-token functionals include the probability that the next token is novel (also known as the surprise probability), the tail probability of the minimum distance between the next token and training points, and the test error of a classifier trained on the observed points. All of these quantities are classically estimated by the leave-one-out method, which is inconsistent under temporal dependence. We propose a leave-a-window-out estimator, which deletes a window of length $τ$ after each index before forming the empirical measure and reduces to leave-one-out at $τ= 1$. Under natural assumptions, we show that the error of our estimator decays at a parametric rate for any stationary $β$-mixing process that also admits a Marton coupling. Our results thus cover several natural functionals on a large class of stochastic processes. We complement these upper bounds with a sharp minimax lower bound for estimating the surprise probability on mixing Markov chains. Simulations on Markov chains, moving-average processes, and autoregressive processes show that our estimator succeeds in many scenarios where leave-one-out and add-constant baselines fail.

</details>


### [222] [Federated Soft Clustering via Generalized Total Variation Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19202)
*Shamsiiat Abdurakhmanova,Alexander Jung*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究联邦网络中的软聚类问题，比较三种图正则化差异度量（参数欧氏距离、MC近似KL散度、闭式MMD）构建的GTVMin实例，并用同步投影梯度法优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中各设备持有私有数据并拟合个性化高斯混合模型，GTVMin通过图正则项耦合各节点的最大似然问题，而差异度量的选取是关键设计决策，需要系统比较不同度量在无需组件匹配、计算成本与数据异质性鲁棒性方面的表现。

Method: 以GTVMin为框架，比较三种差异度量：模型参数间的平方欧氏距离（需组件匹配）、蒙特卡洛近似的KL散度（无需匹配）、闭式MMD（无需匹配）；三者均通过同步投影梯度更新求解，并对光滑的MMD实例给出收敛到稳定点的保证，同时刻画其计算成本。

Result: 对MMD对应的光滑GTVMin实例证明了收敛到稳定点的理论保证；刻画了三种实例的计算成本，并评估了它们对数据异质性的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 直接比较局部模型分布的差异度量（MC-KL与MMD）可避免组件匹配问题，其中MMD实例兼具闭式形式与收敛保证，是联邦软聚类中兼顾可解性与鲁棒性的可行选择。

Abstract: We study federated soft clustering over federated learning (FL) networks of devices that each hold a private local dataset and fit a personalized Gaussian mixture model (GMM). Generalized total variation minimization (GTVMin) couples the local maximum likelihood problems through a graph regularizer that penalizes a discrepancy between the models of connected nodes. The choice of discrepancy measure is a key design decision: we compare a squared Euclidean distance between model parameters, which requires component matching, with two measures that compare the local model distributions directly and hence need no matching: a Monte-Carlo approximated Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence and a closed-form maximum mean discrepancy (MMD). All three resulting GTVMin instances are optimized by synchronous projected gradient updates; for the smooth MMD instance we provide a convergence guarantee to stationary points. We characterize their computational cost and evaluate their robustness to data heterogeneity.

</details>


### [223] [Learning Submanifolds for Subsequent Inference on Random Dot Product Graphs, Part 1: Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19357)
*Michael W. Trosset,Carey E. Priebe*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该文提出一个针对随机点积图的受限推断框架，其潜在位置位于未知低维支撑流形上，并利用Isomap半监督决策规则学习该流形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机点积图推断通常假设潜在位置位于已知或全维空间。当潜在位置实际分布于未知低维流形时，需要利用辅助数据学习该支撑流形，以实现受限且更有效的推断，现有方法缺乏对此情形的系统处理。

Method: 针对一般决策问题，提出半监督决策规则：使用辅助数据与Isomap流形学习构造观测图的低维欧氏表示，并在此空间中用等距不变函数将点构型映射为动作；分析规则随辅助样本量增大的行为。

Result: 当辅助样本量增大时，半监督规则的风险收敛于一个oracle规则的风险，该oracle规则依赖于可从支撑流形中提取的最大低维欧氏结构。举例、应用与仿真研究见续篇。

Conclusion: 该框架为潜在位置位于未知低维支撑流形上的随机点积图提供了基于流形学习的受限半监督推断方法，并证明了其渐近风险最优性。

Abstract: We propose a framework for restricted inference on random dot product graphs whose latent positions lie on an unknown low-dimensional support manifold. For general decision problems, we propose semisupervised decision rules that use auxiliary data to learn the support manifold. Specifically, our rules use the Isomap manifold learning procedure to construct a low-dimensional Euclidean representation of the observed graph, in which space an isometrically invariant function maps configurations of points to actions. We study the behavior of the proposed rules as the quantity of auxiliary data sampled from the unknown support manifold increases. We show that, as the auxiliary sample size increases, the risk of the semisupervised rule converges to the risk of an oracle rule that relies on the maximal amount of low-dimensional Euclidean structure that can be extracted from the support manifold. Examples, applications, and simulation studies are deferred to a sequel.

</details>


### [224] [Null importance: Disentangling relevance for interpretable machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19511)
*Garvesh Raskutti,Kris Sankaran,Jiaxin Ye*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出基于"零重要性(null importance)"的统一视角,梳理并连接特征重要性的不同统计含义,阐明其适用条件、方法对应关系及实际后果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征重要性是可解释机器学习的核心,但"重要性"一词涵盖多种本质上不同的相关性概念,这种语义混用容易导致分析结论被误读或夸大,因此需要一个统一框架来厘清不同重要性定义之间的关系。

Method: 作者以总体层面的"零重要性"作为刻画特征在特定相关性概念下是否无关的统一工具,考察源自边际统计相关、条件统计相关、预测风险、函数不变性以及因果效应等多种标准零重要性概念;给出不同零概念一致的充分条件及条件失效时的反例;刻画不同方法族所针对的零概念及其零重要性统计量何时能识别目标;并通过涵盖特征依赖、冗余、非线性、隐藏特征等现象的模拟,以及图像与多组学数据的案例研究进行实证验证。

Result: 研究建立了不同零重要性概念何时重合的充分条件,并用反例展示其在条件不满足时的分歧;明确揭示了各方法族所对应的零概念及其统计量的识别条件;模拟与图像、多组学案例从经验上支持了这些理论区分及其实际影响,并在算法公平性(不同公平性准则对应不同零重要性)与基因组扰动建模(不同相关性概念得出关于模型所学内容的不同结论)两个应用中显示了区分的实际后果。

Conclusion: 该框架将界定相关性的科学问题、影响零概念关系的数据与模型假设、以及评估重要性的方法三者统一起来,为连接科学问题、数据生成假设与算法提供了共同的统计语言,并澄清了特征重要性分析所能支持的结论边界。

Abstract: Feature importance is central to interpretable machine learning, but the term "importance" encompasses several fundamentally different notions of relevance. We develop a unified perspective based on null importance: a population-level characterization of when a feature is irrelevant under a specified notion of relevance. We consider standard notions of null importance arising from marginal and conditional statistical relevance, predictive risk, functional invariance, and causal effects, and show how these notions answer different scientific questions. We illustrate the framework in two applications in which the distinction is particularly consequential: algorithmic fairness, where common fairness criteria correspond to different notions of null importance, and genomic perturbation modeling, where different notions of relevance lead to different conclusions about what a prediction model has learned. The framework connects three aspects of feature analysis: the scientific question defining relevance, the data and model assumptions that shape how different null notions relate, and the methods used to assess importance. We establish sufficient conditions under which null notions coincide and give counterexamples showing how they diverge when those conditions fail. We then characterize which nulls different method families target and when their zero-importance statistics identify those targets. Finally, simulations spanning feature dependence, redundancy, nonlinearity, hidden features and other standard phenomena, along with case studies on image and multiomics data, provide empirical evidence for these theoretical distinctions and their practical consequences. Taken together, these results provide a common statistical language for relating scientific questions, data-generating assumptions, and algorithms, and clarify the conclusions that feature-importance analyses can support.

</details>


### [225] [Error bounds in Sobolev norms for approximations with norm constrained ReLU neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19937)
*Xianjun Li,Yunfei Yang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文将 ReLU 网络对光滑函数的路径范数约束逼近结果从一致逼近推广到 Sobolev 范数逼近，分别给出浅层与深层网络的误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明，在权重路径范数约束下，ReLU 神经网络能很好地逼近光滑函数，但这些结果主要针对一致逼近。作者希望将结论推广到 Sobolev 范数（W^{1,p}-范数）下的逼近问题，从而刻画网络在函数光滑性更弱或更一般的空间中的逼近能力。

Method: 在权重路径范数不超过 K、宽度为 W、深度为 L 的 ReLU 网络类中，研究对 W^{n,p} 空间中 d 维 Sobolev 函数的 W^{1,p}-范数逼近误差。分别分析浅层网络（L=1）与深层网络两种情况，推导误差上界。

Result: 对于浅层网络 L=1，当光滑指数满足 n<s=(d+3)/2 时，逼近误差为 O(max{W^{-(n-1)/d}, K^{-(n-1)/(s-n)}})。对于深层网络，在宽度 W 与深度 L 充分大的条件下，去除了对光滑性的限制，逼近界为 O(K^{-(n-1)/(d+d/p+1)})。

Conclusion: 路径范数约束的 ReLU 网络可在 Sobolev 范数下有效逼近 Sobolev 函数；深层结构相比浅层结构能放宽光滑性限制，并获得仅依赖路径范数 K 的收敛速率。

Abstract: Recent studies have shown that smooth functions can be well approximated by ReLU neural networks with path norm constraint on the weights. We extend these results from uniform approximation to approximation in Sobolev norm. Specifically, we analyze how well Sobolev functions in $W^{n,p}$ can be approximated by neural networks with width $W$, depth $L$ and path norm bounded by $K$, when the approximation error is measured in the $W^{1,p}$-norm. For shallow networks with depth $L=1$, we derive the approximation error bound $\mathcal{O}(\max\{W^{-(n-1)/d}, K^{-(n-1)/(s-n)}\})$, when the smoothness index satisfies $n<s=(d+3)/2$ and the input is $d$-dimensional. For deep networks, we remove the restriction on the smoothness by showing that the approximation bound $\mathcal{O}(K^{-(n-1)/(d+d/p+1)})$ holds if the width $W$ and depth $L$ are sufficiently large.

</details>


### [226] [Model-based Bootstrap for Offline Policy Evaluation in Tabular Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20389)
*Weiwei Wang,Yuqiang Li,Xianyi Wu,Bingyi Jing*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于模型的bootstrap框架，用于有限时域、时间非齐次MDP中离线策略评估（OPE）的不确定性量化，通过从估计的MDP重新生成轨迹来估计评估误差的抽样分布，并证明了其分布一致性、渐近有效的置信区间和一致的方差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险强化学习应用中，新策略部署前需可靠评估，仅有point estimate不够，还需置信区间和方差估计等原则性不确定性量化以支持安全、风险感知决策。统一这些任务的关键是估计评估误差的抽样分布，但现有方法在鲁棒性、可扩展性或有限样本有效性方面存在不足。

Method: 提出面向有限时域、时间非齐次MDP的model-based bootstrap不确定性量化框架。不同于依赖重采样完整episode的经典bootstrap，该方法从估计的MDP中重新生成轨迹，从而适配更广泛的离线数据格式，包括完整轨迹、transition-level观测和轨迹片段，并提升有限样本统计效率。

Result: 理论上建立了bootstrap分布一致性、目标策略价值的渐近有效置信区间和一致方差估计。大量仿真表明，该方法能准确捕捉OPE估计量的抽样分布，在多数设置下给出更紧的置信区间和更准确的方差估计。

Conclusion: 基于模型的bootstrap为OPE提供了更灵活、有限样本有效的统一不确定性量化方案，在多种离线数据格式下优于经典重采样方法。

Abstract: Offline policy evaluation (OPE) is crucial in high-stakes reinforcement learning applications, where new policies must be assessed reliably before deployment. In such settings, point estimates alone are insufficient; principled uncertainty quantification, such as confidence intervals and variance estimates, is essential for safe and risk-aware decision-making. A comprehensive way to unify these tasks is to estimate the sampling distribution of the evaluation error. Existing approaches, however, often suffer from limited robustness, scalability, or finite-sample validity. In this paper, we propose a model-based bootstrap framework for uncertainty quantification of OPE in finite-horizon, time-inhomogeneous Markov decision processes (MDPs). Unlike classical bootstrap methods that rely on resampling complete episodes, the proposed method regenerates trajectories from an estimated MDP and can therefore accommodate a much broader range of offline data formats, including complete trajectories, transition-level observations, and trajectory fragments. This flexibility further improves finite-sample statistical efficiency. We establish bootstrap distributional consistency, asymptotically valid confidence intervals, and consistent variance estimation for the target policy value. Extensive simulations show that the proposed method accurately captures the sampling distribution of the OPE estimator, yielding tighter confidence intervals and more accurate variance estimates in most settings.

</details>


### [227] [Online Supervised Dimension Reduction with Random Features: Diagnostics and Computational Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20454)
*Zhenlin Yao,Wei Xiong*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文区分了"监督谱目标的优化精度"与"总体子空间/预测表示质量"，以OKSPCA为对象，通过固定映射一致性、集中与扰动分析，以及同目标对比实验，表明终端优化精度不足以解释子空间与预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确优化监督谱目标未必能获得准确的总体子空间或更好的预测表示；作者希望厘清终端优化精度、子空间几何质量与预测性能之间的关系，并分离数值成本与质量、秩覆盖、时效性等因素。

Method: 以在线核监督主成分分析（OKSPCA）为研究对象，该方法在有限随机特征坐标中使用中心化交叉矩，并采用Adam式正交基更新；给出固定映射一致性、集中与扰动结果来刻画估计量及其精确子空间，并开展同目标对比以单独评估实际迭代结果。

Result: 在六个预测基准上，性能取决于所声明的流程：用精确经验目标替换跟踪器后，两项回归缺陷基本不变；直接分类秩模型平均可捕获几乎全部终端目标能量，但保存的中间状态存在显著几何偏差；受控样本量研究进一步区分了经验精度与总体恢复。在数值服务负载中，精确按需计算在测试的分类场景更快，而Adam对某些稠密较宽回归请求相比完整薄SVD服务更省时，但伴随持续几何误差。

Conclusion: 这些诊断限制了仅以终端优化精度解释性能的做法，并区分了数值成本与质量、秩覆盖和时效性；既未证明实际跟踪器的收敛性，也未证明基的可用性带来预测或部署收益。

Abstract: Accurate optimization of a supervised spectral objective need not produce an accurate population subspace or a better predictive representation. We investigate these distinctions for Online Kernel Supervised Principal Component Analysis (OKSPCA), which combines a centered cross-moment in finite random-feature coordinates with an Adam-style orthonormal basis update for an established objective. Fixed-map consistency, concentration and perturbation results describe the estimator and its exact subspace; same-target comparisons then assess the practical iterate separately. Across six predictive benchmarks, performance depends on the declared pipeline: replacing the tracker with the exact empirical target leaves the two regression deficits largely unchanged. Direct classification-rank models capture nearly all terminal objective energy on average, but a saved intermediate state exhibits substantial geometric deviation; a controlled sample-size study further separates empirical accuracy from population recovery. In distinct numerical-service workloads, exact on-request computation is faster in the tested classification settings, whereas Adam saves time relative to the tested full thin-SVD service for some dense wider-regression requests, alongside persistent geometric error. These diagnostics limit explanations based solely on terminal optimization accuracy and distinguish numerical cost from quality, rank coverage and freshness; they establish neither practical-tracker convergence nor predictive or deployment benefits from basis availability.

</details>


### [228] [TAP Accuracy Below the Fluctuation Scale and Universal Posterior Geometry in Spherical Linear Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20577)
*Jingbo Liu,Zhiyuan Yu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 在高维按比例增长下，于定量Marchenko--Pastur谱正则条件下证明球面线性模型的Bayes最优TAP近似，并刻画其后验几何：自由能/TAP最优的确定性等价与$O_P(p^{-1/2})$尖锐涨落尺度、TAP极大点与后验均值的$O_P(p^{-1})$平方欧氏距离，以及后验质量在ridge估计定带的尖锐指数集中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究背景是高维比例渐近下的Bayes最优球面线性模型。既有TAP与后验几何结果通常依赖较强的设计假设（如entrywise独立、对奇异向量的条件或更严格谱条件）。本文动机是在更弱、更一般的定量谱正则条件下获得定量TAP近似与后验集中，从而覆盖有限四阶矩的标准化i.i.d.设计且不要求逐元素独立。

Method: 假设环境维度与样本量按比例增长，并在设计上施加定量Marchenko--Pastur谱正则条件。在此条件下建立全温度范围的定量TAP近似，并分析后验几何。对自然有限纵横比TAP泛函，比较归一化球面自由能与TAP最优；给出其显式确定性等价与涨落阶。进一步对所有全局TAP极大点，考察其与球面后验均值的归一化平方欧氏距离。还用ridge估计构造数据依赖带，估计带外后验质量的指数阶，并构造球冠匹配下界，分析随带宽序列变化的捕获性质。

Result: 在所述谱正则条件下，得到定量全温度TAP近似。自然有限纵横比TAP泛函下，归一化球面自由能与TAP最优相差$O_P(p^{-1})$；二者各自与其显式确定性等价相差$O_P(p^{-1/2})$，且该涨落尺度是尖锐的。对所有全局TAP极大点一致地，到球面后验均值的归一化平方欧氏距离为$O_P(p^{-1})$。对由ridge估计确定的数据依赖带，当带宽$\varepsilon$足够小时，带外后验质量的对数上界为$-cp\varepsilon^2+O_P(1)$；对每个固定几何可行宽度，球冠构造给出匹配指数阶下界；对任意确定性宽度序列$\varepsilon_p\gg p^{-1/2}$，相应带渐近捕获全部后验质量。

Conclusion: 论文在定量Marchenko--Pastur谱正则条件下给出了球面线性模型Bayes最优后验的精细高维刻画：TAP近似、自由能与TAP最优的确定性等价及尖锐涨落、TAP极大点与后验均值的一致性距离，以及后验质量围绕ridge估计带的尖锐指数集中。条件可由有限四阶矩的标准化i.i.d.设计满足，且不要求逐元素独立或奇异向量条件，从而推广了相关TAP与后验几何结果。

Abstract: We study the Bayes-optimal spherical linear model as the ambient dimension and sample size grow proportionally, under a quantitative Marchenko--Pastur spectral-regularity condition on the design. This condition is satisfied by normalized i.i.d. designs with standardized entries of finite fourth moment, but does not require entrywise independence or impose conditions on the singular vectors. Under this condition, we prove a quantitative all-temperature TAP approximation and characterize the posterior geometry. For the natural finite-aspect-ratio TAP functional, the normalized spherical free energy and the TAP optimum differ by $O_P(p^{-1})$. Each is within $O_P(p^{-1/2})$ of its explicit deterministic equivalent, and this fluctuation scale is sharp. Uniformly over all global TAP maximizers, the normalized squared Euclidean distance to the spherical posterior mean is $O_P(p^{-1})$. We also prove that the posterior mass outside a data-dependent band determined by the ridge estimator has sharp exponential order. More precisely, uniformly over sufficiently small band widths $\varepsilon$, the logarithm of this mass is at most $-cp\varepsilon^2+O_P(1)$. For every fixed geometrically admissible width, a spherical-cap construction gives a matching exponential-order lower bound on this mass. For every deterministic sequence of widths $\varepsilon_p\gg p^{-1/2}$, the corresponding bands capture asymptotically all posterior mass.

</details>


### [229] [Prediction-Powered Smoothing and Validation for Disaggregated AI Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20758)
*Sho Kawano,Zehang Richard Li,Paul A. Parker*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文将评测集视为有限总体，针对分域均值估计提出贝叶斯“预测驱动平滑”（PP-S）及跨分类层级借力的扩展（PP-TS），并推导了一种近似无偏的设计型交叉验证评分用于在直接与平滑估计器间选择，在基准与部署智能体流量数据上均提升点估计与区间估计精度且覆盖率接近名义水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统评测需要分域（如基准任务类型、部署智能体的对话类型）的细分评估，但穷举测试代价过高，只能依赖抽样的带标签单元。直接估计器（含PPI）仅使用本域标签，在小样本域上精度不足，因此需要借助小域估计思想在有限总体框架下同时改进点估计与区间估计，并解决估计器选择的验证问题。

Method: 将评测集视为有限总体，目标是各域均值的准确点估计与区间估计。估计方面提出PP-S：对每个域的预测驱动估计拟合贝叶斯模型，并扩展为PP-TS以在报告分类层级间借力。验证方面推导出一个新的、近似无偏的设计型交叉验证评分，用于在直接估计器与平滑估计器之间做出选择。

Result: 在一个可验证评分的整理基准数据集与由人工评分的部署智能体流量数据上（两者均观测到全部结果）进行实验。两种方法在点估计与区间估计上均优于直接估计器，区间覆盖率接近名义水平。在相同抽样预算下，所提出的评分在估计器选择上的表现与独立验证样本相当，且对所选估计器误差的估计准确度显著更高。

Conclusion: 将评测集视为有限总体并结合小域估计思想，可以形成从估计到验证的一体化工作流；PP-S/PP-TS在有限标签条件下改善分域估计，配套的设计型交叉验证评分可在不额外采样的情况下可靠选择估计器并更精确地量化其误差。

Abstract: Evaluating an AI system requires disaggregated assessment, as performance varies across domains such as benchmark task types or conversation types in deployed agents. Exhaustive testing is expensive, so evaluation rests on a sample of labeled units. We treat the evaluation set as a finite population and seek accurate point and interval estimates of each domain mean. Direct estimators, including prediction-powered inference (PPI), use only a domain's own labels and are imprecise where labels are few. Small area estimation addresses this problem, and we build on it to develop an integrated workflow for estimation and validation. For estimation, we propose prediction-powered smoothing (PP-S), a Bayesian model fit to each domain's prediction-powered estimate, with an extension that borrows strength across a reporting taxonomy (PP-TS). For validation, we derive a new, approximately unbiased design-based cross-validation score for choosing among direct and smoothed estimators. We study a curated benchmark with verifiable grading and deployed agent traffic graded by humans, each with every outcome observed. In both, the proposed estimators improve on the direct estimators in point and interval estimation, with near-nominal coverage. At the same sampling budget, our score selects as well as an independent validation sample does and estimates the selected estimator's error far more accurately.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [230] [Robust Error Bounds for Vector-Valued Kernel Ridge Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19443)
*Wenbin Wang,Colin N. Jones*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究向量值算子的学习问题，在向量值核岭回归框架下，针对输出取值于任意Hilbert空间且受有界范数加性噪声污染的情形，假设未知算子属于向量值再生核Hilbert空间，推导了估计量与真实值之间在相应Hilbert范数下的逐点确定性误差界，并通过两类数值仿真验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量值函数的核学习方法已被广泛研究，但非线性向量值算子的相关工作相对较少；而此类向量值输出算子（如状态向量、轨迹）在系统辨识等问题中普遍存在，因此有必要在向量值核岭回归框架下建立相应的学习理论与误差保证。

Method: 采用向量值核岭回归（vector-valued kernel ridge regression）框架学习向量值算子；假设输出取值于任意Hilbert空间，并叠加范数有界的加性噪声；在未知算子属于向量值再生核Hilbert空间（vector-valued RKHS）的假设下，推导核岭回归估计量与真实算子之间差距的逐点确定性界。

Result: 得到了核岭回归估计量与真实算子在相应Hilbert空间范数下的逐点确定性误差上界；并通过两种不同场景的数值仿真验证了理论结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在向量值RKHS假设与有界噪声条件下，向量值核岭回归可获得具有理论保证的逐点误差界，为系统辨识等向量值算子学习任务提供了理论支撑，数值实验进一步支持了该理论分析。

Abstract: While kernel-based learning methods for scalar-valued functions have been extensively studied, comparatively little work considers nonlinear vector-valued operators. Such operators with vector-valued outputs, e.g., state vectors and trajectories, commonly arise in system identification problems. In this paper, we study the problem of learning a vector-valued operator under the framework of vector-valued kernel ridge regression. We assume that the outputs take values in an arbitrary Hilbert space and are corrupted by additive noise with bounded norm. Under the assumption that the unknown operator belongs to a vector-valued reproducing kernel Hilbert space, we derive a pointwise deterministic bound on the discrepancy between the kernel ridge regression estimator and the ground truth in the corresponding Hilbert space norm. Numerical simulations under two different scenarios are presented to validate the theoretical results.

</details>


### [231] [Human-Anchored Inference for Ranking New Models with Large Language Model Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19599)
*Xin Zhou,Sinian Zhang,Zhanyan Yang,Mingyuan Xu,Molei Liu,Doudou Zhou*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出ANCHOR框架，利用历史人类与LLM评判对比，学习各评判者对人类分差的敏感度及特征依赖偏差，从而在新模型仅有LLM评判时推断其"人类参照"得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类成对比较是评估大语言模型的参照标准，但为每个新发布的模型收集足够的人类评判既昂贵又耗时；LLM评判虽可扩展，却与人类偏好存在系统性差异，且不同评判者之间也不一致。因此需要一种方法，在新模型只有LLM评判、没有人类评判的情况下，仍能给出可比的人类参照排名。

Method: 提出ANCHOR（ANchored Comparisons for Human-reference inference with Orthogonal Riesz correction）。用历史人类与LLM对比数据学习两点：(1) 各评判者对人类分数差的敏感度；(2) 特征依赖的评判者偏差。再据此从新模型的LLM评判中推断其人类参照得分。框架允许历史对比与新模型对比之间的特征分布发生变化。推断上构造Neyman正交估计量，通过联合Riesz校正消除估计历史人类得分、评判者敏感度与偏差函数所带来的第一阶影响。

Result: 理论上建立了可识别性、收敛速率与渐近正态性，并给出可一致估计的方差，证明ANCHOR达到半参数效率界。模拟实验显示在得分估计与排名准确度上均有提升。在Chatbot Arena上，ANCHOR在竞争方法中取得最低的得分RMSE与插入MAE，且平均得分区间更窄。

Conclusion: ANCHOR通过校正LLM评判相对人类偏好的系统偏差，实现了在新模型缺乏人类评判时仍可进行人类参照的得分推断与排名，兼具统计效率与实证优势。

Abstract: Human pairwise comparisons provide a reference for evaluating large language models (LLMs), but collecting sufficient judgments for each new release is costly and time-consuming. LLM judges offer a scalable alternative, although their comparisons may differ systematically from human preferences and across judges. We study the ranking of a new model that has received LLM-judge comparisons but no human comparisons. We propose ANCHOR (ANchored Comparisons for Human-reference inference with Orthogonal Riesz correction), which uses historical human and LLM comparisons to learn judge-specific sensitivities to human score differences and feature-dependent judge biases. These estimates are then used to infer the new model's human-reference score from its judge comparisons. The framework allows the feature distribution to change between historical and new-model comparisons. For inference, we construct a Neyman-orthogonal estimator through a joint Riesz correction that removes the first-order effects of estimating the historical human scores, judge sensitivities, and bias functions. We establish identification, convergence rates, and asymptotic normality with consistently estimable variance, and show that ANCHOR attains the semiparametric efficiency bound. Simulations demonstrate gains in score estimation and ranking accuracy. On Chatbot Arena, ANCHOR achieves the lowest score RMSE and insertion MAE among competing methods, with narrower score intervals on average.

</details>


### [232] [Optimal estimation and goodness-of-fit testing of the mean for sparse longitudinal functional data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19889)
*Valentin Patilea,Mahdi Saidi*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 研究纵向函数数据的均值函数估计与检验问题，采用时间方向正交基投影、系数函数加权平均的策略，覆盖确定性权重与数据驱动权重，给出非渐近风险界、最优截断水平及极小极大最优性证明，并提供拟合优度检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向函数数据中每个个体仅在随机访问时刻贡献少量完整曲线，观测时间不规则且样本量有限，经典渐近理论难以刻画有限样本下的风险与截断选择，因此需要非渐近分析框架来指导估计与检验。

Method: 将均值函数沿时间方向投影到正交基上，对每个系数函数以曲线剖面的加权平均进行估计；权重方案包括确定性权重与随机权重，并特别引入无需设计密度的闭式数据驱动权重；针对各权重方案推导积分二次风险的非渐近界与显式最优截断水平；同时基于该估计构造均值函数的拟合优度检验，并在原假设与备择假设下建立检验统计量分布的高斯逼近非渐近界。

Result: 各权重方案达到相同的收敛速率，且该速率在相应系数衰减类上为极小极大最优，仅主导常数不同；给出了显式最优截断水平；检验统计量在原假设与备择假设下的分布均可由高斯分布逼近，并具有非渐近误差界。

Conclusion: 所提估计与检验流程易于实现、计算高效，在实际应用中表现良好；统一的非渐近分析揭示了不同加权策略的等价收敛性，为纵向函数数据均值推断提供了理论完备且实用的工具。

Abstract: We study the mean function of longitudinal functional data, where each subject contributes a small number of complete profiles over a general domain, observed at random visit times. The mean is projected onto an orthonormal basis in the time direction, and each coefficient function is estimated by a weighted average of the profiles. We consider deterministic and random weights, including closed-form, data-driven weights that dispense with the design density entirely. For each weighting scheme we derive non-asymptotic bounds on the integrated quadratic risk and an explicit optimal truncation level. All schemes share the same convergence rate, which we show to be minimax optimal over the corresponding coefficient-decay class, only the leading constants differ. We also provide a goodness-of-fit test for the mean function, and derive non-asymptotic bounds for the Gaussian approximation of its distribution under both the null and alternative hypotheses. Our estimation and testing procedures are easy to implement, fast, and perform well in applications.

</details>


### [233] [The exact region determined by Spearman's rho and Gini's gamma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19890)
*Jonathan Ansari,Marcus Rockel,Stefanie Steinmaßl*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文确定了两元Copula上Spearman's rho与Gini's gamma的精确可达区域，解决了二者之间剩余的成对精确区域问题，给出边界参数化、极值Copula构造及符号阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Spearman's rho、Kendall's tau、Gini's gamma、Blomqvist's beta与Spearman's footrule五个相关性度量中，多数成对精确可达区域已被研究，而rho与gamma之间的精确区域问题尚未解决，本文旨在补齐这一空缺。

Method: 将中心化秩的符号与大小分离，把rho与gamma线性组合的优化约化为两个均匀大小的最优传输问题；极值元由一个中心反对角块与重标定的辅助传输优化器组合而成；通过粘合相应对偶片段的Kantorovich对偶势证明全局最优性；并显式参数化给定gamma下rho的最大边界。

Result: 得到rho-gamma可达区域的完整刻画：rho最大边界由一段初等弧至单一交点，其后为在共单调性处累积的可数个代数片段；给出|rho-gamma|的最大可能值，以及rho与gamma必须同号的尖锐阈值，并构造出达到边界每一点的Copula。

Conclusion: 该结果补全了五个相关性度量两两精确可达区域的研究，为rho与gamma的联合取值范围提供了精确、显式且具构造性的描述。

Abstract: We determine the exact attainable region of Spearman's rho and Gini's gamma over all bivariate copulas, resolving the remaining pairwise exact-region problem among Spearman's rho, Kendall's tau, Gini's gamma, Blomqvist's beta and Spearman's footrule. We give an explicit parametrization of the rho-maximal boundary for prescribed Gini's gamma and construct copulas attaining every boundary point. The boundary consists of an elementary arc up to a single junction and, beyond it, countably many algebraic pieces accumulating at comonotonicity. This description also gives the largest possible value of $|ρ-γ|$ and the sharp thresholds beyond which rho and gamma must have the same sign. Our proof separates the signs and magnitudes of centred ranks, reducing the optimization of linear combinations of $ρ$ and $γ$ to an optimal transport problem for two uniform magnitudes. The extremizers combine a central antidiagonal block with rescaled auxiliary transport optimizers, and global optimality follows from a Kantorovich dual potential obtained by gluing the corresponding dual pieces.

</details>


### [234] [Revisiting the Brunner-Munzel test from the viewpoint of local linear approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19986)
*Makito Oku*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文从局部线性近似的视角重新解释Brunner-Munzel检验统计量，指出其方差估计量可在"各观测对样本随机优效性影响可加"的假设下导出，从而使BM检验的含义更易直观理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BM检验适用范围比Mann-Whitney U检验更广，因为它不假设两组方差相等，但其检验统计量的含义难以直观理解，这一障碍可能妨碍了BM检验的广泛使用。作者希望缓解该问题，帮助实践者无顾虑地采用BM检验。

Method: 作者提出对BM检验统计量的另一种解释，基于局部线性近似（local linear approximation）的方法，将每个观测对样本随机优效性（sample stochastic superiority）的影响视为可加的，进而推导出BM检验所使用的方差估计量。

Result: 研究表明，BM检验中所用的样本随机优效性方差估计量可以通过局部线性近似导出，在此框架下各观测的影响被假定为加性。

Conclusion: 该简单解释有助于实践者毫不犹豫地决定使用BM检验，从而促进BM检验在实践中的推广。

Abstract: The Brunner-Munzel (BM) test is a nonparametric test for two independent samples that evaluates whether observations from one group tend to be greater than observations from another group, or vice versa. The BM test has a broader scope of application than the Mann-Whitney $U$ test because it does not assume equal variances between the two groups. However, the meaning of the BM test statistic is difficult to understand intuitively, which may be one of the factors hindering the widespread use of the BM test. To alleviate this problem, in this paper, I introduce an alternative interpretation of the BM test statistic from the viewpoint of local linear approximation. It is shown that the variance estimator for the sample stochastic superiority used in the BM test can be derived using local linear approximation, in which the influence of each observation on the sample stochastic superiority is assumed to be additive. This simple interpretation will help practitioners decide to use the BM test without hesitation.

</details>


### [235] [Inference for two-stage sampling in spatial surveys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20065)
*Guillaume Chauvet,Olivier Bouriaud,Trinh H. K. Duong*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文为连续空间域上的两阶段抽样建立了基于设计的渐近理论，推导了Horvitz-Thompson估计量的方差阶数，证明了估计量与方差估计量的设计相合性，并在高熵第一阶段设计下证明了Hájek型方差估计量的相合性及估计量的渐近正态性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续空间域上的两阶段抽样（如森林资源清查）中，目标参数为积分总量及其光滑函数，而传统基于设计的渐近理论多针对离散有限总体，缺乏在固定区域、初级抽样单元不断细化框架下的严格理论支撑。

Method: 在固定面积框架下，将区域划分为逐渐细化的初级抽样单元，次级抽样单元为从连续子区域中抽取的点；推导Horvitz-Thompson估计量各方差分量阶数，在包含概率、抽样密度和研究变量的显式正则条件下证明设计相合性与方差估计相合性，并扩展至总量的光滑尺度不变函数的代入估计量；针对高熵第一阶段设计，证明仅依赖一阶包含概率的Hájek型方差估计量的相合性。

Result: 建立了估计量的设计相合性及方差估计量相合性；证明了高熵第一阶段设计下Hájek型方差估计量相合；证明了总量估计量与代入估计量的渐近正态性；通过受森林清查启发的模拟研究验证了所提估计量与方差估计量的有限样本表现。

Conclusion: 该研究为连续空间域两阶段抽样提供了统一的基于设计的渐近理论，覆盖点抽样情形下的方差分量阶数、相合性与渐近正态性，可用于森林清查等空间抽样应用。

Abstract: This paper develops a design-based asymptotic theory for two-stage sampling over a continuous spatial domain. The target parameters are integral totals, or smooth functions of such totals, defined over a fixed bounded territory partitioned into an increasingly fine collection of primary sampling units. Within this fixed-area framework, we derive the order of the variance components of the Horvitz-Thompson estimator when secondary sampling units are points selected from continuous sub-regions. We establish design consistency of the estimator, as well as consistency of variance estimators under explicit regularity conditions on inclusion probabilities, sampling densities, and the study variable. The results are extended to plug-in estimators of smooth scaleinvariant functions of totals. For high-entropy first-stage designs, we further show that a H{á}jek-type variance estimator based only on first-order inclusion probabilities is consistent, and we prove asymptotic normality of both total and plug-in estimators. A simulation study inspired by forest inventory applications illustrates the finite-sample performance of the proposed estimators and variance estimators.

</details>


### [236] [On the parametric and semiparametric Fisher information matrix for non-zero mean stationary spherical invariant random processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20469)
*Jean-Pierre Delmas,Habti Abeida,Stefano Fortunati*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文将经典Whittle公式从零均值高斯过程推广到具有任意均值的多维实值平稳复合高斯过程（CGP），在完全已知、参数化及完全未知密度生成器的统一框架下，推导出渐近Fisher信息矩阵（FIM）率的闭式谱域表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Whittle公式仅适用于高斯过程，而现实中许多信号呈现重尾或非高斯特性，高斯假设限制了其适用范围，因此需要将该结果推广到更具一般性的复合高斯过程。

Method: 基于连续n次观测的Slepian-Bangs公式并将其扩展到半参数情形，利用分块Toeplitz矩阵的渐近性质，在完全已知、参数化和完全未知密度生成器的统一框架下推导渐近FIM率的闭式谱域表达。

Result: 得到了对所有密度生成器族通用的闭式谱域FIM率表达式，该表达式在标准零均值高斯公式基础上，加入了与非零均值相关的分布依赖项，以及一个对非高斯分布选择不变的额外协方差项。

Conclusion: 该推广为重尾信号处理应用提供了高效且具理论基础的性能分析工具，拓展了Whittle公式的适用范围。

Abstract: The classical Whittle formula provides a closed-form expression for the asymptotic Fisher information matrix (FIM) rate of multidimensional, real-valued, zero-mean, purely nondeterministic stationary Gaussian processes (GPs), expressed in terms of their parameterized spectra within a maximum-likelihood framework. However, the Gaussian assumption underlying this result restricts its applicability to many real-world signals exhibiting heavy-tailed or non-Gaussian behavior. In this paper, we extend Whittle's result to multidimensional, real-valued stationary compound Gaussian processes (CGPs) with arbitrary mean, under a unified framework encompassing fully known, parameterized, and completely unknown density generators. Building upon the Slepian-Bangs formula for $n$ consecutive observations and extending it to the semiparametric setting, we leverage the asymptotic properties of block Toeplitz matrices to obtain a closed-form spectral-domain expression for the asymptotic FIM rate. The resulting expression, common to all density generator families, generalizes the standard zero-mean Gaussian formula by incorporating a distribution-dependent contribution associated with the nonzero mean, and an additional covariance term that is invariant to the choice of non-Gaussian distribution. This extension enables efficient and theoretically grounded performance analysis for heavy-tailed signal processing applications.

</details>


### [237] [Approximation Theorems for High-Dimensional Canonical U-Statistics: Gaussian Chaos and Phase Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20529)
*Leheng Cai,Qirui Hu*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 研究高维退化二阶U统计量最大值的同步推断，证明其极限为联合符号高斯二次混沌，识别有效秩驱动的相变，并构造免特征系统估计的高斯乘子自助法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 退化性导致二阶U统计量出现二次涨落，即使方差精确归一化，普通高斯校准也会失效；且一般反集中界在高维推断中过于粗糙，需要针对最大值同步推断的更精细理论工具。

Method: 在高维下研究标准二阶U统计量最大值的同步推断：建立允许不定核的一般联合符号高斯二次混沌逼近结果；在额外谱结构下推导更尖锐的反集中界；刻画由有效秩驱动的从非高斯符号混沌最大值到协方差匹配高斯对应物的相变；提出无需估计特征系统的高斯乘子自助法并证明其有效性。

Result: 得到一般逼近定理（允许不定核）、更尖锐的谱条件下反集中界、有效秩驱动的相变结论，以及可行的免特征系统高斯乘子自助推断方法；两个应用与大量数值模拟验证了框架的适用范围与实用性能。

Conclusion: 高维退化U统计量最大值推断应面向联合符号高斯二次混沌而非普通高斯；在适当谱结构下可获得有效推断，所提自助法实用且理论有效。

Abstract: We study simultaneous inference for maxima of canonical order-two $U$-statistics in high dimension. Degeneracy makes quadratic fluctuations leading, so ordinary Gaussian calibration can fail even after exact variance normalization. We show that the appropriate general target is a joint signed Gaussian quadratic chaos and establish a general approximation result that permits indefinite kernels. The general anti-concentration bound is too crude for high-dimensional inference, and we obtain sharper bounds under additional spectral structure. We also identify a phase transition from a non-Gaussian signed-chaos maximum to its covariance-matched Gaussian counterpart driven by the effective rank. For feasible inference, we propose a Gaussian multiplier bootstrap that avoid estimating eigensystems, and establish its validity. Two applications and extensive numerical simulations further illustrate the scope and practical performance of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [238] [Principal component error in high-dimensional factor models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20550)
*Alex Bernstein,Lisa R. Goldberg,Nicholas Gunther,Alec N. Kercheval,Tian Lan,Yian Lin,Dayi Yao*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该文将统计因子模型中主成分估计的误差分解为"子空间外误差"与"子空间内误差"两项，并在变量数趋于无穷而样本量有界的渐近框架下给出二者的几乎必然极限，为PCA因子模型估计提供可量化的误差度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在金融经济学、基因组学、机器学习和信号处理等领域，常用样本协方差矩阵的主成分（特征向量）来估计真实的"主方向"。但这类估计存在常被忽视的显著误差，缺乏清晰、可解释的误差刻画，使得依赖PCA的因子模型的预测与归因结论难以被准确评估。

Method: 作者将估计误差显式分解为两个可解释的部分：一是子空间外误差，度量估计值与总体因子暴露张成子空间之间的距离；二是子空间内误差，源于潜在因子收益的固定样本量。在变量数随样本量有界这一渐近设定下，推导两项各自几乎必然的极限，并利用一个美国公开股票市场的三因子模拟来检验误差及其组成部分随维度与样本量的变化。

Result: 两项误差均有几乎必然渐近极限。子空间外误差可由数据表达，从而给出一个可估计的误差下界；子空间内误差由于固定样本量的潜在因子收益而无法仅凭数据估计。三因子模拟显示误差大小及其构成依赖于维度和样本量，且在该模拟中子空间外误差占主导。

Conclusion: 该误差分解为依赖PCA估计因子模型的研究者提供了量化模型预测与归因误差的工具，明确了哪些误差可从数据中估计、哪些不能，具有方法论与实际应用价值。

Abstract: In a statistical factor model, principal components (or eigenvectors) of a sample covariance matrix serve as estimates of {\it principal directions}, the true drivers of co-movement of a collection of observed variables. We write the often substantial error in these estimates as a sum of two interpretable terms, which we show have almost sure asymptotic limits as the number of variables grows with sample size bounded. This scenario is commonplace in financial economics, genomics, machine learning and signal processing. {\it Out-of-subspace error} measures the distance from an estimate to the subspace spanned by population factor exposures. It can be expressed in terms of data, providing an estimable floor for error. {\it In-subspace error} arises from the fixed sample size of the latent factor returns and cannot be estimated from data alone. We illustrate our error analysis with a three-factor simulation of the US public equity market, showing the dependence of the magnitude of the error and its components on dimension and sample size. In that simulation, out-of-subspace error dominates. Researchers who rely on principal component analysis to estimate factor models can use our results to quantify errors in model-based predictions and attributions.

</details>


### [239] [Robust Multi-Task Learning for Principal Component Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20733)
*Dali Liu,Haolei Weng*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出多任务主成分分析（PCA）新方法，利用任务间相似结构提升特征子空间估计，并对离群任务保持稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多源数据中任务分布相似度未知，部分任务可能来自任意分布，传统PCA难以同时利用共享结构并抵抗离群任务干扰。

Method: 提出多任务PCA流程，利用任务间相似结构进行特征子空间估计；其中一个流程基于Chen、Gao和Ren（2018）的矩阵深度（matrix-depth）概念。

Result: 建立非渐近收敛率，并在多种情形下达到极小极大最优率；基于矩阵深度的方法在估计误差对离群任务比例依赖上达到最优；大量模拟与真实数据分析验证方法有效性。

Conclusion: 所提多任务PCA方法能有效利用任务相似性并稳健应对离群任务，在理论与实验上均表现优越。

Abstract: Principal component analysis (PCA) is a fundamental tool for learning low-dimensional structure from high-dimensional data.
